# 最高人民法院关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定

**分类**：司法解释  
**法律部门**：诉讼与非诉讼程序法  
**发布机关**：最高人民法院  
**发文字号**：法释〔2022〕11号  
**公布日期**：2022-03-22  
**生效日期**：2022-04-10

> 《最高人民法院关于修改〈最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释〉的决定》已于2022年3月22日由最高人民法院审判委员会第1866次会议通过，现予公布，自2022年4月10日起施行。 2022年4月1日 最高人民法院 关于修改《最高人民法院关于适用 〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》的决定 法释〔2022〕11号 2021年12月24日，第十三届全国人大常委会第三十二次会议审议通过了《全国人民代表大会常务委员会关于修改〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的决定》。根据修改后的民事诉讼法，结合人民法院民事审判和执行工作实际，最高人民法院审判委员会第1866次会议决定，对《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉的解释》作如下修改： 一、将第九条修改为：“追索赡养费、扶养费、抚养费案件的几个被告住所地不在同一辖区的，可以由原告住所地人民法院管辖。” 二、将第四十五条修改为：“在一个审判程序中参与过本案审判工作的审判人员，不得再参与该案其他程序的审判。 “发回重审的案件，在一审法院作出裁判后又进入第二审程序的，原第二审程序中审判人员不受前款规定的限制。” 三、将第四十八条修改为：“民事诉讼法第四十七条所称的审判人员，包括参与本案审理的人民法院院长、副院长、审判委员会委员、庭长、副庭长、审判员和人民陪审员。” 四、将第六十一条修改为：“当事人之间的纠纷经人民调解委员会或者其他依法设立的调解组织调解达成协议后，一方当事人不履行调解协议，另一方当事人向人民法院提起诉讼的，应以对方当事人为被告。” 五、将第二百一十八条修改为：“赡养费、扶养费、抚养费案件，裁判发生法律效力后，因新情况、新理由，一方当事人再行起诉要求增加或者减少费用的，人民法院应作为新案受理。” 六、将第二百五十八条修改为：“适用简易程序审理的案件，审理期限到期后，有特殊情况需要延长的，经本院院长批准，可以延长审理期限。延长后的审理期限累计不得超过四个月。 “人民法院发现案件不宜适用简易程序，需要转为普通程序审理的，应当在审理期限届满前作出裁定并将审判人员及相关事项书面通知双方当事人。

---

### 一、管辖

<a id="art-1"></a>第一条　[民事诉讼法第十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-19)第一项规定的重大涉外案件，包括争议标的额大的案件、案情复杂的案件，或者一方当事人人数众多等具有重大影响的案件。

**Article 1** The major foreign-related cases as specified in Item 1 of Article 19 of the Civil Procedure Law include cases with a large amount in dispute, cases with complex facts, or cases with significant impact such as those involving a large number of parties on one side.

<a id="art-2"></a>第二条　专利纠纷案件由知识产权法院、最高人民法院确定的中级人民法院和基层人民法院管辖。  
海事、海商案件由海事法院管辖。

**Article 2** Patent dispute cases shall be under the jurisdiction of the Intellectual Property Court, the Intermediate People's Courts and the Primary People's Courts designated by the Supreme People's Court.  
Maritime and admiralty cases shall be under the jurisdiction of the Maritime Courts.

<a id="art-3"></a>第三条　公民的住所地是指公民的户籍所在地，法人或者其他组织的住所地是指法人或者其他组织的主要办事机构所在地。  
法人或者其他组织的主要办事机构所在地不能确定的，法人或者其他组织的注册地或者登记地为住所地。

**Article 3** The domicile of a citizen means the place of his/her registered permanent residence; the domicile of a legal person or any other organization means the place where its principal office is located.  
Where the principal office of a legal person or any other organization cannot be determined, the place of its registration or record shall be the domicile.

<a id="art-4"></a>第四条　公民的经常居住地是指公民离开住所地至起诉时已连续居住一年以上的地方，但公民住院就医的地方除外。

**Article 4** The habitual residence of a citizen means a place where he/she has continuously resided for one year or more after leaving his/her domicile and up to the time of filing the action, except the place where he/she is hospitalized for medical treatment.

<a id="art-5"></a>第五条　对没有办事机构的个人合伙、合伙型联营体提起的诉讼，由被告注册登记地人民法院管辖。没有注册登记，几个被告又不在同一辖区的，被告住所地的人民法院都有管辖权。

**Article 5** An action brought against an individual partnership or a partnership-type joint venture that has no office shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the place where the defendant is registered. Where there is no registration and the several defendants are not in the same jurisdictional district, the People's Court at the place of domicile of each defendant shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-6"></a>第六条　被告被注销户籍的，依照[民事诉讼法第二十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-23)规定确定管辖；原告、被告均被注销户籍的，由被告居住地人民法院管辖。

**Article 6** Where the defendant's registered permanent residence has been cancelled, jurisdiction shall be determined in accordance with the provisions of Article 23 of the Civil Procedure Law; where both the plaintiff and the defendant have had their registered permanent residence cancelled, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the defendant's place of residence.

<a id="art-7"></a>第七条　当事人的户籍迁出后尚未落户，有经常居住地的，由该地人民法院管辖；没有经常居住地的，由其原户籍所在地人民法院管辖。

**Article 7** Where a party's registered permanent residence has been transferred out but not yet settled in a new place, if he/she has a habitual residence, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at that place; if there is no habitual residence, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at his/her original place of registered permanent residence.

<a id="art-8"></a>第八条　双方当事人都被监禁或者被采取强制性教育措施的，由被告原住所地人民法院管辖。被告被监禁或者被采取强制性教育措施一年以上的，由被告被监禁地或者被采取强制性教育措施地人民法院管辖。

**Article 8** Where both parties are imprisoned or subjected to compulsory educational measures, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the defendant's original place of domicile. Where the defendant has been imprisoned or subjected to compulsory educational measures for one year or more, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the place where the defendant is imprisoned or subjected to compulsory educational measures.

<a id="art-9"></a>第九条　追索赡养费、扶养费、抚养费案件的几个被告住所地不在同一辖区的，可以由原告住所地人民法院管辖。

**Article 9** In an action for the recovery of alimony, support or child support, where the domiciles of several defendants are not in the same jurisdictional district, the case may be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the plaintiff's place of domicile.

<a id="art-10"></a>第十条　不服指定监护或者变更监护关系的案件，可以由被监护人住所地人民法院管辖。

**Article 10** A case of dissatisfaction with the designation of a guardian or modification of guardianship may be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the place of domicile of the ward.

<a id="art-11"></a>第十一条　双方当事人均为军人或者军队单位的民事案件由军事法院管辖。

**Article 11** A civil case where both parties are military personnel or military units shall be under the jurisdiction of a military court.

<a id="art-12"></a>第十二条　夫妻一方离开住所地超过一年，另一方起诉离婚的案件，可以由原告住所地人民法院管辖。  
夫妻双方离开住所地超过一年，一方起诉离婚的案件，由被告经常居住地人民法院管辖；没有经常居住地的，由原告起诉时被告居住地人民法院管辖。

**Article 12** Where one spouse has left the place of domicile for more than one year and the other spouse files an action for divorce, the case may be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the plaintiff's place of domicile.  
Where both spouses have left their places of domicile for more than one year and one spouse files an action for divorce, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the defendant's habitual residence; if there is no habitual residence, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the defendant's place of residence at the time the plaintiff files the action.

<a id="art-13"></a>第十三条　在国内结婚并定居国外的华侨，如定居国法院以离婚诉讼须由婚姻缔结地法院管辖为由不予受理，当事人向人民法院提出离婚诉讼的，由婚姻缔结地或者一方在国内的最后居住地人民法院管辖。

**Article 13** For an overseas Chinese who married in China and is residing abroad, if the court of the country of residence refuses to accept the case on the ground that a divorce action shall be under the jurisdiction of the court at the place where the marriage was contracted, and a party files a divorce action with a People's Court, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the place where the marriage was contracted or at the last place of residence in China of either party.

<a id="art-14"></a>第十四条　在国外结婚并定居国外的华侨，如定居国法院以离婚诉讼须由国籍所属国法院管辖为由不予受理，当事人向人民法院提出离婚诉讼的，由一方原住所地或者在国内的最后居住地人民法院管辖。

**Article 14** For an overseas Chinese who married abroad and is residing abroad, if the court of the country of residence refuses to accept the case on the ground that a divorce action shall be under the jurisdiction of the court of the country of nationality, and a party files a divorce action with a People's Court, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at either party's original place of domicile or the last place of residence in China.

<a id="art-15"></a>第十五条　中国公民一方居住在国外，一方居住在国内，不论哪一方向人民法院提起离婚诉讼，国内一方住所地人民法院都有权管辖。国外一方在居住国法院起诉，国内一方向人民法院起诉的，受诉人民法院有权管辖。

**Article 15** Where one Chinese citizen resides abroad and the other resides in China, regardless of which party files a divorce action with a People's Court, the People's Court at the place of domicile of the party residing in China shall have jurisdiction. If the party abroad files an action in the court of the country of residence and the party in China files an action with a People's Court, the People's Court that accepts the case shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-16"></a>第十六条　中国公民双方在国外但未定居，一方向人民法院起诉离婚的，应由原告或者被告原住所地人民法院管辖。

**Article 16** Where both Chinese citizens are abroad but have not settled there, and one party files a divorce action with a People's Court, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the original place of domicile of the plaintiff or the defendant.

<a id="art-17"></a>第十七条　已经离婚的中国公民，双方均定居国外，仅就国内财产分割提起诉讼的，由主要财产所在地人民法院管辖。

**Article 17** Where a divorced Chinese citizen and his/her former spouse both reside abroad and an action is filed solely concerning the division of property located in China, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the place where the principal property is located.

<a id="art-18"></a>第十八条　合同约定履行地点的，以约定的履行地点为合同履行地。  
合同对履行地点没有约定或者约定不明确，争议标的为给付货币的，接收货币一方所在地为合同履行地；交付不动产的，不动产所在地为合同履行地；其他标的，履行义务一方所在地为合同履行地。即时结清的合同，交易行为地为合同履行地。  
合同没有实际履行，当事人双方住所地都不在合同约定的履行地的，由被告住所地人民法院管辖。

**Article 18** Where a contract specifies a place of performance, the specified place of performance shall be the place of performance of the contract.  
Where a contract has no specification or an unclear specification of the place of performance, if the subject matter of the dispute is payment of money, the place of the party receiving the money shall be the place of performance; if it is delivery of immovable property, the place where the immovable property is located shall be the place of performance; for other subject matters, the place of the party performing the obligation shall be the place of performance. For a contract performed instantly, the place where the transaction takes place shall be the place of performance.  
Where a contract is not actually performed and the places of domicile of both parties are not the place of performance specified in the contract, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the defendant's place of domicile.

<a id="art-19"></a>第十九条　财产租赁合同、融资租赁合同以租赁物使用地为合同履行地。合同对履行地有约定的，从其约定。

**Article 19** For a property lease contract or a financial leasing contract, the place where the leased property is used shall be the place of performance of the contract. If the contract specifies a place of performance, that specification shall prevail.

<a id="art-20"></a>第二十条　以信息网络方式订立的买卖合同，通过信息网络交付标的的，以买受人住所地为合同履行地；通过其他方式交付标的的，收货地为合同履行地。合同对履行地有约定的，从其约定。

**Article 20** For a sales contract concluded through information network, if the subject matter is delivered through information network, the place of domicile of the buyer shall be the place of performance of the contract; if the subject matter is delivered by other means, the place of receipt of the goods shall be the place of performance. If the contract specifies a place of performance, that specification shall prevail.

<a id="art-21"></a>第二十一条　因财产保险合同纠纷提起的诉讼，如果保险标的物是运输工具或者运输中的货物，可以由运输工具登记注册地、运输目的地、保险事故发生地人民法院管辖。  
因人身保险合同纠纷提起的诉讼，可以由被保险人住所地人民法院管辖。

**Article 21** An action arising from a dispute over a property insurance contract, if the subject matter insured is a means of transport or goods in transit, may be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the place of registration of the means of transport, the place of destination of the transport, or the place where the insurance accident occurred.  
An action arising from a dispute over a personal insurance contract may be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the place of domicile of the insured.

<a id="art-22"></a>第二十二条　因股东名册记载、请求变更公司登记、股东知情权、公司决议、公司合并、公司分立、公司减资、公司增资等纠纷提起的诉讼，依照[民事诉讼法第二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-27)规定确定管辖。

**Article 22** An action brought for a dispute over the record of the register of shareholders, request for modification of company registration, shareholder's right to information, company resolution, merger of companies, division of companies, capital reduction, capital increase, etc., shall be subject to jurisdiction determined in accordance with the provisions of Article 27 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-23"></a>第二十三条　债权人申请支付令，适用[民事诉讼法第二十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-22)规定，由债务人住所地基层人民法院管辖。

**Article 23** Where a creditor applies for a payment order, the provisions of Article 22 of the Civil Procedure Law shall apply, and the application shall be under the jurisdiction of the Primary People's Court at the debtor's place of domicile.

<a id="art-24"></a>第二十四条　[民事诉讼法第二十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-29)规定的侵权行为地，包括侵权行为实施地、侵权结果发生地。

**Article 24** The place of the tortious act as stipulated in Article 29 of the Civil Procedure Law includes the place where the tortious act is committed and the place where the consequences of the tort occur.

<a id="art-25"></a>第二十五条　信息网络侵权行为实施地包括实施被诉侵权行为的计算机等信息设备所在地，侵权结果发生地包括被侵权人住所地。

**Article 25** The place where an information network tortious act is committed includes the location of the computer or other information equipment used to commit the alleged tortious act; the place where the consequences of the tort occur includes the place of domicile of the infringed party.

<a id="art-26"></a>第二十六条　因产品、服务质量不合格造成他人财产、人身损害提起的诉讼，产品制造地、产品销售地、服务提供地、侵权行为地和被告住所地人民法院都有管辖权。

**Article 26** In an action brought for damage to property or personal injury caused by substandard quality of a product or service, the People's Court at the place of manufacture of the product, the place of sale of the product, the place where the service was provided, the place of the tortious act, and the defendant's place of domicile shall all have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-27"></a>第二十七条　当事人申请诉前保全后没有在法定期间起诉或者申请仲裁，给被申请人、利害关系人造成损失引起的诉讼，由采取保全措施的人民法院管辖。  
当事人申请诉前保全后在法定期间内起诉或者申请仲裁，被申请人、利害关系人因保全受到损失提起的诉讼，由受理起诉的人民法院或者采取保全措施的人民法院管辖。

**Article 27** Where a party who has applied for pre-litigation preservation fails to file an action or apply for arbitration within the statutory period, causing losses to the respondent or interested parties, an action arising therefrom shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court that adopted the preservation measures.  
Where a party who has applied for pre-litigation preservation files an action or applies for arbitration within the statutory period, and the respondent or interested parties bring an action for losses caused by the preservation, the action shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court that accepted the action or the People's Court that adopted the preservation measures.

<a id="art-28"></a>第二十八条　[民事诉讼法第三十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-34)第一项规定的不动产纠纷是指因不动产的权利确认、分割、相邻关系等引起的物权纠纷。  
农村土地承包经营合同纠纷、房屋租赁合同纠纷、建设工程施工合同纠纷、政策性房屋买卖合同纠纷，按照不动产纠纷确定管辖。  
不动产已登记的，以不动产登记簿记载的所在地为不动产所在地；不动产未登记的，以不动产实际所在地为不动产所在地。

**Article 28** The disputes over immovable property as stipulated in Item 1 of Article 34 of the Civil Procedure Law refer to real right disputes arising from the confirmation of rights, partition, or neighbor relations of immovable property.  
Disputes over rural land contract and management contracts, house lease contracts, construction project construction contracts, and policy-based house sales contracts shall be subject to jurisdiction determined as disputes over immovable property.  
Where the immovable property has been registered, the place recorded in the immovable property register shall be the location of the immovable property; where the immovable property is not registered, the actual location of the immovable property shall be its location.

<a id="art-29"></a>第二十九条　[民事诉讼法第三十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-35)规定的书面协议，包括书面合同中的协议管辖条款或者诉讼前以书面形式达成的选择管辖的协议。

**Article 29** The written agreement as stipulated in Article 35 of the Civil Procedure Law includes an agreement on jurisdiction clause in a written contract or an agreement on choice of jurisdiction reached in writing before litigation.

<a id="art-30"></a>第三十条　根据管辖协议，起诉时能够确定管辖法院的，从其约定；不能确定的，依照民事诉讼法的相关规定确定管辖。  
管辖协议约定两个以上与争议有实际联系的地点的人民法院管辖，原告可以向其中一个人民法院起诉。

**Article 30** Where, according to the jurisdiction agreement, the jurisdictional court can be determined at the time of filing the action, the agreement shall prevail; where it cannot be determined, jurisdiction shall be determined in accordance with the relevant provisions of the Civil Procedure Law.  
Where a jurisdiction agreement provides that the People's Courts at two or more places having actual connection with the dispute shall have jurisdiction, the plaintiff may file an action with any one of those People's Courts.

<a id="art-31"></a>第三十一条　经营者使用格式条款与消费者订立管辖协议，未采取合理方式提请消费者注意，消费者主张管辖协议无效的，人民法院应予支持。

**Article 31** Where a business operator uses standard terms to conclude a jurisdiction agreement with a consumer without taking reasonable means to call the consumer's attention to it, and the consumer claims that the jurisdiction agreement is invalid, the People's Court shall uphold the claim.

<a id="art-32"></a>第三十二条　管辖协议约定由一方当事人住所地人民法院管辖，协议签订后当事人住所地变更的，由签订管辖协议时的住所地人民法院管辖，但当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 32** Where a jurisdiction agreement provides that a People's Court at the place of domicile of one party shall have jurisdiction, and after the agreement is concluded, the domicile of that party changes, the People's Court at the place of domicile at the time of conclusion of the jurisdiction agreement shall have jurisdiction, unless the parties agree otherwise.

<a id="art-33"></a>第三十三条　合同转让的，合同的管辖协议对合同受让人有效，但转让时受让人不知道有管辖协议，或者转让协议另有约定且原合同相对人同意的除外。

**Article 33** Where a contract is assigned, the jurisdiction agreement in the contract shall be binding on the assignee of the contract, unless the assignee did not know of the jurisdiction agreement at the time of the assignment, or the assignment agreement provides otherwise and the original counterparty to the contract consents.

<a id="art-34"></a>第三十四条　当事人因同居或者在解除婚姻、收养关系后发生财产争议，约定管辖的，可以适用[民事诉讼法第三十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-35)规定确定管辖。

**Article 34** Where parties have a property dispute arising from cohabitation or after dissolution of marriage or adoption relationship, and they have agreed on jurisdiction, the provisions of Article 35 of the Civil Procedure Law may be applied to determine jurisdiction.

<a id="art-35"></a>第三十五条　当事人在答辩期间届满后未应诉答辩，人民法院在一审开庭前，发现案件不属于本院管辖的，应当裁定移送有管辖权的人民法院。

**Article 35** Where a party fails to respond and defend after the expiration of the defense period, and before the first-instance trial, the People's Court discovers that the case does not fall within its jurisdiction, it shall make a ruling to transfer the case to the People's Court that has jurisdiction.

<a id="art-36"></a>第三十六条　两个以上人民法院都有管辖权的诉讼，先立案的人民法院不得将案件移送给另一个有管辖权的人民法院。人民法院在立案前发现其他有管辖权的人民法院已先立案的，不得重复立案；立案后发现其他有管辖权的人民法院已先立案的，裁定将案件移送给先立案的人民法院。

**Article 36** In a lawsuit over which two or more People's Courts have jurisdiction, the People's Court that first dockets the case shall not transfer the case to another People's Court that also has jurisdiction. Where a People's Court discovers before docketing that another People's Court which also has jurisdiction has already docketed the case, it shall not docket the case again; where it discovers after docketing that another People's Court which also has jurisdiction has already docketed the case, it shall make a ruling to transfer the case to the People's Court that first docketed the case.

<a id="art-37"></a>第三十七条　案件受理后，受诉人民法院的管辖权不受当事人住所地、经常居住地变更的影响。

**Article 37** After a case is accepted, the jurisdiction of the People's Court that accepted the case shall not be affected by changes in the parties' places of domicile or habitual residence.

<a id="art-38"></a>第三十八条　有管辖权的人民法院受理案件后，不得以行政区域变更为由，将案件移送给变更后有管辖权的人民法院。判决后的上诉案件和依审判监督程序提审的案件，由原审人民法院的上级人民法院进行审判；上级人民法院指令再审、发回重审的案件，由原审人民法院再审或者重审。

**Article 38** After a People's Court with jurisdiction accepts a case, it shall not transfer the case to the People's Court that has jurisdiction after the change on the ground of a change in administrative division. For appealed cases after judgment and cases brought up for trial under the trial supervision procedure, the trial shall be conducted by the People's Court at the next higher level of the original trial People's Court; where the People's Court at the next higher level orders a retrial or remands the case for retrial, the retrial or retrial shall be conducted by the original trial People's Court.

<a id="art-39"></a>第三十九条　人民法院对管辖异议审查后确定有管辖权的，不因当事人提起反诉、增加或者变更诉讼请求等改变管辖，但违反级别管辖、专属管辖规定的除外。  
人民法院发回重审或者按第一审程序再审的案件，当事人提出管辖异议的，人民法院不予审查。

**Article 39** Where a People's Court, after examining a challenge to jurisdiction, determines that it has jurisdiction, the jurisdiction shall not be changed by the party's filing of a counterclaim, addition or modification of claims, etc., unless it violates the provisions on level jurisdiction or exclusive jurisdiction.  
Where a People's Court remands a case for retrial or retries a case under the first-instance procedure, and a party raises a challenge to jurisdiction, the People's Court shall not review it.

<a id="art-40"></a>第四十条　依照[民事诉讼法第三十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-38)第二款规定，发生管辖权争议的两个人民法院因协商不成报请它们的共同上级人民法院指定管辖时，双方为同属一个地、市辖区的基层人民法院的，由该地、市的中级人民法院及时指定管辖；同属一个省、自治区、直辖市的两个人民法院的，由该省、自治区、直辖市的高级人民法院及时指定管辖；双方为跨省、自治区、直辖市的人民法院，高级人民法院协商不成的，由最高人民法院及时指定管辖。  
依照前款规定报请上级人民法院指定管辖时，应当逐级进行。

**Article 40** When, in accordance with the provisions of paragraph 2 of Article 38 of the Civil Procedure Law, two People's Courts that have a jurisdictional dispute cannot resolve it through consultation and refer the matter to their common superior People's Court for designation of jurisdiction, if both are Primary People's Courts within the same prefecture or city, the Intermediate People's Court of that prefecture or city shall promptly designate jurisdiction; if both are People's Courts within the same province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government, the Higher People's Court of that province, autonomous region, or municipality shall promptly designate jurisdiction; if the two are People's Courts from different provinces, autonomous regions, or municipalities directly under the Central Government, and the Higher People's Courts cannot reach an agreement, the Supreme People's Court shall promptly designate jurisdiction.  
When referring the matter to the superior People's Court for designation of jurisdiction in accordance with the preceding paragraph, the referral shall be made level by level.

<a id="art-41"></a>第四十一条　人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第三十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-38)第二款规定指定管辖的，应当作出裁定。  
对报请上级人民法院指定管辖的案件，下级人民法院应当中止审理。指定管辖裁定作出前，下级人民法院对案件作出判决、裁定的，上级人民法院应当在裁定指定管辖的同时，一并撤销下级人民法院的判决、裁定。

**Article 41** A People's Court that designates jurisdiction in accordance with paragraph 2 of Article 38 of the Civil Procedure Law shall make a ruling.  
For a case referred to a superior People's Court for designation of jurisdiction, the lower People's Court shall suspend the trial. If, before the ruling on designation of jurisdiction is made, the lower People's Court renders a judgment or ruling on the case, the superior People's Court shall, at the same time as it rules on the designation of jurisdiction, rescind the judgment or ruling of the lower People's Court.

<a id="art-42"></a>第四十二条　下列第一审民事案件，人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第三十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-39)第一款规定，可以在开庭前交下级人民法院审理：  
（一）破产程序中有关债务人的诉讼案件；  
（二）当事人人数众多且不方便诉讼的案件；  
（三）最高人民法院确定的其他类型案件。  
人民法院交下级人民法院审理前，应当报请其上级人民法院批准。上级人民法院批准后，人民法院应当裁定将案件交下级人民法院审理。

**Article 42** For the following first-instance civil cases, the People's Court may, in accordance with paragraph 1 of Article 39 of the Civil Procedure Law, transfer them to a lower People's Court for trial before the hearing:  
(1) Cases concerning the debtor in bankruptcy proceedings;  
(2) Cases involving a large number of parties and inconvenience for litigation;  
(3) Other types of cases designated by the Supreme People's Court.  
Before a People's Court transfers a case to a lower People's Court for trial, it shall report to its superior People's Court for approval. After the superior People's Court approves, the People's Court shall make a ruling to transfer the case to the lower People's Court for trial.

### 二、回避

<a id="art-43"></a>第四十三条　审判人员有下列情形之一的，应当自行回避，当事人有权申请其回避：  
（一）是本案当事人或者当事人近亲属的；  
（二）本人或者其近亲属与本案有利害关系的；  
（三）担任过本案的证人、鉴定人、辩护人、诉讼代理人、翻译人员的；  
（四）是本案诉讼代理人近亲属的；  
（五）本人或者其近亲属持有本案非上市公司当事人的股份或者股权的；  
（六）与本案当事人或者诉讼代理人有其他利害关系，可能影响公正审理的。

**Article 43** In any of the following circumstances, a judicial officer shall withdraw on his/her own initiative, and a party has the right to apply for his/her withdrawal:  
(1) He/she is a party to the case or a close relative of a party;  
(2) He/she or a close relative of his/hers has an interest in the case;  
(3) He/she has served as a witness, expert evaluator, defender, litigation agent, or translator in the case;  
(4) He/she is a close relative of a litigation agent in the case;  
(5) He/she or a close relative of his/hers holds shares or equity of a non-listed company that is a party to the case;  
(6) He/she has any other relationship of interest with a party or a litigation agent in the case that may affect the impartial trial.

<a id="art-44"></a>第四十四条　审判人员有下列情形之一的，当事人有权申请其回避：  
（一）接受本案当事人及其受托人宴请，或者参加由其支付费用的活动的；  
（二）索取、接受本案当事人及其受托人财物或者其他利益的；  
（三）违反规定会见本案当事人、诉讼代理人的；  
（四）为本案当事人推荐、介绍诉讼代理人，或者为律师、其他人员介绍代理本案的；  
（五）向本案当事人及其受托人借用款物的；  
（六）有其他不正当行为，可能影响公正审理的。

**Article 44** In any of the following circumstances, a party has the right to apply for the withdrawal of a judicial officer:  
(1) He/she accepts banquets from the parties to the case or their representatives, or participates in activities paid for by them;  
(2) He/she solicits or accepts property or other benefits from the parties to the case or their representatives;  
(3) He/she meets with a party or a litigation agent in the case in violation of regulations;  
(4) He/she recommends or introduces a litigation agent to a party in the case, or introduces a lawyer or other person to represent the case;  
(5) He/she borrows money or property from a party to the case or their representatives;  
(6) He/she engages in any other improper conduct that may affect the impartial trial.

<a id="art-45"></a>第四十五条　在一个审判程序中参与过本案审判工作的审判人员，不得再参与该案其他程序的审判。  
发回重审的案件，在一审法院作出裁判后又进入第二审程序的，原第二审程序中审判人员不受前款规定的限制。

**Article 45** A judicial officer who has participated in the trial of a case in one trial procedure shall not participate in the trial of the same case in another procedure.  
For a case remanded for retrial, if it enters the second-instance procedure after the first-instance court renders a judgment, the judicial officers who participated in the original second-instance procedure shall not be subject to the restriction in the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-46"></a>第四十六条　审判人员有应当回避的情形，没有自行回避，当事人也没有申请其回避的，由院长或者审判委员会决定其回避。

**Article 46** Where a judicial officer is in a circumstance requiring withdrawal but neither withdraws on his/her own initiative nor is applied for withdrawal by a party, the president of the court or the judicial committee shall decide on his/her withdrawal.

<a id="art-47"></a>第四十七条　人民法院应当依法告知当事人对合议庭组成人员、独任审判员和书记员等人员有申请回避的权利。

**Article 47** A People's Court shall, according to law, inform the parties of their right to apply for the withdrawal of the members of the collegial panel, the sole judge, the court clerk, and other personnel.

<a id="art-48"></a>第四十八条　[民事诉讼法第四十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-47)所称的审判人员，包括参与本案审理的人民法院院长、副院长、审判委员会委员、庭长、副庭长、审判员和人民陪审员。

**Article 48** The term "judicial officers" as used in Article 47 of the Civil Procedure Law includes the president, vice-president, members of the judicial committee, division chiefs, deputy division chiefs, judges, and people's assessors of the People's Court who participate in the trial of the case.

<a id="art-49"></a>第四十九条　书记员和执行员适用审判人员回避的有关规定。

**Article 49** The relevant provisions on withdrawal of judicial officers shall apply to court clerks and bailiffs.

### 三、诉讼参加人

<a id="art-50"></a>第五十条　法人的法定代表人以依法登记的为准，但法律另有规定的除外。依法不需要办理登记的法人，以其正职负责人为法定代表人；没有正职负责人的，以其主持工作的副职负责人为法定代表人。  
法定代表人已经变更，但未完成登记，变更后的法定代表人要求代表法人参加诉讼的，人民法院可以准许。  
其他组织，以其主要负责人为代表人。

**Article 50** The legal representative of a legal person shall be the person registered according to law, unless otherwise provided by law. For a legal person that is not required to undergo registration according to law, its principal responsible person shall be the legal representative; if there is no principal responsible person, the deputy responsible person in charge of work shall be the legal representative.  
Where the legal representative has changed but the change has not been registered, and the changed legal representative requests to represent the legal person in litigation, the People's Court may permit it.  
For any other organization, its principal responsible person shall be the representative.

<a id="art-51"></a>第五十一条　在诉讼中，法人的法定代表人变更的，由新的法定代表人继续进行诉讼，并应向人民法院提交新的法定代表人身份证明书。原法定代表人进行的诉讼行为有效。  
前款规定，适用于其他组织参加的诉讼。

**Article 51** In litigation, if the legal representative of a legal person changes, the new legal representative shall continue the litigation and shall submit a certificate of identity of the new legal representative to the People's Court. The litigation acts performed by the former legal representative shall be valid.  
The provisions of the preceding paragraph shall apply to litigation in which any other organization participates.

<a id="art-52"></a>第五十二条　[民事诉讼法第五十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-51)规定的其他组织是指合法成立、有一定的组织机构和财产，但又不具备法人资格的组织，包括：  
（一）依法登记领取营业执照的个人独资企业；  
（二）依法登记领取营业执照的合伙企业；  
（三）依法登记领取我国营业执照的中外合作经营企业、外资企业；  
（四）依法成立的社会团体的分支机构、代表机构；  
（五）依法设立并领取营业执照的法人的分支机构；  
（六）依法设立并领取营业执照的商业银行、政策性银行和非银行金融机构的分支机构；  
（七）经依法登记领取营业执照的乡镇企业、街道企业；  
（八）其他符合本条规定条件的组织。

**Article 52** The "other organizations" as referred to in Article 51 of the Civil Procedure Law mean organizations that are lawfully established, have a certain organizational structure and property, but do not possess the status of a legal person, including:  
(1) Sole proprietorship enterprises that are registered according to law and have obtained a business license;  
(2) Partnership enterprises that are registered according to law and have obtained a business license;  
(3) Chinese-foreign contractual joint ventures and foreign-funded enterprises that are registered according to law and have obtained a business license in China;  
(4) Branches and representative offices of social organizations lawfully established;  
(5) Branches of legal persons that are lawfully established and have obtained a business license;  
(6) Branches of commercial banks, policy banks, and non-bank financial institutions that are lawfully established and have obtained a business license;  
(7) Township enterprises and street enterprises that are registered according to law and have obtained a business license;  
(8) Other organizations that meet the conditions provided in this Article.

<a id="art-53"></a>第五十三条　法人非依法设立的分支机构，或者虽依法设立，但没有领取营业执照的分支机构，以设立该分支机构的法人为当事人。

**Article 53** For a branch established by a legal person not according to law, or a branch that, although established according to law, has not obtained a business license, the legal person that established the branch shall be the party.

<a id="art-54"></a>第五十四条　以挂靠形式从事民事活动，当事人请求由挂靠人和被挂靠人依法承担民事责任的，该挂靠人和被挂靠人为共同诉讼人。

**Article 54** Where a person engages in civil activities in the form of affiliation, and a party requests that the affiliate and the affiliated party bear civil liability according to law, the affiliate and the affiliated party shall be co-litigants.

<a id="art-55"></a>第五十五条　在诉讼中，一方当事人死亡，需要等待继承人表明是否参加诉讼的，裁定中止诉讼。人民法院应当及时通知继承人作为当事人承担诉讼，被继承人已经进行的诉讼行为对承担诉讼的继承人有效。

**Article 55** In litigation, where a party dies and it is necessary to await the heir's decision on whether to participate in the litigation, the litigation shall be suspended by ruling. The People's Court shall promptly notify the heir to assume the litigation as a party. The litigation acts already performed by the decedent shall be valid against the heir who assumes the litigation.

<a id="art-56"></a>第五十六条　法人或者其他组织的工作人员执行工作任务造成他人损害的，该法人或者其他组织为当事人。

**Article 56** Where an employee of a legal person or any other organization causes damage to another person in the performance of work tasks, the legal person or the other organization shall be the party.

<a id="art-57"></a>第五十七条　提供劳务一方因劳务造成他人损害，受害人提起诉讼的，以接受劳务一方为被告。

**Article 57** Where a person providing labor services causes damage to another person in the course of providing such services, and the victim brings an action, the party accepting the labor services shall be the defendant.

<a id="art-58"></a>第五十八条　在劳务派遣期间，被派遣的工作人员因执行工作任务造成他人损害的，以接受劳务派遣的用工单位为当事人。当事人主张劳务派遣单位承担责任的，该劳务派遣单位为共同被告。

**Article 58** During the period of labor dispatch, where a dispatched worker causes damage to another person in the performance of work tasks, the entity accepting the labor dispatch shall be the party. Where a party claims that the labor dispatch unit shall bear liability, the labor dispatch unit shall be a co-defendant.

<a id="art-59"></a>第五十九条　在诉讼中，个体工商户以营业执照上登记的经营者为当事人。有字号的，以营业执照上登记的字号为当事人，但应同时注明该字号经营者的基本信息。  
营业执照上登记的经营者与实际经营者不一致的，以登记的经营者和实际经营者为共同诉讼人。

**Article 59** In litigation, an individual industrial or commercial household shall have the operator registered on the business license as the party. If it has a trade name, the trade name registered on the business license shall be the party, but the basic information of the operator of such trade name shall also be indicated.  
Where the operator registered on the business license is inconsistent with the actual operator, the registered operator and the actual operator shall be co-litigants.

<a id="art-60"></a>第六十条　在诉讼中，未依法登记领取营业执照的个人合伙的全体合伙人为共同诉讼人。个人合伙有依法核准登记的字号的，应在法律文书中注明登记的字号。全体合伙人可以推选代表人；被推选的代表人，应由全体合伙人出具推选书。

**Article 60** In litigation, all partners of an individual partnership that has not registered and obtained a business license according to law shall be co-litigants. If the individual partnership has a trade name lawfully approved and registered, such registered trade name shall be indicated in the legal documents. All partners may elect a representative; the elected representative shall be certified by a letter of authorization issued by all partners.

<a id="art-61"></a>第六十一条　当事人之间的纠纷经人民调解委员会或者其他依法设立的调解组织调解达成协议后，一方当事人不履行调解协议，另一方当事人向人民法院提起诉讼的，应以对方当事人为被告。

**Article 61** Where a dispute between parties is mediated and an agreement is reached by a People's Mediation Committee or any other mediation organization established according to law, and one party fails to perform the mediation agreement, and the other party brings an action in a People's Court, the other party shall be the defendant.

<a id="art-62"></a>第六十二条　下列情形，以行为人为当事人：  
（一）法人或者其他组织应登记而未登记，行为人即以该法人或者其他组织名义进行民事活动的；  
（二）行为人没有代理权、超越代理权或者代理权终止后以被代理人名义进行民事活动的，但相对人有理由相信行为人有代理权的除外；  
（三）法人或者其他组织依法终止后，行为人仍以其名义进行民事活动的。

**Article 62** In the following circumstances, the actor shall be the party:  
(1) Where a legal person or any other organization is required to register but fails to do so, and the actor engages in civil activities in the name of the legal person or the other organization;  
(2) Where an actor engages in civil activities in the name of a principal without authority, beyond the scope of authority, or after the termination of authority, unless the counterparty has reason to believe that the actor has authority;  
(3) Where a legal person or any other organization is lawfully terminated, but the actor still engages in civil activities in its name.

<a id="art-63"></a>第六十三条　企业法人合并的，因合并前的民事活动发生的纠纷，以合并后的企业为当事人；企业法人分立的，因分立前的民事活动发生的纠纷，以分立后的企业为共同诉讼人。

**Article 63** Where an enterprise legal person is merged, the enterprise after the merger shall be the party in disputes arising from civil activities before the merger; where an enterprise legal person is divided, the enterprises after the division shall be co-litigants in disputes arising from civil activities before the division.

<a id="art-64"></a>第六十四条　企业法人解散的，依法清算并注销前，以该企业法人为当事人；未依法清算即被注销的，以该企业法人的股东、发起人或者出资人为当事人。

**Article 64** Where an enterprise legal person is dissolved, before it is liquidated and cancelled according to law, the enterprise legal person shall be the party; if it is cancelled without liquidation according to law, the shareholders, founders, or contributors of the enterprise legal person shall be the parties.

<a id="art-65"></a>第六十五条　借用业务介绍信、合同专用章、盖章的空白合同书或者银行账户的，出借单位和借用人为共同诉讼人。

**Article 65** Where a party borrows a business reference letter, a special contract seal, a blank contract with a seal, or a bank account from another party, the lender and the borrower shall be co-litigants.

<a id="art-66"></a>第六十六条　因保证合同纠纷提起的诉讼，债权人向保证人和被保证人一并主张权利的，人民法院应当将保证人和被保证人列为共同被告。保证合同约定为一般保证，债权人仅起诉保证人的，人民法院应当通知被保证人作为共同被告参加诉讼；债权人仅起诉被保证人的，可以只列被保证人为被告。

**Article 66** In an action brought due to a dispute over a guarantee contract, if the creditor asserts rights against both the guarantor and the guaranteed person, the People's Court shall list the guarantor and the guaranteed person as co-defendants. If the guarantee contract stipulates general guarantee and the creditor only sues the guarantor, the People's Court shall notify the guaranteed person to participate in the proceedings as a co-defendant; if the creditor only sues the guaranteed person, only the guaranteed person may be listed as the defendant.

<a id="art-67"></a>第六十七条　无民事行为能力人、限制民事行为能力人造成他人损害的，无民事行为能力人、限制民事行为能力人和其监护人为共同被告。

**Article 67** Where a person without civil capacity or a person with limited civil capacity causes damage to another person, such person and his or her guardian shall be co-defendants.

<a id="art-68"></a>第六十八条　居民委员会、村民委员会或者村民小组与他人发生民事纠纷的，居民委员会、村民委员会或者有独立财产的村民小组为当事人。

**Article 68** Where a residents' committee, villagers' committee or villagers' group has a civil dispute with another person, the residents' committee, villagers' committee or villagers' group with independent property shall be the party.

<a id="art-69"></a>第六十九条　对侵害死者遗体、遗骨以及姓名、肖像、名誉、荣誉、隐私等行为提起诉讼的，死者的近亲属为当事人。

**Article 69** If an action is brought against an act that infringes upon the remains, bones, name, portrait, reputation, honor, privacy, etc. of a deceased person, the close relatives of the deceased person shall be the parties.

<a id="art-70"></a>第七十条　在继承遗产的诉讼中，部分继承人起诉的，人民法院应通知其他继承人作为共同原告参加诉讼；被通知的继承人不愿意参加诉讼又未明确表示放弃实体权利的，人民法院仍应将其列为共同原告。

**Article 70** In an action concerning succession of an estate, if some heirs bring the action, the People's Court shall notify the other heirs to participate in the proceedings as co-plaintiffs; if a notified heir is unwilling to participate in the proceedings and has not explicitly expressed waiver of substantive rights, the People's Court shall still list that heir as a co-plaintiff.

<a id="art-71"></a>第七十一条　原告起诉被代理人和代理人，要求承担连带责任的，被代理人和代理人为共同被告。  
原告起诉代理人和相对人，要求承担连带责任的，代理人和相对人为共同被告。

**Article 71** Where the plaintiff sues the principal and the agent, demanding that they bear joint and several liability, the principal and the agent shall be co-defendants.  
Where the plaintiff sues the agent and the counterparty, demanding that they bear joint and several liability, the agent and the counterparty shall be co-defendants.

<a id="art-72"></a>第七十二条　共有财产权受到他人侵害，部分共有权人起诉的，其他共有权人为共同诉讼人。

**Article 72** Where a joint property right is infringed by another person and some co-owners bring an action, the other co-owners shall be co-litigants.

<a id="art-73"></a>第七十三条　必须共同进行诉讼的当事人没有参加诉讼的，人民法院应当依照[民事诉讼法第一百三十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-135)的规定，通知其参加；当事人也可以向人民法院申请追加。人民法院对当事人提出的申请，应当进行审查，申请理由不成立的，裁定驳回；申请理由成立的，书面通知被追加的当事人参加诉讼。

**Article 73** If a party who must jointly participate in the proceedings fails to do so, the People's Court shall, in accordance with Article 135 of the Civil Procedure Law, notify the party to participate; the party may also apply to the People's Court for joinder. The People's Court shall review the application filed by the party; if the grounds for the application are not established, the People's Court shall rule to reject it; if the grounds are established, the People's Court shall notify the joined party in writing to participate in the proceedings.

<a id="art-74"></a>第七十四条　人民法院追加共同诉讼的当事人时，应当通知其他当事人。应当追加的原告，已明确表示放弃实体权利的，可不予追加；既不愿意参加诉讼，又不放弃实体权利的，仍应追加为共同原告，其不参加诉讼，不影响人民法院对案件的审理和依法作出判决。

**Article 74** When the People's Court joins a party to a joint action, it shall notify the other parties. If a plaintiff to be joined has explicitly expressed waiver of substantive rights, joinder may be waived; if the plaintiff is neither willing to participate in the action nor waives substantive rights, the plaintiff shall still be joined as a co-plaintiff, and the failure of such plaintiff to participate shall not affect the People's Court's trial of the case and the rendition of a judgment according to law.

<a id="art-75"></a>第七十五条　[民事诉讼法第五十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-56)、第五十七条和第二百零六条规定的人数众多，一般指十人以上。

**Article 75** The term 'large number of persons' as provided in Articles 56, 57 and 206 of the Civil Procedure Law generally refers to ten or more persons.

<a id="art-76"></a>第七十六条　依照[民事诉讼法第五十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-56)规定，当事人一方人数众多在起诉时确定的，可以由全体当事人推选共同的代表人，也可以由部分当事人推选自己的代表人；推选不出代表人的当事人，在必要的共同诉讼中可以自己参加诉讼，在普通的共同诉讼中可以另行起诉。

**Article 76** Where, in accordance with Article 56 of the Civil Procedure Law, a large number of persons on one side are determined at the time of prosecution, the entire group of parties may elect a common representative, or some parties may elect their own representative; parties who fail to elect a representative may participate in the action on their own in a necessary joint action, or may file a separate action in an ordinary joint action.

<a id="art-77"></a>第七十七条　根据[民事诉讼法第五十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-57)规定，当事人一方人数众多在起诉时不确定的，由当事人推选代表人。当事人推选不出的，可以由人民法院提出人选与当事人协商；协商不成的，也可以由人民法院在起诉的当事人中指定代表人。

**Article 77** In accordance with Article 57 of the Civil Procedure Law, where a large number of persons on one side are not determined at the time of prosecution, the parties shall elect a representative. If the parties fail to elect one, the People's Court may propose candidates and consult with the parties; if consultation fails, the People's Court may also designate a representative from among the parties who have initiated the action.

<a id="art-78"></a>第七十八条　[民事诉讼法第五十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-56)和第五十七条规定的代表人为二至五人，每位代表人可以委托一至二人作为诉讼代理人。

**Article 78** The representatives provided for in Articles 56 and 57 of the Civil Procedure Law shall be two to five persons, and each representative may appoint one or two persons as litigation agents.

<a id="art-79"></a>第七十九条　依照[民事诉讼法第五十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-57)规定受理的案件，人民法院可以发出公告，通知权利人向人民法院登记。公告期间根据案件的具体情况确定，但不得少于三十日。

**Article 79** For a case accepted in accordance with Article 57 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court may issue a public notice to inform the right holders to register with the People's Court. The period of the public notice shall be determined according to the specific circumstances of the case, but shall not be less than thirty days.

<a id="art-80"></a>第八十条　根据[民事诉讼法第五十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-57)规定向人民法院登记的权利人，应当证明其与对方当事人的法律关系和所受到的损害。证明不了的，不予登记，权利人可以另行起诉。人民法院的裁判在登记的范围内执行。未参加登记的权利人提起诉讼，人民法院认定其请求成立的，裁定适用人民法院已作出的判决、裁定。

**Article 80** Right holders who register with the People's Court in accordance with Article 57 of the Civil Procedure Law shall prove their legal relationship with the opposing party and the damage suffered. If they fail to prove, the registration shall not be made, and the right holder may file a separate action. The judgment or ruling of the People's Court shall be enforced within the scope of the registration. If a right holder who did not participate in the registration initiates an action and the People's Court finds the claim established, it shall rule to apply the judgment or ruling already made by the People's Court.

<a id="art-81"></a>第八十一条　根据[民事诉讼法第五十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-59)的规定，有独立请求权的第三人有权向人民法院提出诉讼请求和事实、理由，成为当事人；无独立请求权的第三人，可以申请或者由人民法院通知参加诉讼。  
第一审程序中未参加诉讼的第三人，申请参加第二审程序的，人民法院可以准许。

**Article 81** In accordance with Article 59 of the Civil Procedure Law, a third party with an independent claim has the right to file a statement of claims and the facts and reasons therewith to the People's Court and become a party; a third party without an independent claim may apply to participate in the proceedings or be notified by the People's Court to do so.  
If a third party who did not participate in the proceedings in the first instance applies to participate in the second instance proceedings, the People's Court may permit it.

<a id="art-82"></a>第八十二条　在一审诉讼中，无独立请求权的第三人无权提出管辖异议，无权放弃、变更诉讼请求或者申请撤诉，被判决承担民事责任的，有权提起上诉。

**Article 82** In an action of first instance, a third party without an independent claim shall have no right to raise an objection to jurisdiction, nor to waive or change the claims or apply for withdrawal of the action; if a judgment orders such third party to bear civil liability, the third party shall have the right to appeal.

<a id="art-83"></a>第八十三条　在诉讼中，无民事行为能力人、限制民事行为能力人的监护人是他的法定代理人。事先没有确定监护人的，可以由有监护资格的人协商确定；协商不成的，由人民法院在他们之中指定诉讼中的法定代理人。当事人没有[民法典第二十七条](../../民事与商事/民法典/中华人民共和国民法典.md#art-27)、第二十八条规定的监护人的，可以指定[民法典第三十二条](../../民事与商事/民法典/中华人民共和国民法典.md#art-32)规定的有关组织担任诉讼中的法定代理人。

**Article 83** In an action, the guardian of a person without civil capacity or a person with limited civil capacity shall be his or her legal representative. If no guardian has been determined beforehand, persons qualified to be guardians may agree on one; if no agreement is reached, the People's Court shall designate a legal representative for the action from among them. If the party has no guardian as provided in Articles 27 and 28 of the Civil Code of the People's Republic of China, the relevant organization provided in Article 32 of the Civil Code of the People's Republic of China may be designated as the legal representative for the action.

<a id="art-84"></a>第八十四条　无民事行为能力人、限制民事行为能力人以及其他依法不能作为诉讼代理人的，当事人不得委托其作为诉讼代理人。

**Article 84** A party shall not appoint as a litigation agent a person without civil capacity, a person with limited civil capacity, or any other person who cannot serve as a litigation agent according to law.

<a id="art-85"></a>第八十五条　根据[民事诉讼法第六十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-61)第二款第二项规定，与当事人有夫妻、直系血亲、三代以内旁系血亲、近姻亲关系以及其他有抚养、赡养关系的亲属，可以当事人近亲属的名义作为诉讼代理人。

**Article 85** In accordance with Item (2) of Paragraph 2 of Article 61 of the Civil Procedure Law, relatives who have a spousal relationship, lineal blood relationship, collateral blood relationship within three generations, close in-law relationship, or other relationship of support or maintenance with the party may act as litigation agents in the name of the party's close relative.

<a id="art-86"></a>第八十六条　根据[民事诉讼法第六十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-61)第二款第二项规定，与当事人有合法劳动人事关系的职工，可以当事人工作人员的名义作为诉讼代理人。

**Article 86** In accordance with Item (2) of Paragraph 2 of Article 61 of the Civil Procedure Law, an employee who has a lawful labor or personnel relationship with the party may act as a litigation agent in the name of the party's staff member.

<a id="art-87"></a>第八十七条　根据[民事诉讼法第六十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-61)第二款第三项规定，有关社会团体推荐公民担任诉讼代理人的，应当符合下列条件：  
（一）社会团体属于依法登记设立或者依法免予登记设立的非营利性法人组织；  
（二）被代理人属于该社会团体的成员，或者当事人一方住所地位于该社会团体的活动地域；  
（三）代理事务属于该社会团体章程载明的业务范围；  
（四）被推荐的公民是该社会团体的负责人或者与该社会团体有合法劳动人事关系的工作人员。  
专利代理人经中华全国专利代理人协会推荐，可以在专利纠纷案件中担任诉讼代理人。

**Article 87** Where, in accordance with Item (3) of Paragraph 2 of Article 61 of the Civil Procedure Law, a relevant social organization recommends a citizen to act as a litigation agent, the following conditions shall be satisfied:  
(1) The social organization is a non-profit legal person organization established by registration according to law or exempted from registration according to law;  
(2) The principal is a member of the social organization, or the domicile of one party is located within the area of activity of the social organization;  
(3) The agency matter falls within the business scope specified in the articles of association of the social organization;  
(4) The recommended citizen is the responsible person of the social organization or a staff member who has a lawful labor or personnel relationship with the social organization.  
A patent agent recommended by the All-China Patent Attorneys Association may act as a litigation agent in patent dispute cases.

<a id="art-88"></a>第八十八条　诉讼代理人除根据[民事诉讼法第六十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-62)规定提交授权委托书外，还应当按照下列规定向人民法院提交相关材料：  
（一）律师应当提交律师执业证、律师事务所证明材料；  
（二）基层法律服务工作者应当提交法律服务工作者执业证、基层法律服务所出具的介绍信以及当事人一方位于本辖区内的证明材料；  
（三）当事人的近亲属应当提交身份证件和与委托人有近亲属关系的证明材料；  
（四）当事人的工作人员应当提交身份证件和与当事人有合法劳动人事关系的证明材料；  
（五）当事人所在社区、单位推荐的公民应当提交身份证件、推荐材料和当事人属于该社区、单位的证明材料；  
（六）有关社会团体推荐的公民应当提交身份证件和符合[本解释第八十七条](#art-87)规定条件的证明材料。

**Article 88** In addition to submitting the power of attorney in accordance with Article 62 of the Civil Procedure Law, a litigation agent shall also submit the following relevant materials to the People's Court according to the following provisions:  
(1) A lawyer shall submit the lawyer's practicing certificate and the supporting materials from the law firm;  
(2) A primary-level legal service worker shall submit the legal service worker's practicing certificate, the letter of introduction issued by the primary-level legal service office, and the supporting materials showing that one party is located within the jurisdiction;  
(3) A close relative of a party shall submit the identification document and the supporting materials showing the close relative relationship with the principal;  
(4) A staff member of a party shall submit the identification document and the supporting materials showing the lawful labor or personnel relationship with the party;  
(5) A citizen recommended by the community or work unit to which the party belongs shall submit the identification document, the recommendation materials, and the supporting materials showing that the party belongs to such community or work unit;  
(6) A citizen recommended by a relevant social organization shall submit the identification document and the supporting materials showing that the conditions provided in Article 87 of this Interpretation are satisfied.

<a id="art-89"></a>第八十九条　当事人向人民法院提交的授权委托书，应当在开庭审理前送交人民法院。授权委托书仅写“全权代理”而无具体授权的，诉讼代理人无权代为承认、放弃、变更诉讼请求，进行和解，提出反诉或者提起上诉。  
适用简易程序审理的案件，双方当事人同时到庭并径行开庭审理的，可以当场口头委托诉讼代理人，由人民法院记入笔录。

**Article 89** The power of attorney submitted by a party to the People's Court shall be delivered to the People's Court before the hearing. If the power of attorney only states 'general代理' without specific authorization, the litigation agent shall have no authority to admit, waive or modify the claims, reach a settlement, file a counterclaim or appeal.  
For a case tried under summary procedure, if both parties appear in court simultaneously and the trial proceeds directly, the parties may orally appoint a litigation agent on the spot, and the People's Court shall record it in the minutes.

### 四、证据

<a id="art-90"></a>第九十条　当事人对自己提出的诉讼请求所依据的事实或者反驳对方诉讼请求所依据的事实，应当提供证据加以证明，但法律另有规定的除外。  
在作出判决前，当事人未能提供证据或者证据不足以证明其事实主张的，由负有举证证明责任的当事人承担不利的后果。

**Article 90** A party shall provide evidence to prove the facts on which its claim is based or to rebut the facts on which the opposing party's claim is based, unless otherwise provided by law.  
Before a judgment is rendered, if a party fails to provide evidence or the evidence is insufficient to prove its factual assertions, the party bearing the burden of proof shall bear the adverse consequences.

<a id="art-91"></a>第九十一条　人民法院应当依照下列原则确定举证证明责任的承担，但法律另有规定的除外：  
（一）主张法律关系存在的当事人，应当对产生该法律关系的基本事实承担举证证明责任；  
（二）主张法律关系变更、消灭或者权利受到妨害的当事人，应当对该法律关系变更、消灭或者权利受到妨害的基本事实承担举证证明责任。

**Article 91** The People's Court shall determine the burden of proof in accordance with the following principles, unless otherwise provided by law:  
(1) The party asserting the existence of a legal relationship shall bear the burden of proof for the basic facts giving rise to such legal relationship;  
(2) The party asserting a change, termination, or impairment of a right in a legal relationship shall bear the burden of proof for the basic facts of such change, termination, or impairment.

<a id="art-92"></a>第九十二条　一方当事人在法庭审理中，或者在起诉状、答辩状、代理词等书面材料中，对于己不利的事实明确表示承认的，另一方当事人无需举证证明。  
对于涉及身份关系、国家利益、社会公共利益等应当由人民法院依职权调查的事实，不适用前款自认的规定。  
自认的事实与查明的事实不符的，人民法院不予确认。

**Article 92** If a party expressly admits facts unfavorable to itself during the court hearing or in written materials such as the statement of claim, statement of defense, or representation, the other party shall not be required to provide proof.  
The provisions on admission in the preceding paragraph shall not apply to facts concerning personal status, national interests, social public interests, etc., which shall be investigated by the People's Court ex officio.  
If the facts admitted are inconsistent with the facts ascertained, the People's Court shall not confirm them.

<a id="art-93"></a>第九十三条　下列事实，当事人无须举证证明：  
（一）自然规律以及定理、定律；  
（二）众所周知的事实；  
（三）根据法律规定推定的事实；  
（四）根据已知的事实和日常生活经验法则推定出的另一事实；  
（五）已为人民法院发生法律效力的裁判所确认的事实；  
（六）已为仲裁机构生效裁决所确认的事实；  
（七）已为有效公证文书所证明的事实。  
前款第二项至第四项规定的事实，当事人有相反证据足以反驳的除外；第五项至第七项规定的事实，当事人有相反证据足以推翻的除外。

**Article 93** The following facts need not be proved by the parties:  
(1) Natural laws, theorems, and laws;  
(2) Facts that are generally known;  
(3) Facts presumed according to legal provisions;  
(4) Facts inferred from known facts and everyday life experience rules;  
(5) Facts confirmed by a legally effective judgment or ruling of the People's Court;  
(6) Facts confirmed by an effective award of an arbitration institution;  
(7) Facts proven by a valid notarized document.  
For facts provided in Items (2) through (4) of the preceding paragraph, except where the opposing party has sufficient counter-evidence to rebut; for facts provided in Items (5) through (7), except where the opposing party has sufficient counter-evidence to overturn.

<a id="art-94"></a>第九十四条　[民事诉讼法第六十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-67)第二款规定的当事人及其诉讼代理人因客观原因不能自行收集的证据包括：  
（一）证据由国家有关部门保存，当事人及其诉讼代理人无权查阅调取的；  
（二）涉及国家秘密、商业秘密或者个人隐私的；  
（三）当事人及其诉讼代理人因客观原因不能自行收集的其他证据。  
当事人及其诉讼代理人因客观原因不能自行收集的证据，可以在举证期限届满前书面申请人民法院调查收集。

**Article 94** The evidence that a party and its litigation agent cannot collect on their own due to objective reasons as provided in Paragraph 2 of Article 67 of the Civil Procedure Law includes:  
(1) Evidence kept by a relevant state authority which the party and its litigation agent have no right to inspect or obtain;  
(2) Evidence involving state secrets, trade secrets, or personal privacy;  
(3) Other evidence that the party and its litigation agent cannot collect on their own due to objective reasons.  
If the party and its litigation agent cannot collect evidence on their own due to objective reasons, they may apply in writing to the People's Court to investigate and collect it before the expiration of the time limit for producing evidence.

<a id="art-95"></a>第九十五条　当事人申请调查收集的证据，与待证事实无关联、对证明待证事实无意义或者其他无调查收集必要的，人民法院不予准许。

**Article 95** If the evidence that a party applies for investigation and collection is irrelevant to the facts to be proved, meaningless for proving the facts to be proved, or otherwise unnecessary for investigation and collection, the People's Court shall not grant the application.

<a id="art-96"></a>第九十六条　[民事诉讼法第六十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-67)第二款规定的人民法院认为审理案件需要的证据包括：  
（一）涉及可能损害国家利益、社会公共利益的；  
（二）涉及身份关系的；  
（三）涉及[民事诉讼法第五十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-58)规定诉讼的；  
（四）当事人有恶意串通损害他人合法权益可能的；  
（五）涉及依职权追加当事人、中止诉讼、终结诉讼、回避等程序性事项的。  
除前款规定外，人民法院调查收集证据，应当依照当事人的申请进行。

**Article 96** The evidence that the People's Court considers necessary for the trial of a case as provided in Paragraph 2 of Article 67 of the Civil Procedure Law includes:  
(1) Evidence that may involve damage to national interests or social public interests;  
(2) Evidence involving personal status;  
(3) Evidence involving an action as provided in Article 58 of the Civil Procedure Law;  
(4) Evidence involving a possibility that a party may collude maliciously to harm the lawful rights and interests of others;  
(5) Evidence involving procedural matters such as joinder of parties ex officio, suspension of proceedings, termination of proceedings, or withdrawal.  
Except as provided in the preceding paragraph, the People's Court shall investigate and collect evidence upon a party's application.

<a id="art-97"></a>第九十七条　人民法院调查收集证据，应当由两人以上共同进行。调查材料要由调查人、被调查人、记录人签名、捺印或者盖章。

**Article 97** When the People's Court investigates and collects evidence, it shall be carried out by two or more persons. The investigation materials shall be signed, fingerprinted, or sealed by the investigator, the person investigated, and the recorder.

<a id="art-98"></a>第九十八条　当事人根据[民事诉讼法第八十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-84)第一款规定申请证据保全的，可以在举证期限届满前书面提出。  
证据保全可能对他人造成损失的，人民法院应当责令申请人提供相应的担保。

**Article 98** Where a party applies for preservation of evidence in accordance with Paragraph 1 of Article 84 of the Civil Procedure Law, the application may be made in writing before the expiration of the time limit for producing evidence.  
If the preservation of evidence may cause loss to another person, the People's Court shall order the applicant to provide corresponding security.

<a id="art-99"></a>第九十九条　人民法院应当在审理前的准备阶段确定当事人的举证期限。举证期限可以由当事人协商，并经人民法院准许。  
人民法院确定举证期限，第一审普通程序案件不得少于十五日，当事人提供新的证据的第二审案件不得少于十日。  
举证期限届满后，当事人对已经提供的证据，申请提供反驳证据或者对证据来源、形式等方面的瑕疵进行补正的，人民法院可以酌情再次确定举证期限，该期限不受前款规定的限制。

**Article 99** The People's Court shall determine the time limit for producing evidence of the parties during the preparatory stage before trial. The time limit for producing evidence may be agreed upon by the parties and shall be approved by the People's Court.  
Where the People's Court determines the time limit for producing evidence, for cases under ordinary procedure of first instance, the time limit shall not be less than fifteen days; for cases of second instance where a party provides new evidence, the time limit shall not be less than ten days.  
After the expiration of the time limit for producing evidence, if a party applies to provide rebuttal evidence or to cure defects in the source or form of the evidence already provided, the People's Court may, as it deems appropriate, set a new time limit for producing evidence, and such time limit shall not be restricted by the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-100"></a>第一百条　当事人申请延长举证期限的，应当在举证期限届满前向人民法院提出书面申请。  
申请理由成立的，人民法院应当准许，适当延长举证期限，并通知其他当事人。延长的举证期限适用于其他当事人。  
申请理由不成立的，人民法院不予准许，并通知申请人。

**Article 100** Where a party applies for extension of the time limit for producing evidence, it shall file a written application with the People's Court before the expiration of the time limit for producing evidence.  
If the grounds for the application are established, the People's Court shall grant the application, appropriately extend the time limit for producing evidence, and notify the other parties. The extended time limit for producing evidence shall apply to the other parties.  
If the grounds for the application are not established, the People's Court shall not grant the application and shall notify the applicant.

<a id="art-101"></a>第一百零一条　当事人逾期提供证据的，人民法院应当责令其说明理由，必要时可以要求其提供相应的证据。  
当事人因客观原因逾期提供证据，或者对方当事人对逾期提供证据未提出异议的，视为未逾期。

**Article 101** Where a party provides evidence beyond the time limit, the People's Court shall order it to explain the reasons, and may, if necessary, require it to provide corresponding evidence.  
If a party provides evidence beyond the time limit due to objective reasons, or if the opposing party does not raise an objection to the late production of evidence, the evidence shall be deemed as provided within the time limit.

<a id="art-102"></a>第一百零二条　当事人因故意或者重大过失逾期提供的证据，人民法院不予采纳。但该证据与案件基本事实有关的，人民法院应当采纳，并依照[民事诉讼法第六十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-68)、第一百一十八条第一款的规定予以训诫、罚款。  
当事人非因故意或者重大过失逾期提供的证据，人民法院应当采纳，并对当事人予以训诫。  
当事人一方要求另一方赔偿因逾期提供证据致使其增加的交通、住宿、就餐、误工、证人出庭作证等必要费用的，人民法院可予支持。

**Article 102** Where a party provides evidence beyond the time limit due to intentional conduct or gross negligence, the People's Court shall not admit the evidence. However, if such evidence is related to the basic facts of the case, the People's Court shall admit it and, in accordance with Article 68 and Paragraph 1 of Article 118 of the Civil Procedure Law, impose a reprimand or a fine.  
Where a party provides evidence beyond the time limit not due to intentional conduct or gross negligence, the People's Court shall admit the evidence and shall reprimand the party.  
If one party requests the other party to compensate for the necessary expenses incurred as a result of the late production of evidence, such as increased transportation, accommodation, meals, loss of working time, and witness attendance, the People's Court may support such request.

<a id="art-103"></a>第一百零三条　证据应当在法庭上出示，由当事人互相质证。未经当事人质证的证据，不得作为认定案件事实的根据。  
当事人在审理前的准备阶段认可的证据，经审判人员在庭审中说明后，视为质证过的证据。  
涉及国家秘密、商业秘密、个人隐私或者法律规定应当保密的证据，不得公开质证。

**Article 103** Evidence shall be presented in court and be subject to cross-examination by the parties. Evidence that has not been cross-examined by the parties shall not be used as the basis for ascertaining the facts of the case.  
Evidence admitted by the parties during the preparatory stage before trial shall, after being explained by the adjudicators during the court hearing, be deemed as evidence that has been cross-examined.  
Evidence involving state secrets, trade secrets, personal privacy, or other matters that shall be kept confidential according to law shall not be publicly cross-examined.

<a id="art-104"></a>第一百零四条　人民法院应当组织当事人围绕证据的真实性、合法性以及与待证事实的关联性进行质证，并针对证据有无证明力和证明力大小进行说明和辩论。  
能够反映案件真实情况、与待证事实相关联、来源和形式符合法律规定的证据，应当作为认定案件事实的根据。

**Article 104** The People's Court shall organize the parties to conduct cross-examination on the authenticity, legality, and relevance of the evidence to the facts to be proved, and to present arguments and debate regarding whether the evidence has probative value and the extent of such probative value.  
Evidence that can reflect the true circumstances of the case, is relevant to the facts to be proved, and whose source and form comply with the provisions of law shall be used as the basis for ascertaining the facts of the case.

<a id="art-105"></a>第一百零五条　人民法院应当按照法定程序，全面、客观地审核证据，依照法律规定，运用逻辑推理和日常生活经验法则，对证据有无证明力和证明力大小进行判断，并公开判断的理由和结果。

**Article 105** The People's Court shall, in accordance with statutory procedures, examine evidence comprehensively and objectively, and, pursuant to the provisions of law, using logical reasoning and everyday life experience rules, determine whether evidence has probative value and the extent of such probative value, and shall make public the reasons and results of such determination.

<a id="art-106"></a>第一百零六条　对以严重侵害他人合法权益、违反法律禁止性规定或者严重违背公序良俗的方法形成或者获取的证据，不得作为认定案件事实的根据。

**Article 106** Evidence that is formed or obtained through means that seriously infringe upon the lawful rights and interests of others, violate prohibitive provisions of law, or seriously contravene public order and good customs, shall not be used as the basis for ascertaining the facts of the case.

<a id="art-107"></a>第一百零七条　在诉讼中，当事人为达成调解协议或者和解协议作出妥协而认可的事实，不得在后续的诉讼中作为对其不利的根据，但法律另有规定或者当事人均同意的除外。

**Article 107** In an action, facts admitted by a party as a compromise for reaching a mediation agreement or settlement agreement shall not be used as adverse evidence against that party in subsequent proceedings, unless otherwise provided by law or agreed by all parties.

<a id="art-108"></a>第一百零八条　对负有举证证明责任的当事人提供的证据，人民法院经审查并结合相关事实，确信待证事实的存在具有高度可能性的，应当认定该事实存在。  
对一方当事人为反驳负有举证证明责任的当事人所主张事实而提供的证据，人民法院经审查并结合相关事实，认为待证事实真伪不明的，应当认定该事实不存在。  
法律对于待证事实所应达到的证明标准另有规定的，从其规定。

**Article 108** Where, after examining the evidence provided by the party bearing the burden of proof and taking into account the relevant facts, the People's Court is convinced that the existence of the fact to be proved is highly probable, it shall find that fact to exist.  
Where, after examining the evidence provided by one party to rebut the facts asserted by the party bearing the burden of proof and taking into account the relevant facts, the People's Court determines that the truthfulness of the fact to be proved is unclear, it shall find that fact not to exist.  
If the law has otherwise provided for the standard of proof to be met for the fact to be proved, such provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-109"></a>第一百零九条　当事人对欺诈、胁迫、恶意串通事实的证明，以及对口头遗嘱或者赠与事实的证明，人民法院确信该待证事实存在的可能性能够排除合理怀疑的，应当认定该事实存在。

**Article 109** If, for the proof of fraud, duress, or malicious collusion, or for the proof of an oral will or a gift, the People's Court is convinced that the possibility of the existence of the fact to be proved can exclude reasonable doubt, it shall find that fact to exist.

<a id="art-110"></a>第一百一十条　人民法院认为有必要的，可以要求当事人本人到庭，就案件有关事实接受询问。在询问当事人之前，可以要求其签署保证书。  
保证书应当载明据实陈述、如有虚假陈述愿意接受处罚等内容。当事人应当在保证书上签名或者捺印。  
负有举证证明责任的当事人拒绝到庭、拒绝接受询问或者拒绝签署保证书，待证事实又欠缺其他证据证明的，人民法院对其主张的事实不予认定。

**Article 110** If the People's Court deems it necessary, it may require the party to appear in court to undergo interrogation concerning the relevant facts of the case. Before interrogating the party, the People's Court may require the party to sign an undertaking.  
The undertaking shall state that the party shall testify truthfully and shall be subject to sanctions if making false statements. The party shall sign or affix a fingerprint on the undertaking.  
If the party bearing the burden of proof refuses to appear in court, refuses to undergo interrogation, or refuses to sign the undertaking, and there is a lack of other evidence to prove the fact to be proved, the People's Court shall not find the fact asserted by the party.

<a id="art-111"></a>第一百一十一条　[民事诉讼法第七十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-73)规定的提交书证原件确有困难，包括下列情形：  
（一）书证原件遗失、灭失或者毁损的；  
（二）原件在对方当事人控制之下，经合法通知提交而拒不提交的；  
（三）原件在他人控制之下，而其有权不提交的；  
（四）原件因篇幅或者体积过大而不便提交的；  
（五）承担举证证明责任的当事人通过申请人民法院调查收集或者其他方式无法获得书证原件的。  
前款规定情形，人民法院应当结合其他证据和案件具体情况，审查判断书证复制品等能否作为认定案件事实的根据。

**Article 111** The circumstances where it is genuinely difficult to produce the original documentary evidence as provided in Article 73 of the Civil Procedure Law include:  
(1) The original documentary evidence is lost, destroyed, or damaged;  
(2) The original is in the control of the opposing party, and such party refuses to produce it after being lawfully notified to do so;  
(3) The original is in the control of another person, and such person has the right not to produce it;  
(4) The original is inconvenient to produce due to its length or size;  
(5) The party bearing the burden of proof cannot obtain the original documentary evidence through applying to the People's Court for investigation and collection or through other means.  
In the circumstances specified in the preceding paragraph, the People's Court shall, taking into account other evidence and the specific circumstances of the case, examine and determine whether a copy or other reproduction of the documentary evidence may be used as the basis for ascertaining the facts of the case.

<a id="art-112"></a>第一百一十二条　书证在对方当事人控制之下的，承担举证证明责任的当事人可以在举证期限届满前书面申请人民法院责令对方当事人提交。  
申请理由成立的，人民法院应当责令对方当事人提交，因提交书证所产生的费用，由申请人负担。对方当事人无正当理由拒不提交的，人民法院可以认定申请人所主张的书证内容为真实。

**Article 112** Where the documentary evidence is in the control of the opposing party, the party bearing the burden of proof may, before the expiration of the time limit for producing evidence, apply in writing to the People's Court to order the opposing party to produce it.  
If the grounds for the application are established, the People's Court shall order the opposing party to produce the documentary evidence, and the expenses incurred from producing the documentary evidence shall be borne by the applicant. If the opposing party refuses to produce it without justifiable reasons, the People's Court may deem the content of the documentary evidence asserted by the applicant to be true.

<a id="art-113"></a>第一百一十三条　持有书证的当事人以妨碍对方当事人使用为目的，毁灭有关书证或者实施其他致使书证不能使用行为的，人民法院可以依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-114)规定，对其处以罚款、拘留。

**Article 113** If a party in possession of documentary evidence destroys such documentary evidence or engages in other conduct rendering it unusable, with the purpose of hindering the opposing party from using it, the People's Court may, in accordance with Article 114 of the Civil Procedure Law, impose a fine or detention on that party.

<a id="art-114"></a>第一百一十四条　国家机关或者其他依法具有社会管理职能的组织，在其职权范围内制作的文书所记载的事项推定为真实，但有相反证据足以推翻的除外。必要时，人民法院可以要求制作文书的机关或者组织对文书的真实性予以说明。

**Article 114** Matters recorded in a document made by a state authority or other organization lawfully vested with social management functions within the scope of its functions shall be presumed to be true, except where there is sufficient counter-evidence to overturn such presumption. If necessary, the People's Court may require the authority or organization that made the document to explain the authenticity of the document.

<a id="art-115"></a>第一百一十五条　单位向人民法院提出的证明材料，应当由单位负责人及制作证明材料的人员签名或者盖章，并加盖单位印章。人民法院就单位出具的证明材料，可以向单位及制作证明材料的人员进行调查核实。必要时，可以要求制作证明材料的人员出庭作证。  
单位及制作证明材料的人员拒绝人民法院调查核实，或者制作证明材料的人员无正当理由拒绝出庭作证的，该证明材料不得作为认定案件事实的根据。

**Article 115** Evidentiary materials submitted by an entity to the People's Court shall be signed or sealed by the responsible person of the entity and the person who prepared the evidentiary materials, and shall be affixed with the seal of the entity. The People's Court may investigate and verify the evidentiary materials issued by an entity with the entity and the person who prepared them. If necessary, the People's Court may require the person who prepared the evidentiary materials to appear in court to testify.  
If the entity and the person who prepared the evidentiary materials refuse the investigation and verification by the People's Court, or if the person who prepared the evidentiary materials refuses to appear in court to testify without justifiable reasons, such evidentiary materials shall not be used as the basis for ascertaining the facts of the case.

<a id="art-116"></a>第一百一十六条　视听资料包括录音资料和影像资料。  
电子数据是指通过电子邮件、电子数据交换、网上聊天记录、博客、微博客、手机短信、电子签名、域名等形成或者存储在电子介质中的信息。  
存储在电子介质中的录音资料和影像资料，适用电子数据的规定。

**Article 116** Audio-visual materials include audio recordings and video recordings.  
Electronic data refers to information formed or stored in electronic media, such as through email, electronic data interchange, online chat records, blogs, microblogs, text messages, electronic signatures, domain names, etc.  
Audio recordings and video recordings stored in electronic media shall be subject to the provisions on electronic data.

<a id="art-117"></a>第一百一十七条　当事人申请证人出庭作证的，应当在举证期限届满前提出。  
符合[本解释第九十六条](#art-96)第一款规定情形的，人民法院可以依职权通知证人出庭作证。  
未经人民法院通知，证人不得出庭作证，但双方当事人同意并经人民法院准许的除外。

**Article 117** Where a party applies to have a witness testify in court, the application shall be made before the expiration of the time limit for adducing evidence.  
Where the circumstances set forth in paragraph 1 of Article 96 of this Interpretation are met, the People's Court may, on its own authority, summon a witness to testify in court.  
Without being summoned by the People's Court, a witness shall not testify in court, unless both parties agree and the People's Court approves.

<a id="art-118"></a>第一百一十八条　[民事诉讼法第七十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-77)规定的证人因履行出庭作证义务而支出的交通、住宿、就餐等必要费用，按照机关事业单位工作人员差旅费用和补贴标准计算；误工损失按照国家上年度职工日平均工资标准计算。  
人民法院准许证人出庭作证申请的，应当通知申请人预缴证人出庭作证费用。

**Article 118** The necessary expenses such as transportation, accommodation, and meals incurred by a witness for performing the obligation to testify in court as specified in Article 77 of the Civil Procedure Law shall be calculated according to the travel expense and subsidy standards for staff of government agencies and public institutions; the loss of working time shall be calculated according to the national average daily wage of employees in the previous year.  
Where the People's Court approves an application for a witness to testify in court, it shall notify the applicant to pay the witness's expenses for testifying in court in advance.

<a id="art-119"></a>第一百一十九条　人民法院在证人出庭作证前应当告知其如实作证的义务以及作伪证的法律后果，并责令其签署保证书，但无民事行为能力人和限制民事行为能力人除外。  
证人签署保证书适用本解释关于当事人签署保证书的规定。

**Article 119** Before a witness testifies in court, the People's Court shall inform the witness of the obligation to testify truthfully and the legal consequences of perjury, and shall order the witness to sign an undertaking, except for persons without capacity for civil conduct or with limited capacity for civil conduct.  
The signing of an undertaking by a witness shall be governed by the provisions of this Interpretation on the signing of an undertaking by a party.

<a id="art-120"></a>第一百二十条　证人拒绝签署保证书的，不得作证，并自行承担相关费用。

**Article 120** If a witness refuses to sign an undertaking, the witness shall not testify and shall bear the relevant expenses on its own.

<a id="art-121"></a>第一百二十一条　当事人申请鉴定，可以在举证期限届满前提出。申请鉴定的事项与待证事实无关联，或者对证明待证事实无意义的，人民法院不予准许。  
人民法院准许当事人鉴定申请的，应当组织双方当事人协商确定具备相应资格的鉴定人。当事人协商不成的，由人民法院指定。  
符合依职权调查收集证据条件的，人民法院应当依职权委托鉴定，在询问当事人的意见后，指定具备相应资格的鉴定人。

**Article 121** A party may apply for expert evaluation before the expiration of the time limit for adducing evidence. If the matter for which expert evaluation is applied for is irrelevant to the facts to be proved, or is of no significance in proving the facts to be proved, the People's Court shall not approve the application.  
Where the People's Court approves a party's application for expert evaluation, it shall organize the parties to consult and determine an expert with corresponding qualifications. If the parties fail to reach an agreement through consultation, the People's Court shall designate the expert.  
Where the conditions for investigating and collecting evidence on its own authority are met, the People's Court shall, on its own authority, commission an expert evaluation, and after consulting the parties' opinions, designate an expert with corresponding qualifications.

<a id="art-122"></a>第一百二十二条　当事人可以依照[民事诉讼法第八十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-82)的规定，在举证期限届满前申请一至二名具有专门知识的人出庭，代表当事人对鉴定意见进行质证，或者对案件事实所涉及的专业问题提出意见。  
具有专门知识的人在法庭上就专业问题提出的意见，视为当事人的陈述。  
人民法院准许当事人申请的，相关费用由提出申请的当事人负担。

**Article 122** A party may, in accordance with Article 82 of the Civil Procedure Law, apply before the expiration of the time limit for adducing evidence for one or two persons with specialized knowledge to appear in court to cross-examine the expert opinion on behalf of the party, or to render opinions on the specialized issues involved in the facts of the case.  
The opinions rendered by a person with specialized knowledge on specialized issues in court shall be deemed as statements of the party.  
Where the People's Court approves a party's application, the relevant expenses shall be borne by the party making the application.

<a id="art-123"></a>第一百二十三条　人民法院可以对出庭的具有专门知识的人进行询问。经法庭准许，当事人可以对出庭的具有专门知识的人进行询问，当事人各自申请的具有专门知识的人可以就案件中的有关问题进行对质。  
具有专门知识的人不得参与专业问题之外的法庭审理活动。

**Article 123** The People's Court may question a person with specialized knowledge who appears in court. With the permission of the court, a party may question the person with specialized knowledge appearing in court, and the persons with specialized knowledge respectively applied for by the parties may confront each other on the relevant issues in the case.  
A person with specialized knowledge shall not participate in court trial activities beyond specialized issues.

<a id="art-124"></a>第一百二十四条　人民法院认为有必要的，可以根据当事人的申请或者依职权对物证或者现场进行勘验。勘验时应当保护他人的隐私和尊严。  
人民法院可以要求鉴定人参与勘验。必要时，可以要求鉴定人在勘验中进行鉴定。

**Article 124** Where the People's Court deems it necessary, it may, upon a party's application or on its own authority, conduct an inspection of a physical evidence or the scene. During the inspection, the privacy and dignity of others shall be protected.  
The People's Court may request an expert to participate in the inspection. Where necessary, it may require the expert to conduct expert evaluation during the inspection.

### 五、期间和送达

<a id="art-125"></a>第一百二十五条　依照[民事诉讼法第八十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-85)第二款规定，民事诉讼中以时起算的期间从次时起算；以日、月、年计算的期间从次日起算。

**Article 125** In accordance with the provisions of paragraph 2 of Article 85 of the Civil Procedure Law, a period calculated by hours in civil actions shall commence from the next hour; a period calculated by days, months, or years shall commence from the next day.

<a id="art-126"></a>第一百二十六条　[民事诉讼法第一百二十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-126)规定的立案期限，因起诉状内容欠缺通知原告补正的，从补正后交人民法院的次日起算。由上级人民法院转交下级人民法院立案的案件，从受诉人民法院收到起诉状的次日起算。

**Article 126** The time limit for filing a case as provided in Article 126 of the Civil Procedure Law shall, if the plaintiff is notified to make corrections due to deficiencies in the content of the complaint, be calculated from the day after the corrected complaint is submitted to the People's Court. For a case transferred by a higher People's Court to a lower People's Court for filing, the time limit shall be calculated from the day after the lower People's Court receives the complaint.

<a id="art-127"></a>第一百二十七条　[民事诉讼法第五十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-59)第三款、第二百一十二条以及[本解释第三百七十二条](#art-372)、第三百八十二条、第三百九十九条、第四百二十条、第四百二十一条规定的六个月，[民事诉讼法第二百三十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-230)规定的一年，为不变期间，不适用诉讼时效中止、中断、延长的规定。

**Article 127** The six-month period specified in paragraph 3 of Article 59, Article 212 of the Civil Procedure Law, and Articles 372, 382, 399, 420, and 421 of this Interpretation, and the one-year period specified in Article 230 of the Civil Procedure Law, are fixed periods, to which the provisions on suspension, interruption, and extension of the limitation of action shall not apply.

<a id="art-128"></a>第一百二十八条　再审案件按照第一审程序或者第二审程序审理的，适用[民事诉讼法第一百五十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-152)、第一百八十三条规定的审限。审限自再审立案的次日起算。

**Article 128** Where a retrial case is tried in accordance with the procedure of first instance or second instance, the trial time limits provided in Articles 152 and 183 of the Civil Procedure Law shall apply. The trial time limit shall be calculated from the day after the retrial case is filed.

<a id="art-129"></a>第一百二十九条　对申请再审案件，人民法院应当自受理之日起三个月内审查完毕，但公告期间、当事人和解期间等不计入审查期限。有特殊情况需要延长的，由本院院长批准。

**Article 129** For a case of applying for retrial, the People's Court shall complete its review within three months from the date of acceptance, provided that the period for public announcement, the period for settlement between the parties, etc., shall not be counted in the review time limit. Where there are special circumstances requiring an extension, it shall be approved by the president of the court.

<a id="art-130"></a>第一百三十条　向法人或者其他组织送达诉讼文书，应当由法人的法定代表人、该组织的主要负责人或者办公室、收发室、值班室等负责收件的人签收或者盖章，拒绝签收或者盖章的，适用留置送达。  
[民事诉讼法第八十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-89)规定的有关基层组织和所在单位的代表，可以是受送达人住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会的工作人员以及受送达人所在单位的工作人员。
