**Article 130** When serving litigation documents on a legal person or other organization, the service shall be signed for or sealed by the legal representative of the legal person, the principal responsible person of the organization, or the person responsible for receiving mail in the office, mail room, duty room, etc. If the recipient refuses to sign for or seal the documents, service by leaving the documents at the place of service shall apply.  
The representatives of the relevant grassroots organization and the entity where the addressee works as provided in Article 89 of the Civil Procedure Law may be the staff of the residents' committee or villagers' committee in the addressee's domicile, as well as the staff of the entity where the addressee works.

<a id="art-131"></a>第一百三十一条　人民法院直接送达诉讼文书的，可以通知当事人到人民法院领取。当事人到达人民法院，拒绝签署送达回证的，视为送达。审判人员、书记员应当在送达回证上注明送达情况并签名。  
人民法院可以在当事人住所地以外向当事人直接送达诉讼文书。当事人拒绝签署送达回证的，采用拍照、录像等方式记录送达过程即视为送达。审判人员、书记员应当在送达回证上注明送达情况并签名。

**Article 131** Where the People's Court directly serves litigation documents, it may notify the party to collect them at the People's Court. If the party arrives at the People's Court but refuses to sign the service receipt, the service shall be deemed to have been completed. The adjudicator and court clerk shall indicate the service situation on the service receipt and affix their signatures.  
The People's Court may directly serve litigation documents on a party outside the party's domicile. If the party refuses to sign the service receipt, the service process recorded by means of photographing, video recording, etc., shall be deemed as service. The adjudicator and court clerk shall indicate the service situation on the service receipt and affix their signatures.

<a id="art-132"></a>第一百三十二条　受送达人有诉讼代理人的，人民法院既可以向受送达人送达，也可以向其诉讼代理人送达。受送达人指定诉讼代理人为代收人的，向诉讼代理人送达时，适用留置送达。

**Article 132** If the addressee has a litigation agent, the People's Court may serve documents either on the addressee or on the litigation agent. If the addressee designates the litigation agent as the person to receive documents on its behalf, service by leaving the documents at the place of service shall apply when serving on the litigation agent.

<a id="art-133"></a>第一百三十三条　调解书应当直接送达当事人本人，不适用留置送达。当事人本人因故不能签收的，可由其指定的代收人签收。

**Article 133** A mediation document shall be directly served on the party himself or herself, and service by leaving the documents at the place of service shall not apply. If the party himself or herself cannot sign for it for any reason, the designated recipient may sign for it.

<a id="art-134"></a>第一百三十四条　依照[民事诉讼法第九十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-91)规定，委托其他人民法院代为送达的，委托法院应当出具委托函，并附需要送达的诉讼文书和送达回证，以受送达人在送达回证上签收的日期为送达日期。  
委托送达的，受委托人民法院应当自收到委托函及相关诉讼文书之日起十日内代为送达。

**Article 134** Where service is entrusted to another People's Court in accordance with Article 91 of the Civil Procedure Law, the entrusting court shall issue a letter of entrustment and attach the litigation documents to be served and the service receipt. The date on which the addressee signs the service receipt shall be the date of service.  
In the case of entrusted service, the entrusted People's Court shall complete the service on behalf of the entrusting court within ten days from the date of receiving the letter of entrustment and the relevant litigation documents.

<a id="art-135"></a>第一百三十五条　电子送达可以采用传真、电子邮件、移动通信等即时收悉的特定系统作为送达媒介。  
[民事诉讼法第九十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-90)第二款规定的到达受送达人特定系统的日期，为人民法院对应系统显示发送成功的日期，但受送达人证明到达其特定系统的日期与人民法院对应系统显示发送成功的日期不一致的，以受送达人证明到达其特定系统的日期为准。

**Article 135** Electronic service may use specific systems capable of instant receipt, such as fax, email, and mobile communication, as the service medium.  
The date of arrival at the addressee's specific system as provided in paragraph 2 of Article 90 of the Civil Procedure Law shall be the date on which the People's Court's corresponding system shows successful transmission; provided that if the addressee proves that the date of arrival at its specific system is inconsistent with the date shown by the People's Court's corresponding system, the date proved by the addressee as the date of arrival shall prevail.

<a id="art-136"></a>第一百三十六条　受送达人同意采用电子方式送达的，应当在送达地址确认书中予以确认。

**Article 136** If the addressee agrees to electronic service, it shall confirm such agreement in the service address confirmation statement.

<a id="art-137"></a>第一百三十七条　当事人在提起上诉、申请再审、申请执行时未书面变更送达地址的，其在第一审程序中确认的送达地址可以作为第二审程序、审判监督程序、执行程序的送达地址。

**Article 137** If a party does not change its service address in writing when filing an appeal, applying for retrial, or applying for enforcement, the service address confirmed in the first instance procedure may serve as the service address for the second instance procedure, adjudication supervision procedure, and enforcement procedure.

<a id="art-138"></a>第一百三十八条　公告送达可以在法院的公告栏和受送达人住所地张贴公告，也可以在报纸、信息网络等媒体上刊登公告，发出公告日期以最后张贴或者刊登的日期为准。对公告送达方式有特殊要求的，应当按要求的方式进行。公告期满，即视为送达。  
人民法院在受送达人住所地张贴公告的，应当采取拍照、录像等方式记录张贴过程。

**Article 138** Service by public announcement may be made by posting an announcement on the court's bulletin board and at the addressee's domicile, or by publishing the announcement in newspapers, information networks, or other media. The date of making the announcement shall be the date of the last posting or publication. If there are special requirements for the method of service by public announcement, it shall be carried out in accordance with the requirements. Upon expiration of the period for the public announcement, service shall be deemed to have been completed.  
Where the People's Court posts an announcement at the addressee's domicile, it shall record the posting process by taking photographs, videos, or other means.

<a id="art-139"></a>第一百三十九条　公告送达应当说明公告送达的原因；公告送达起诉状或者上诉状副本的，应当说明起诉或者上诉要点，受送达人答辩期限及逾期不答辩的法律后果；公告送达传票，应当说明出庭的时间和地点及逾期不出庭的法律后果；公告送达判决书、裁定书的，应当说明裁判主要内容，当事人有权上诉的，还应当说明上诉权利、上诉期限和上诉的人民法院。

**Article 139** Service by public announcement shall state the reason for such service; when serving a copy of the complaint or the appeal petition by public announcement, it shall state the main points of the action or appeal, the time limit for the addressee to file a defense and the legal consequences of failing to do so within the time limit; when serving a summons by public announcement, it shall state the time and place for appearance in court and the legal consequences of failing to appear in court within the time limit; when serving a judgment or ruling by public announcement, it shall state the main content of the adjudication, and if the party has the right to appeal, it shall also state the right to appeal, the time limit for appeal, and the People's Court to which the appeal may be filed.

<a id="art-140"></a>第一百四十条　适用简易程序的案件，不适用公告送达。

**Article 140** Service by public announcement shall not apply to cases subject to summary procedure.

<a id="art-141"></a>第一百四十一条　人民法院在定期宣判时，当事人拒不签收判决书、裁定书的，应视为送达，并在宣判笔录中记明。

**Article 141** If, at a scheduled pronouncement of judgment, a party refuses to sign for the judgment or ruling, service shall be deemed to have been completed, and the fact shall be recorded in the pronouncement record.

### 六、调解

<a id="art-142"></a>第一百四十二条　人民法院受理案件后，经审查，认为法律关系明确、事实清楚，在征得当事人双方同意后，可以径行调解。

**Article 142** After accepting a case, if upon examination the People's Court considers that the legal relationship is clear and the facts are clear, it may, after obtaining the consent of both parties, directly conduct mediation.

<a id="art-143"></a>第一百四十三条　适用特别程序、督促程序、公示催告程序的案件，婚姻等身份关系确认案件以及其他根据案件性质不能进行调解的案件，不得调解。

**Article 143** Cases subject to special procedures, supervision procedures, and public summons procedures, cases for confirmation of marital and other personal relationships, and other cases that cannot be mediated based on the nature of the case, shall not be mediated.

<a id="art-144"></a>第一百四十四条　人民法院审理民事案件，发现当事人之间恶意串通，企图通过和解、调解方式侵害他人合法权益的，应当依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-115)的规定处理。

**Article 144** When hearing a civil case, if the People's Court discovers that the parties have maliciously colluded, attempting to infringe upon the lawful rights and interests of others through settlement or mediation, it shall handle the matter in accordance with Article 115 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-145"></a>第一百四十五条　人民法院审理民事案件，应当根据自愿、合法的原则进行调解。当事人一方或者双方坚持不愿调解的，应当及时裁判。  
人民法院审理离婚案件，应当进行调解，但不应久调不决。

**Article 145** When hearing a civil case, the People's Court shall conduct mediation on the principles of voluntariness and lawfulness. If one or both parties insist on not mediating, the court shall render a judgment in a timely manner.  
When hearing a divorce case, the People's Court shall conduct mediation, but shall not prolong mediation without resolving the case.

<a id="art-146"></a>第一百四十六条　人民法院审理民事案件，调解过程不公开，但当事人同意公开的除外。  
调解协议内容不公开，但为保护国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益，人民法院认为确有必要公开的除外。  
主持调解以及参与调解的人员，对调解过程以及调解过程中获悉的国家秘密、商业秘密、个人隐私和其他不宜公开的信息，应当保守秘密，但为保护国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益的除外。

**Article 146** When hearing a civil case, the People's Court shall not make the mediation process public, unless the parties agree to make it public.  
The content of the mediation agreement shall not be made public, unless the People's Court deems it truly necessary to do so for the protection of national interests, public interests, or the lawful rights and interests of others.  
The persons presiding over or participating in mediation shall keep confidential the mediation process and any state secrets, trade secrets, personal privacy, or other information not suitable for disclosure learned during the process, unless such disclosure is necessary for the protection of national interests, public interests, or the lawful rights and interests of others.

<a id="art-147"></a>第一百四十七条　人民法院调解案件时，当事人不能出庭的，经其特别授权，可由其委托代理人参加调解，达成的调解协议，可由委托代理人签名。  
离婚案件当事人确因特殊情况无法出庭参加调解的，除本人不能表达意志的以外，应当出具书面意见。

**Article 147** When the People's Court mediates a case, if a party cannot appear in court, its authorized agent may participate in the mediation upon special authorization by the party, and the mediation agreement reached may be signed by the authorized agent.  
If a party to a divorce case truly cannot appear in court for mediation due to special circumstances, the party shall provide a written opinion, unless the party is unable to express its will.

<a id="art-148"></a>第一百四十八条　当事人自行和解或者调解达成协议后，请求人民法院按照和解协议或者调解协议的内容制作判决书的，人民法院不予准许。  
无民事行为能力人的离婚案件，由其法定代理人进行诉讼。法定代理人与对方达成协议要求发给判决书的，可根据协议内容制作判决书。

**Article 148** After the parties have reached a settlement agreement between themselves or a mediation agreement, if they request the People's Court to prepare a judgment in accordance with the content of the settlement agreement or mediation agreement, the People's Court shall not approve such request.  
In a divorce case of a person without capacity for civil conduct, the legal representative shall conduct the litigation. If the legal representative reaches an agreement with the other party and requests a judgment to be issued, a judgment may be prepared in accordance with the content of the agreement.

<a id="art-149"></a>第一百四十九条　调解书需经当事人签收后才发生法律效力的，应当以最后收到调解书的当事人签收的日期为调解书生效日期。

**Article 149** If a mediation document takes legal effect only after being signed by the parties, the date on which the last party receiving the mediation document signs for it shall be the effective date of the mediation document.

<a id="art-150"></a>第一百五十条　人民法院调解民事案件，需由无独立请求权的第三人承担责任的，应当经其同意。该第三人在调解书送达前反悔的，人民法院应当及时裁判。

**Article 150** If, when the People's Court mediates a civil case, a third party with no independent claim is required to bear liability, the consent of such third party shall be obtained. If such third party repudiates the agreement before the mediation document is served, the People's Court shall render a judgment in a timely manner.

<a id="art-151"></a>第一百五十一条　根据[民事诉讼法第一百零一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-101)第一款第四项规定，当事人各方同意在调解协议上签名或者盖章后即发生法律效力的，经人民法院审查确认后，应当记入笔录或者将调解协议附卷，并由当事人、审判人员、书记员签名或者盖章后即具有法律效力。  
前款规定情形，当事人请求制作调解书的，人民法院审查确认后可以制作调解书送交当事人。当事人拒收调解书的，不影响调解协议的效力。

**Article 151** In accordance with Item 4 of Paragraph 1 of Article 101 of the Civil Procedure Law, if all parties agree that the mediation agreement shall take legal effect upon signature or sealing, after examination and confirmation by the People's Court, the agreement shall be recorded in the record or attached to the case file, and it shall have legal effect upon signature or sealing by the parties, the adjudicator, and the court clerk.  
In the circumstances provided in the preceding paragraph, if the parties request the preparation of a mediation document, the People's Court may, after examination and confirmation, prepare a mediation document and deliver it to the parties. If a party refuses to accept the mediation document, it shall not affect the validity of the mediation agreement.

### 七、保全和先予执行

<a id="art-152"></a>第一百五十二条　人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第一百零三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-103)、第一百零四条规定，在采取诉前保全、诉讼保全措施时，责令利害关系人或者当事人提供担保的，应当书面通知。  
利害关系人申请诉前保全的，应当提供担保。申请诉前财产保全的，应当提供相当于请求保全数额的担保；情况特殊的，人民法院可以酌情处理。申请诉前行为保全的，担保的数额由人民法院根据案件的具体情况决定。  
在诉讼中，人民法院依申请或者依职权采取保全措施的，应当根据案件的具体情况，决定当事人是否应当提供担保以及担保的数额。

**Article 152** When the People's Court, in accordance with Articles 103 and 104 of the Civil Procedure Law, takes pre-litigation preservation or litigation preservation measures and orders an interested party or a party to provide security, it shall do so by a written notice.  
An interested party applying for pre-litigation preservation shall provide security. If applying for pre-litigation property preservation, the interested party shall provide security equivalent to the amount requested to be preserved; under special circumstances, the People's Court may handle the matter at its discretion. If applying for pre-litigation preservation of conduct, the amount of security shall be determined by the People's Court based on the specific circumstances of the case.  
In litigation, when the People's Court takes preservation measures upon application or on its own authority, it shall, based on the specific circumstances of the case, decide whether a party shall provide security and the amount of such security.

<a id="art-153"></a>第一百五十三条　人民法院对季节性商品、鲜活、易腐烂变质以及其他不宜长期保存的物品采取保全措施时，可以责令当事人及时处理，由人民法院保存价款；必要时，人民法院可予以变卖，保存价款。

**Article 153** When the People's Court takes preservation measures on seasonal goods, fresh and live goods, perishable goods, or other items not suitable for long-term storage, it may order the party to dispose of them in a timely manner, with the proceeds preserved by the People's Court; if necessary, the People's Court may sell the items and preserve the proceeds.

<a id="art-154"></a>第一百五十四条　人民法院在财产保全中采取查封、扣押、冻结财产措施时，应当妥善保管被查封、扣押、冻结的财产。不宜由人民法院保管的，人民法院可以指定被保全人负责保管；不宜由被保全人保管的，可以委托他人或者申请保全人保管。  
查封、扣押、冻结担保物权人占有的担保财产，一般由担保物权人保管；由人民法院保管的，质权、留置权不因采取保全措施而消灭。

**Article 154** When the People's Court takes measures to seal up, seize, or freeze property in property preservation, it shall properly keep the property sealed up, seized, or frozen. If it is not appropriate for the People's Court to keep the property, the court may designate the person subject to preservation to be responsible for its keeping; if it is not appropriate for the person subject to preservation to keep it, the court may entrust another person or the applicant for preservation to keep it.  
Where the secured property in the possession of a secured creditor is sealed up, seized, or frozen, the secured creditor shall generally keep it; if it is kept by the People's Court, the right of pledge or lien shall not be extinguished by the preservation measure.

<a id="art-155"></a>第一百五十五条　由人民法院指定被保全人保管的财产，如果继续使用对该财产的价值无重大影响，可以允许被保全人继续使用；由人民法院保管或者委托他人、申请保全人保管的财产，人民法院和其他保管人不得使用。

**Article 155** If the property is kept by the person subject to preservation as designated by the People's Court, and continued use of the property does not significantly affect its value, the person subject to preservation may be allowed to continue using it; if the property is kept by the People's Court or by another person or the applicant for preservation as entrusted, the People's Court and the other keeper shall not use the property.

<a id="art-156"></a>第一百五十六条　人民法院采取财产保全的方法和措施，依照执行程序相关规定办理。

**Article 156** The methods and measures for property preservation taken by the People's Court shall be handled in accordance with the relevant provisions of enforcement procedures.

<a id="art-157"></a>第一百五十七条　人民法院对抵押物、质押物、留置物可以采取财产保全措施，但不影响抵押权人、质权人、留置权人的优先受偿权。

**Article 157** The People's Court may take property preservation measures on mortgaged property, pledged property, or property subject to lien, but such measures shall not affect the right of the mortgagee, pledgee, or lien holder to receive priority satisfaction.

<a id="art-158"></a>第一百五十八条　人民法院对债务人到期应得的收益，可以采取财产保全措施，限制其支取，通知有关单位协助执行。

**Article 158** The People's Court may take property preservation measures on the debtor's due receivables, restrict the debtor from withdrawing them, and notify the relevant entity to assist in enforcement.

<a id="art-159"></a>第一百五十九条　债务人的财产不能满足保全请求，但对他人有到期债权的，人民法院可以依债权人的申请裁定该他人不得对本案债务人清偿。该他人要求偿付的，由人民法院提存财物或者价款。

**Article 159** Where the debtor's property is insufficient to satisfy the preservation claim, but the debtor has a due claim against another person, the People's Court may, upon the application of the creditor, issue a ruling that such other person shall not make payment to the debtor in the case. If such other person requests to pay, the People's Court shall have the property or proceeds deposited.

<a id="art-160"></a>第一百六十条　当事人向采取诉前保全措施以外的其他有管辖权的人民法院起诉的，采取诉前保全措施的人民法院应当将保全手续移送受理案件的人民法院。诉前保全的裁定视为受移送人民法院作出的裁定。

**Article 160** Where a party initiates an action in another People's Court with jurisdiction other than the one that took the pre-litigation preservation measures, the People's Court that took the pre-litigation preservation measures shall transfer the preservation procedures to the People's Court accepting the case. The ruling on pre-litigation preservation shall be deemed as a ruling made by the transferee People's Court.

<a id="art-161"></a>第一百六十一条　对当事人不服一审判决提起上诉的案件，在第二审人民法院接到报送的案件之前，当事人有转移、隐匿、出卖或者毁损财产等行为，必须采取保全措施的，由第一审人民法院依当事人申请或者依职权采取。第一审人民法院的保全裁定，应当及时报送第二审人民法院。

**Article 161** In a case where a party files an appeal against a first-instance judgment, before the second-instance People's Court receives the case submitted, if a party engages in conduct such as transferring, concealing, selling, or destroying property, and it is necessary to take preservation measures, the first-instance People's Court shall take such measures upon a party's application or on its own authority. The preservation ruling of the first-instance People's Court shall be promptly submitted to the second-instance People's Court.

<a id="art-162"></a>第一百六十二条　第二审人民法院裁定对第一审人民法院采取的保全措施予以续保或者采取新的保全措施的，可以自行实施，也可以委托第一审人民法院实施。  
再审人民法院裁定对原保全措施予以续保或者采取新的保全措施的，可以自行实施，也可以委托原审人民法院或者执行法院实施。

**Article 162** Where the second-instance People's Court rules to renew the preservation measures taken by the first-instance People's Court or to take new preservation measures, it may implement them itself or entrust the first-instance People's Court to implement them.  
Where the retrial People's Court rules to renew the original preservation measures or to take new preservation measures, it may implement them itself or entrust the original trial court or the enforcement court to implement them.

<a id="art-163"></a>第一百六十三条　法律文书生效后，进入执行程序前，债权人因对方当事人转移财产等紧急情况，不申请保全将可能导致生效法律文书不能执行或者难以执行的，可以向执行法院申请采取保全措施。债权人在法律文书指定的履行期间届满后五日内不申请执行的，人民法院应当解除保全。

**Article 163** After a legal document takes effect but before the enforcement procedure commences, if the creditor, due to emergency circumstances such as the other party's transfer of property, fails to apply for preservation and such failure may render the effective legal document impossible or difficult to enforce, the creditor may apply to the enforcement court for taking preservation measures. If the creditor does not apply for enforcement within five days after the expiration of the performance period specified in the legal document, the People's Court shall discharge the preservation.

<a id="art-164"></a>第一百六十四条　对申请保全人或者他人提供的担保财产，人民法院应当依法办理查封、扣押、冻结等手续。

**Article 164** With respect to the security property provided by the applicant for preservation or another person, the People's Court shall, according to law, handle formalities such as sealing up, seizing, or freezing.

<a id="art-165"></a>第一百六十五条　人民法院裁定采取保全措施后，除作出保全裁定的人民法院自行解除或者其上级人民法院决定解除外，在保全期限内，任何单位不得解除保全措施。

**Article 165** After the People's Court rules to take preservation measures, no entity shall discharge the preservation measures during the preservation period, except where the People's Court that made the preservation ruling discharges them on its own or its higher People's Court decides to discharge them.

<a id="art-166"></a>第一百六十六条　裁定采取保全措施后，有下列情形之一的，人民法院应当作出解除保全裁定：  
（一）保全错误的；  
（二）申请人撤回保全申请的；  
（三）申请人的起诉或者诉讼请求被生效裁判驳回的；  
（四）人民法院认为应当解除保全的其他情形。  
解除以登记方式实施的保全措施的，应当向登记机关发出协助执行通知书。

**Article 166** After a ruling to take preservation measures is made, under any of the following circumstances, the People's Court shall make a ruling to discharge the preservation:  
(1) The preservation is erroneous;  
(2) The applicant withdraws the preservation application;  
(3) The applicant's prosecution or claim is dismissed by a final judgment;  
(4) Other circumstances under which the People's Court considers that the preservation shall be discharged.  
Where preservation measures implemented by registration are discharged, a notice of assistance in enforcement shall be issued to the registration authority.

<a id="art-167"></a>第一百六十七条　财产保全的被保全人提供其他等值担保财产且有利于执行的，人民法院可以裁定变更保全标的物为被保全人提供的担保财产。

**Article 167** If the person subject to property preservation provides other equivalent secured property that is conducive to enforcement, the People's Court may rule to change the subject matter of preservation to the secured property provided by the person subject to preservation.

<a id="art-168"></a>第一百六十八条　保全裁定未经人民法院依法撤销或者解除，进入执行程序后，自动转为执行中的查封、扣押、冻结措施，期限连续计算，执行法院无需重新制作裁定书，但查封、扣押、冻结期限届满的除外。

**Article 168** If a preservation ruling has not been revoked or discharged according to law by the People's Court, upon entering the enforcement procedure, it shall automatically be transformed into measures of sealing up, seizure, or freezing during enforcement, with the time limit calculated consecutively, and the enforcement court need not make a new ruling, except where the time limit for sealing up, seizure, or freezing has expired.

<a id="art-169"></a>第一百六十九条　民事诉讼法规定的先予执行，人民法院应当在受理案件后终审判决作出前采取。先予执行应当限于当事人诉讼请求的范围，并以当事人的生活、生产经营的急需为限。

**Article 169** The advance execution provided for in the Civil Procedure Law shall be taken by the People's Court after accepting the case and before the final judgment is rendered. Advance execution shall be limited to the scope of the party's claim and shall be confined to the urgent needs of the party's livelihood and production or business operation.

<a id="art-170"></a>第一百七十条　[民事诉讼法第一百零九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-109)第三项规定的情况紧急，包括：  
（一）需要立即停止侵害、排除妨碍的；  
（二）需要立即制止某项行为的；  
（三）追索恢复生产、经营急需的保险理赔费的；  
（四）需要立即返还社会保险金、社会救助资金的；  
（五）不立即返还款项，将严重影响权利人生活和生产经营的。

**Article 170** The urgent circumstances specified in Item 3 of Article 109 of the Civil Procedure Law include:  
(1) The need to immediately stop the infringement or remove the obstruction;  
(2) The need to immediately prevent a certain act;  
(3) The recovery of insurance claim money urgently needed for resuming production or business operation;  
(4) The need to immediately return social insurance funds or social assistance funds;  
(5) The failure to immediately return the money would seriously affect the right holder's life and production or business operation.

<a id="art-171"></a>第一百七十一条　当事人对保全或者先予执行裁定不服的，可以自收到裁定书之日起五日内向作出裁定的人民法院申请复议。人民法院应当在收到复议申请后十日内审查。裁定正确的，驳回当事人的申请；裁定不当的，变更或者撤销原裁定。

**Article 171** If a party is dissatisfied with a ruling on preservation or advance execution, it may apply for reconsideration to the People's Court that made the ruling within five days from the date of receiving the ruling. The People's Court shall review the application within ten days after receiving it. If the ruling is correct, the party's application shall be dismissed; if the ruling is improper, the original ruling shall be varied or revoked.

<a id="art-172"></a>第一百七十二条　利害关系人对保全或者先予执行的裁定不服申请复议的，由作出裁定的人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-111)规定处理。

**Article 172** If an interested party applies for reconsideration due to dissatisfaction with a ruling on preservation or advance execution, the People's Court that made the ruling shall handle the matter in accordance with Article 111 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-173"></a>第一百七十三条　人民法院先予执行后，根据发生法律效力的判决，申请人应当返还因先予执行所取得的利益的，适用[民事诉讼法第二百四十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-240)的规定。

**Article 173** Where, after the People's Court has carried out advance execution, according to a judgment that has taken legal effect, the applicant shall return the benefits obtained from the advance execution, the provisions of Article 240 of the Civil Procedure Law shall apply.

### 八、对妨害民事诉讼的强制措施

<a id="art-174"></a>第一百七十四条　[民事诉讼法第一百一十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-112)规定的必须到庭的被告，是指负有赡养、抚育、扶养义务和不到庭就无法查清案情的被告。  
人民法院对必须到庭才能查清案件基本事实的原告，经两次传票传唤，无正当理由拒不到庭的，可以拘传。

**Article 174** The defendant who must appear in court as prescribed in Article 112 of the Civil Procedure Law refers to a defendant who has the obligation to provide support, maintenance, or alimony and whose absence would make it impossible to ascertain the facts of the case.  
Where a plaintiff who must appear in court to ascertain the basic facts of the case, after being summoned twice by subpoena, refuses to appear in court without justifiable reasons, the People's Court may compel his or her appearance.

<a id="art-175"></a>第一百七十五条　拘传必须用拘传票，并直接送达被拘传人；在拘传前，应当向被拘传人说明拒不到庭的后果，经批评教育仍拒不到庭的，可以拘传其到庭。

**Article 175** A compulsory appearance must be made by a compulsory appearance warrant, which shall be served directly on the person subject to compulsory appearance; before the compulsory appearance, the person shall be informed of the consequences of refusing to appear in court, and if, after criticism and education, the person still refuses to appear in court, the person may be compelled to appear.

<a id="art-176"></a>第一百七十六条　诉讼参与人或者其他人有下列行为之一的，人民法院可以适用[民事诉讼法第一百一十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-113)规定处理：  
（一）未经准许进行录音、录像、摄影的；  
（二）未经准许以移动通信等方式现场传播审判活动的；  
（三）其他扰乱法庭秩序，妨害审判活动进行的。  
有前款规定情形的，人民法院可以暂扣诉讼参与人或者其他人进行录音、录像、摄影、传播审判活动的器材，并责令其删除有关内容；拒不删除的，人民法院可以采取必要手段强制删除。

**Article 176** Where any participant in the proceedings or any other person commits any of the following acts, the People's Court may handle the matter in accordance with the provisions of Article 113 of the Civil Procedure Law:  
(1) making audio recordings, video recordings, or photographs without permission;  
(2) broadcasting trial activities on-site by means of mobile communication or otherwise without permission;  
(3) other acts that disrupt court order and obstruct the conduct of trial activities.  
Where the circumstances set forth in the preceding paragraph exist, the People's Court may temporarily seize the equipment used by the participant in the proceedings or other person for making audio recordings, video recordings, photographs, or broadcasting trial activities, and order the deletion of the relevant content; if the person refuses to delete the content, the People's Court may take necessary measures to compel such deletion.

<a id="art-177"></a>第一百七十七条　训诫、责令退出法庭由合议庭或者独任审判员决定。训诫的内容、被责令退出法庭者的违法事实应当记入庭审笔录。

**Article 177** Admonition and an order to withdraw from the courtroom shall be decided by the collegial panel or the sole adjudicator. The content of the admonition and the illegal facts of the person ordered to withdraw from the courtroom shall be recorded in the trial transcript.

<a id="art-178"></a>第一百七十八条　人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-113)至第一百一十七条的规定采取拘留措施的，应经院长批准，作出拘留决定书，由司法警察将被拘留人送交当地公安机关看管。

**Article 178** Where the People's Court takes detention measures in accordance with the provisions of Articles 113 to 117 of the Civil Procedure Law, it shall obtain the approval of the president, issue a detention decision, and have the judicial police deliver the detainee to the local public security organ for custody.

<a id="art-179"></a>第一百七十九条　被拘留人不在本辖区的，作出拘留决定的人民法院应当派员到被拘留人所在地的人民法院，请该院协助执行，受委托的人民法院应当及时派员协助执行。被拘留人申请复议或者在拘留期间承认并改正错误，需要提前解除拘留的，受委托人民法院应当向委托人民法院转达或者提出建议，由委托人民法院审查决定。

**Article 179** Where the detainee is not within the jurisdiction of the court, the People's Court that made the detention decision shall send personnel to the People's Court at the detainee's location and request that court to assist in enforcement, and the entrusted People's Court shall promptly send personnel to assist in enforcement. Where the detainee applies for reconsideration or admits and corrects errors during the detention period, and the detention needs to be lifted early, the entrusted People's Court shall communicate the situation to or make a recommendation to the entrusting People's Court, and the entrusting People's Court shall review and make a decision.

<a id="art-180"></a>第一百八十条　人民法院对被拘留人采取拘留措施后，应当在二十四小时内通知其家属；确实无法按时通知或者通知不到的，应当记录在案。

**Article 180** After the People's Court takes detention measures against a detainee, it shall notify his or her family members within twenty-four hours; if it is truly impossible to notify within the time limit or the notice cannot be delivered, this shall be recorded in the case file.

<a id="art-181"></a>第一百八十一条　因哄闹、冲击法庭，用暴力、威胁等方法抗拒执行公务等紧急情况，必须立即采取拘留措施的，可在拘留后，立即报告院长补办批准手续。院长认为拘留不当的，应当解除拘留。

**Article 181** Where, due to emergencies such as making a din, storming the courtroom, or resisting the execution of official duties by violence, threats, or other means, detention measures must be taken immediately, such measures may be taken, and the matter shall be reported to the president immediately after the detention to complete the formalities for approval. If the president considers the detention improper, the detention shall be lifted.

<a id="art-182"></a>第一百八十二条　被拘留人在拘留期间认错悔改的，可以责令其具结悔过，提前解除拘留。提前解除拘留，应报经院长批准，并作出提前解除拘留决定书，交负责看管的公安机关执行。

**Article 182** Where a detainee admits the fault and repents during the detention period, he or she may be ordered to make a written statement of repentance and the detention may be lifted early. Early lifting of detention shall be reported to the president for approval, and a decision on early lifting of detention shall be issued, and handed over to the public security organ responsible for custody for enforcement.

<a id="art-183"></a>第一百八十三条　[民事诉讼法第一百一十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-113)至第一百一十六条规定的罚款、拘留可以单独适用，也可以合并适用。

**Article 183** The fines and detention prescribed in Articles 113 to 116 of the Civil Procedure Law may be applied separately or in combination.

<a id="art-184"></a>第一百八十四条　对同一妨害民事诉讼行为的罚款、拘留不得连续适用。发生新的妨害民事诉讼行为的，人民法院可以重新予以罚款、拘留。

**Article 184** Fines and detention for the same act obstructing civil proceedings shall not be applied consecutively. If a new act obstructing civil proceedings occurs, the People's Court may impose a new fine or detention.

<a id="art-185"></a>第一百八十五条　被罚款、拘留的人不服罚款、拘留决定申请复议的，应当自收到决定书之日起三日内提出。上级人民法院应当在收到复议申请后五日内作出决定，并将复议结果通知下级人民法院和当事人。

**Article 185** Where a person who is fined or detained refuses to accept the decision on fine or detention and applies for reconsideration, he or she shall file the application within three days from the date of receipt of the decision. The higher People's Court shall make a decision within five days after receiving the application for reconsideration and notify the lower People's Court and the parties of the result of the reconsideration.

<a id="art-186"></a>第一百八十六条　上级人民法院复议时认为强制措施不当的，应当制作决定书，撤销或者变更下级人民法院作出的拘留、罚款决定。情况紧急的，可以在口头通知后三日内发出决定书。

**Article 186** Where, upon reconsideration, the higher People's Court considers the compulsory measures improper, it shall issue a decision to revoke or modify the decision on detention or fine made by the lower People's Court. In an emergency, the decision may be issued within three days after oral notification.

<a id="art-187"></a>第一百八十七条　[民事诉讼法第一百一十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-114)第一款第五项规定的以暴力、威胁或者其他方法阻碍司法工作人员执行职务的行为，包括：  
（一）在人民法院哄闹、滞留，不听从司法工作人员劝阻的；  
（二）故意毁损、抢夺人民法院法律文书、查封标志的；  
（三）哄闹、冲击执行公务现场，围困、扣押执行或者协助执行公务人员的；  
（四）毁损、抢夺、扣留案件材料、执行公务车辆、其他执行公务器械、执行公务人员服装和执行公务证件的；  
（五）以暴力、威胁或者其他方法阻碍司法工作人员查询、查封、扣押、冻结、划拨、拍卖、变卖财产的；  
（六）以暴力、威胁或者其他方法阻碍司法工作人员执行职务的其他行为。

**Article 187** The act of obstructing judicial officers in performing their duties by violence, threats, or other means as prescribed in Article 114, paragraph 1, item 5 of the Civil Procedure Law includes:  
(1) making a din or staying in the People's Court without following the dissuasion of judicial officers;  
(2) intentionally damaging or seizing legal documents or seizure marks of the People's Court;  
(3) making a din or storming the scene of official duties, surrounding or detaining officers performing or assisting in official duties;  
(4) damaging, seizing, or detaining case materials, official vehicles, other official equipment, uniforms of officers performing official duties, and official credentials;  
(5) obstructing judicial officers from inquiring, sealing, seizing, freezing, transferring, auctioning, or selling property by violence, threats, or other means;  
(6) other acts of obstructing judicial officers in performing their duties by violence, threats, or other means.

<a id="art-188"></a>第一百八十八条　[民事诉讼法第一百一十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-114)第一款第六项规定的拒不履行人民法院已经发生法律效力的判决、裁定的行为，包括：  
（一）在法律文书发生法律效力后隐藏、转移、变卖、毁损财产或者无偿转让财产、以明显不合理的价格交易财产、放弃到期债权、无偿为他人提供担保等，致使人民法院无法执行的；  
（二）隐藏、转移、毁损或者未经人民法院允许处分已向人民法院提供担保的财产的；  
（三）违反人民法院限制高消费令进行消费的；  
（四）有履行能力而拒不按照人民法院执行通知履行生效法律文书确定的义务的；  
（五）有义务协助执行的个人接到人民法院协助执行通知书后，拒不协助执行的。

**Article 188** The act of refusing to perform a judgment or ruling of the People's Court that has become legally effective as prescribed in Article 114, paragraph 1, item 6 of the Civil Procedure Law includes:  
(1) concealing, transferring, selling off, or damaging property, or gratuitously transferring property, trading property at a clearly unreasonable price, waiving due debts, or gratuitously providing security for others after the legal instrument has become legally effective, thereby rendering the People's Court unable to enforce the instrument;  
(2) concealing, transferring, damaging, or disposing of property that has been provided as security to the People's Court without the court's permission;  
(3) engaging in consumption in violation of the People's Court's order restricting high consumption;  
(4) having the ability to perform but refusing to perform the obligations determined by an effective legal instrument in accordance with the enforcement notice of the People's Court;  
(5) an individual who has the obligation to assist in enforcement refuses to assist in enforcement after receiving the notice of assistance in enforcement from the People's Court.

<a id="art-189"></a>第一百八十九条　诉讼参与人或者其他人有下列行为之一的，人民法院可以适用[民事诉讼法第一百一十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-114)的规定处理：  
（一）冒充他人提起诉讼或者参加诉讼的；  
（二）证人签署保证书后作虚假证言，妨碍人民法院审理案件的；  
（三）伪造、隐藏、毁灭或者拒绝交出有关被执行人履行能力的重要证据，妨碍人民法院查明被执行人财产状况的；  
（四）擅自解冻已被人民法院冻结的财产的；  
（五）接到人民法院协助执行通知书后，给当事人通风报信，协助其转移、隐匿财产的。

**Article 189** Where any participant in the proceedings or any other person commits any of the following acts, the People's Court may handle the matter in accordance with the provisions of Article 114 of the Civil Procedure Law:  
(1) impersonating another person to initiate or participate in litigation;  
(2) a witness, after signing an undertaking, gives false testimony, hindering the People's Court in trying the case;  
(3) forging, concealing, destroying, or refusing to hand over important evidence concerning the judgment debtor's ability to perform, hindering the People's Court in ascertaining the property status of the judgment debtor;  
(4) unauthorizedly unfreezing property that has been frozen by the People's Court;  
(5) after receiving the notice of assistance in enforcement from the People's Court, tipping off the parties and assisting them in transferring or concealing property.

<a id="art-190"></a>第一百九十条　[民事诉讼法第一百一十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-115)规定的他人合法权益，包括案外人的合法权益、国家利益、社会公共利益。  
第三人根据[民事诉讼法第五十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-59)第三款规定提起撤销之诉，经审查，原案当事人之间恶意串通进行虚假诉讼的，适用[民事诉讼法第一百一十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-115)规定处理。

**Article 190** The lawful rights and interests of others as prescribed in Article 115 of the Civil Procedure Law include the lawful rights and interests of persons not involved in the case, national interests, and social public interests.  
Where a third party files a revocation action in accordance with Article 59, paragraph 3 of the Civil Procedure Law, and upon review, it is found that the parties in the original case maliciously colluded to engage in false litigation, the provisions of Article 115 of the Civil Procedure Law shall apply.

<a id="art-191"></a>第一百九十一条　单位有[民事诉讼法第一百一十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-115)或者第一百一十六条规定行为的，人民法院应当对该单位进行罚款，并可以对其主要负责人或者直接责任人员予以罚款、拘留；构成犯罪的，依法追究刑事责任。

**Article 191** Where an entity commits an act prescribed in Article 115 or Article 116 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court shall impose a fine on the entity, and may also impose a fine or detention on its principal responsible person or the person directly responsible; if the act constitutes a crime, criminal liability shall be investigated according to law.

<a id="art-192"></a>第一百九十二条　有关单位接到人民法院协助执行通知书后，有下列行为之一的，人民法院可以适用[民事诉讼法第一百一十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-117)规定处理：  
（一）允许被执行人高消费的；  
（二）允许被执行人出境的；  
（三）拒不停止办理有关财产权证照转移手续、权属变更登记、规划审批等手续的；  
（四）以需要内部请示、内部审批，有内部规定等为由拖延办理的。

**Article 192** Where, after receiving the notice of assistance in enforcement from the People's Court, a relevant entity commits any of the following acts, the People's Court may handle the matter in accordance with the provisions of Article 117 of the Civil Procedure Law:  
(1) allowing the judgment debtor to engage in high consumption;  
(2) allowing the judgment debtor to leave the country;  
(3) refusing to stop handling procedures such as the transfer of property right certificates, registration of ownership changes, and planning approvals;  
(4) delaying the handling on grounds such as the need for internal consultation, internal approval, or internal regulations.

<a id="art-193"></a>第一百九十三条　人民法院对个人或者单位采取罚款措施时，应当根据其实施妨害民事诉讼行为的性质、情节、后果，当地的经济发展水平，以及诉讼标的额等因素，在[民事诉讼法第一百一十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-118)第一款规定的限额内确定相应的罚款金额。

**Article 193** When the People's Court imposes a fine on an individual or an entity, it shall determine the amount of the fine within the limit prescribed in Article 118, paragraph 1 of the Civil Procedure Law, taking into account factors such as the nature, circumstances, and consequences of the act obstructing civil proceedings, the local economic development level, and the amount in controversy.

### 九、诉讼费用

<a id="art-194"></a>第一百九十四条　依照[民事诉讼法第五十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-57)审理的案件不预交案件受理费，结案后按照诉讼标的额由败诉方交纳。

**Article 194** For cases tried in accordance with Article 57 of the Civil Procedure Law, the case acceptance fee shall not be prepaid; after the case is concluded, the losing party shall pay the fee based on the amount in controversy.

<a id="art-195"></a>第一百九十五条　支付令失效后转入诉讼程序的，债权人应当按照《诉讼费用交纳办法》补交案件受理费。  
支付令被撤销后，债权人另行起诉的，按照《诉讼费用交纳办法》交纳诉讼费用。

**Article 195** Where, after a payment order becomes invalid, the case is transferred to litigation proceedings, the creditor shall make up the case acceptance fee in accordance with the Measures on Payment of Litigation Costs.  
Where, after a payment order is revoked, the creditor files a separate lawsuit, the litigation costs shall be paid in accordance with the Measures on Payment of Litigation Costs.

<a id="art-196"></a>第一百九十六条　人民法院改变原判决、裁定、调解结果的，应当在裁判文书中对原审诉讼费用的负担一并作出处理。

**Article 196** Where the People's Court modifies the original judgment, ruling, or mediation result, it shall also make a ruling on the bearing of the litigation costs of the original trial in the adjudicatory instrument.

<a id="art-197"></a>第一百九十七条　诉讼标的物是证券的，按照证券交易规则并根据当事人起诉之日前最后一个交易日的收盘价、当日的市场价或者其载明的金额计算诉讼标的金额。

**Article 197** Where the subject matter of the action is securities, the amount in controversy shall be calculated in accordance with the securities trading rules and based on the closing price of the last trading day before the date of the party's prosecution, the market price on that day, or the amount stated thereon.

<a id="art-198"></a>第一百九十八条　诉讼标的物是房屋、土地、林木、车辆、船舶、文物等特定物或者知识产权，起诉时价值难以确定的，人民法院应当向原告释明主张过高或者过低的诉讼风险，以原告主张的价值确定诉讼标的金额。

**Article 198** Where the subject matter of the action is a specific object such as a house, land, trees, vehicle, vessel, or cultural relic, or intellectual property, and the value is difficult to determine at the time of prosecution, the People's Court shall explain to the plaintiff the litigation risks of claiming an excessively high or low value, and determine the amount in controversy based on the value claimed by the plaintiff.

<a id="art-199"></a>第一百九十九条　适用简易程序审理的案件转为普通程序的，原告自接到人民法院交纳诉讼费用通知之日起七日内补交案件受理费。  
原告无正当理由未按期足额补交的，按撤诉处理，已经收取的诉讼费用退还一半。

**Article 199** Where a case tried under the summary procedure is converted to ordinary procedure, the plaintiff shall make up the case acceptance fee within seven days from the date of receipt of the notice from the People's Court to pay litigation costs.  
If the plaintiff fails to make up the full amount within the time limit without justifiable reasons, the case shall be treated as withdrawn, and half of the litigation costs already collected shall be refunded.

<a id="art-200"></a>第二百条　破产程序中有关债务人的民事诉讼案件，按照财产案件标准交纳诉讼费，但劳动争议案件除外。

**Article 200** For civil litigation cases concerning the debtor in bankruptcy proceedings, the litigation costs shall be paid according to the standard for property cases, except for labor dispute cases.

<a id="art-201"></a>第二百零一条　既有财产性诉讼请求，又有非财产性诉讼请求的，按照财产性诉讼请求的标准交纳诉讼费。  
有多个财产性诉讼请求的，合并计算交纳诉讼费；诉讼请求中有多个非财产性诉讼请求的，按一件交纳诉讼费。

**Article 201** Where there are both proprietary claims and non-proprietary claims, the litigation costs shall be paid according to the standard for proprietary claims.  
Where there are multiple proprietary claims, the litigation costs shall be paid on a consolidated basis; where there are multiple non-proprietary claims, the litigation costs shall be paid as for a single claim.

<a id="art-202"></a>第二百零二条　原告、被告、第三人分别上诉的，按照上诉请求分别预交二审案件受理费。  
同一方多人共同上诉的，只预交一份二审案件受理费；分别上诉的，按照上诉请求分别预交二审案件受理费。

**Article 202** Where the plaintiff, defendant, and third party appeal separately, the case acceptance fee for the second instance shall be prepaid separately according to the appeal claims.  
Where multiple persons on the same side jointly appeal, only one copy of the case acceptance fee for the second instance shall be prepaid; where they appeal separately, the fee shall be prepaid separately according to the appeal claims.

<a id="art-203"></a>第二百零三条　承担连带责任的当事人败诉的，应当共同负担诉讼费用。

**Article 203** Where a party bearing joint and several liability loses the lawsuit, the party shall jointly bear the litigation costs.

<a id="art-204"></a>第二百零四条　实现担保物权案件，人民法院裁定拍卖、变卖担保财产的，申请费由债务人、担保人负担；人民法院裁定驳回申请的，申请费由申请人负担。  
申请人另行起诉的，其已经交纳的申请费可以从案件受理费中扣除。

**Article 204** For a case of realizing a security interest, where the People's Court rules to auction or sell off the secured property, the application fee shall be borne by the debtor and the surety; where the People's Court rules to dismiss the application, the application fee shall be borne by the applicant.  
Where the applicant files a separate lawsuit, the application fee already paid may be deducted from the case acceptance fee.

<a id="art-205"></a>第二百零五条　拍卖、变卖担保财产的裁定作出后，人民法院强制执行的，按照执行金额收取执行申请费。

**Article 205** After a ruling on auction or sale of secured property is made, if the People's Court enforces the ruling compulsorily, the enforcement application fee shall be collected based on the enforcement amount.

<a id="art-206"></a>第二百零六条　人民法院决定减半收取案件受理费的，只能减半一次。

**Article 206** Where the People's Court decides to reduce the case acceptance fee by half, the reduction may only be applied once.

<a id="art-207"></a>第二百零七条　判决生效后，胜诉方预交但不应负担的诉讼费用，人民法院应当退还，由败诉方向人民法院交纳，但胜诉方自愿承担或者同意败诉方直接向其支付的除外。  
当事人拒不交纳诉讼费用的，人民法院可以强制执行。

**Article 207** After a judgment becomes effective, the People's Court shall refund the litigation costs prepaid by the winning party that should not be borne by that party, and the losing party shall pay such costs to the People's Court, unless the winning party voluntarily bears such costs or agrees that the losing party may pay them directly to the winning party.  
If a party refuses to pay the litigation costs, the People's Court may enforce payment compulsorily.

### 十、第一审普通程序

<a id="art-208"></a>第二百零八条　人民法院接到当事人提交的民事起诉状时，对符合[民事诉讼法第一百二十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-122)的规定，且不属于第一百二十七条规定情形的，应当登记立案；对当场不能判定是否符合起诉条件的，应当接收起诉材料，并出具注明收到日期的书面凭证。  
需要补充必要相关材料的，人民法院应当及时告知当事人。在补齐相关材料后，应当在七日内决定是否立案。  
立案后发现不符合起诉条件或者属于[民事诉讼法第一百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-127)规定情形的，裁定驳回起诉。

**Article 208** When the People's Court receives a civil complaint submitted by a party, if the complaint complies with the provisions of Article 122 of the Civil Procedure Law and does not fall under the circumstances specified in Article 127, the court shall register and file the case; if it cannot immediately determine whether the conditions for prosecution are met, it shall accept the prosecution materials and issue a written certificate indicating the date of receipt.  
Where supplementary materials are necessary, the People's Court shall promptly inform the party. After the party completes the supplementary materials, the court shall decide whether to file the case within seven days.  
If, after filing, it is found that the conditions for prosecution are not met or the case falls under the circumstances specified in Article 127 of the Civil Procedure Law, the court shall rule to dismiss the prosecution.

<a id="art-209"></a>第二百零九条　原告提供被告的姓名或者名称、住所等信息具体明确，足以使被告与他人相区别的，可以认定为有明确的被告。  
起诉状列写被告信息不足以认定明确的被告的，人民法院可以告知原告补正。原告补正后仍不能确定明确的被告的，人民法院裁定不予受理。

**Article 209** Where the plaintiff provides the name or title, domicile, and other information of the defendant with sufficient specificity and clarity to distinguish the defendant from others, the defendant may be deemed as a definite defendant.  
If the defendant information listed in the complaint is insufficient to establish a definite defendant, the People's Court may inform the plaintiff to make corrections. If, after corrections, a definite defendant still cannot be determined, the People's Court shall rule not to accept the case.

<a id="art-210"></a>第二百一十条　原告在起诉状中有谩骂和人身攻击之辞的，人民法院应当告知其修改后提起诉讼。

**Article 210** Where the plaintiff includes abusive language or personal attacks in the complaint, the People's Court shall inform him or her to amend the complaint before filing the lawsuit.

<a id="art-211"></a>第二百一十一条　对本院没有管辖权的案件，告知原告向有管辖权的人民法院起诉；原告坚持起诉的，裁定不予受理；立案后发现本院没有管辖权的，应当将案件移送有管辖权的人民法院。

**Article 211** For a case over which the court has no jurisdiction, the plaintiff shall be informed to file the lawsuit with a People's Court that has jurisdiction; if the plaintiff insists on filing, the court shall rule not to accept the case; if, after filing, it is found that the court has no jurisdiction, the case shall be transferred to a People's Court that has jurisdiction.

<a id="art-212"></a>第二百一十二条　裁定不予受理、驳回起诉的案件，原告再次起诉，符合起诉条件且不属于[民事诉讼法第一百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-127)规定情形的，人民法院应予受理。

**Article 212** Where, for a case in which a ruling of non-acceptance or dismissal of prosecution has been made, the plaintiff files the lawsuit again, if the conditions for prosecution are met and the case does not fall under the circumstances specified in Article 127 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court shall accept the case.

<a id="art-213"></a>第二百一十三条　原告应当预交而未预交案件受理费，人民法院应当通知其预交，通知后仍不预交或者申请减、缓、免未获批准而仍不预交的，裁定按撤诉处理。

**Article 213** Where the plaintiff should have prepaid the case acceptance fee but has not done so, the People's Court shall notify him or her to prepay; if, after notification, the plaintiff still fails to prepay, or the application for reduction, deferral, or exemption is not approved and the plaintiff still fails to prepay, the court shall rule to treat the case as withdrawn.

<a id="art-214"></a>第二百一十四条　原告撤诉或者人民法院按撤诉处理后，原告以同一诉讼请求再次起诉的，人民法院应予受理。  
原告撤诉或者按撤诉处理的离婚案件，没有新情况、新理由，六个月内又起诉的，比照[民事诉讼法第一百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-127)第七项的规定不予受理。

**Article 214** Where, after the plaintiff withdraws the lawsuit or the People's Court treats the case as withdrawn, the plaintiff files a lawsuit again based on the same claims, the People's Court shall accept the case.  
For a divorce case in which the plaintiff withdraws the lawsuit or the case is treated as withdrawn, if a lawsuit is filed again within six months without new circumstances or new reasons, the case shall not be accepted by analogy to Article 127, item 7 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-215"></a>第二百一十五条　依照[民事诉讼法第一百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-127)第二项的规定，当事人在书面合同中订有仲裁条款，或者在发生纠纷后达成书面仲裁协议，一方向人民法院起诉的，人民法院应当告知原告向仲裁机构申请仲裁，其坚持起诉的，裁定不予受理，但仲裁条款或者仲裁协议不成立、无效、失效、内容不明确无法执行的除外。

**Article 215** In accordance with Article 127, item 2 of the Civil Procedure Law, where the parties have included an arbitration clause in a written contract or have reached a written arbitration agreement after a dispute arises, and one party files a lawsuit with the People's Court, the People's Court shall inform the plaintiff to apply for arbitration with an arbitration institution; if the plaintiff insists on filing the lawsuit, the court shall rule not to accept the case, except where the arbitration clause or arbitration agreement is not formed, is invalid, has ceased to have effect, or its content is unclear and cannot be enforced.

<a id="art-216"></a>第二百一十六条　在人民法院首次开庭前，被告以有书面仲裁协议为由对受理民事案件提出异议的，人民法院应当进行审查。  
经审查符合下列情形之一的，人民法院应当裁定驳回起诉：  
（一）仲裁机构或者人民法院已经确认仲裁协议有效的；  
（二）当事人没有在仲裁庭首次开庭前对仲裁协议的效力提出异议的；  
（三）仲裁协议符合[仲裁法第十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/律师、仲裁与公证/中华人民共和国仲裁法.md#art-16)规定且不具有[仲裁法第十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/律师、仲裁与公证/中华人民共和国仲裁法.md#art-17)规定情形的。

**Article 216** Before the first court session of the People's Court, if the defendant raises an objection to the acceptance of the civil case on the ground that there is a written arbitration agreement, the People's Court shall review the objection.  
Upon review, if any of the following circumstances is met, the People's Court shall rule to dismiss the prosecution:  
(1) an arbitration institution or a People's Court has already confirmed the validity of the arbitration agreement;  
(2) the parties did not raise an objection to the validity of the arbitration agreement before the first court session of the arbitral tribunal;  
(3) the arbitration agreement complies with Article 16 of the Arbitration Law and does not have the circumstances specified in Article 17 of the Arbitration Law.

<a id="art-217"></a>第二百一十七条　夫妻一方下落不明，另一方诉至人民法院，只要求离婚，不申请宣告下落不明人失踪或者死亡的案件，人民法院应当受理，对下落不明人公告送达诉讼文书。

**Article 217** Where one spouse's whereabouts are unknown and the other spouse files a lawsuit with the People's Court only for divorce, without applying for a declaration of disappearance or death of the missing person, the People's Court shall accept the case and serve the litigation documents by public announcement on the missing person.

<a id="art-218"></a>第二百一十八条　赡养费、扶养费、抚养费案件，裁判发生法律效力后，因新情况、新理由，一方当事人再行起诉要求增加或者减少费用的，人民法院应作为新案受理。

**Article 218** For cases involving alimony, support, or child support, after the judgment or ruling becomes legally effective, if a party files a lawsuit again claiming an increase or decrease in the amount due to new circumstances or new reasons, the People's Court shall accept the case as a new case.

<a id="art-219"></a>第二百一十九条　当事人超过诉讼时效期间起诉的，人民法院应予受理。受理后对方当事人提出诉讼时效抗辩，人民法院经审理认为抗辩事由成立的，判决驳回原告的诉讼请求。

**Article 219** Where a party files a lawsuit after the limitation of action has expired, the People's Court shall accept the case. After acceptance, if the other party raises a defense of limitation of action, and the People's Court, upon trial, finds that the defense is established, it shall enter a judgment dismissing the plaintiff's claims.

<a id="art-220"></a>第二百二十条　[民事诉讼法第七十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-71)、第一百三十七条、第一百五十九条规定的商业秘密，是指生产工艺、配方、贸易联系、购销渠道等当事人不愿公开的技术秘密、商业情报及信息。

**Article 220** The trade secrets prescribed in Articles 71, 137, and 159 of the Civil Procedure Law refer to technical secrets, commercial intelligence, and information such as production processes, formulas, trade connections, and purchase and sales channels that the parties are unwilling to disclose.

<a id="art-221"></a>第二百二十一条　基于同一事实发生的纠纷，当事人分别向同一人民法院起诉的，人民法院可以合并审理。

**Article 221** Where disputes arising from the same facts are filed separately with the same People's Court by the parties, the People's Court may consolidate the trials.

<a id="art-222"></a>第二百二十二条　原告在起诉状中直接列写第三人的，视为其申请人民法院追加该第三人参加诉讼。是否通知第三人参加诉讼，由人民法院审查决定。

**Article 222** Where the plaintiff directly lists a third party in the complaint, it shall be deemed as an application to the People's Court to add that third party to participate in the proceedings. Whether to notify the third party to participate in the proceedings shall be determined by the People's Court upon review.

<a id="art-223"></a>第二百二十三条　当事人在提交答辩状期间提出管辖异议，又针对起诉状的内容进行答辩的，人民法院应当依照[民事诉讼法第一百三十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-130)第一款的规定，对管辖异议进行审查。  
当事人未提出管辖异议，就案件实体内容进行答辩、陈述或者反诉的，可以认定为[民事诉讼法第一百三十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-130)第二款规定的应诉答辩。

**Article 223** Where a party raises a jurisdictional objection during the period for submitting the statement of defense and also defends on the merits of the complaint, the People's Court shall review the jurisdictional objection in accordance with Article 130, paragraph 1 of the Civil Procedure Law.  
Where a party does not raise a jurisdictional objection but defends on the merits, makes a statement, or files a counterclaim, this may be deemed as a defense upon appearance as prescribed in Article 130, paragraph 2 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-224"></a>第二百二十四条　依照[民事诉讼法第一百三十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-136)第四项规定，人民法院可以在答辩期届满后，通过组织证据交换、召集庭前会议等方式，作好审理前的准备。

**Article 224** In accordance with Article 136, item 4 of the Civil Procedure Law, after the expiration of the defense period, the People's Court may, by organizing evidence exchange, convening pretrial conferences, or other means, make preparations before trial.

<a id="art-225"></a>第二百二十五条　根据案件具体情况，庭前会议可以包括下列内容：  
（一）明确原告的诉讼请求和被告的答辩意见；  
（二）审查处理当事人增加、变更诉讼请求的申请和提出的反诉，以及第三人提出的与本案有关的诉讼请求；  
（三）根据当事人的申请决定调查收集证据，委托鉴定，要求当事人提供证据，进行勘验，进行证据保全；  
（四）组织交换证据；  
（五）归纳争议焦点；  
（六）进行调解。

**Article 225** Depending on the specific circumstances of the case, the pretrial conference may include the following:  
(1) clarifying the plaintiff's claims and the defendant's defense opinions;  
(2) reviewing and handling applications by parties to add or change claims, and any counterclaim filed, as well as claims related to the case raised by a third party;  
(3) deciding, upon a party's application, to investigate and collect evidence, to commission expert evaluation, to require the parties to provide evidence, to conduct inspections, and to preserve evidence;  
(4) organizing the exchange of evidence;  
(5) summarizing the issues in dispute;  
(6) conducting mediation.

<a id="art-226"></a>第二百二十六条　人民法院应当根据当事人的诉讼请求、答辩意见以及证据交换的情况，归纳争议焦点，并就归纳的争议焦点征求当事人的意见。

**Article 226** The People's Court shall, based on the parties' claims, defense opinions, and the situation of evidence exchange, summarize the issues in dispute, and seek the parties' opinions on the summarized issues in dispute.

<a id="art-227"></a>第二百二十七条　人民法院适用普通程序审理案件，应当在开庭三日前用传票传唤当事人。对诉讼代理人、证人、鉴定人、勘验人、翻译人员应当用通知书通知其到庭。当事人或者其他诉讼参与人在外地的，应当留有必要的在途时间。

**Article 227** Where the People's Court tries a case under ordinary procedure, it shall summon the parties by subpoena at least three days before the court session. The litigation agent, witnesses, expert evaluators, inspectors, and interpreters shall be notified by notice to appear in court. If any party or other participant in the proceedings is located in a different place, sufficient travel time shall be allowed.

<a id="art-228"></a>第二百二十八条　法庭审理应当围绕当事人争议的事实、证据和法律适用等焦点问题进行。

**Article 228** Court trial shall be conducted centering on the issues in dispute, including facts, evidence, and application of law, among others.

<a id="art-229"></a>第二百二十九条　当事人在庭审中对其在审理前的准备阶段认可的事实和证据提出不同意见的，人民法院应当责令其说明理由。必要时，可以责令其提供相应证据。人民法院应当结合当事人的诉讼能力、证据和案件的具体情况进行审查。理由成立的，可以列入争议焦点进行审理。

**Article 229** Where a party, during the court trial, expresses a different opinion on facts and evidence that were admitted during the pretrial preparation stage, the People's Court shall order that party to explain the reasons. If necessary, the court may order the party to provide corresponding evidence. The People's Court shall review the matter taking into account the party's litigation capability, the evidence, and the specific circumstances of the case. If the reasons are established, the matter may be included as an issue in dispute for trial.

<a id="art-230"></a>第二百三十条　人民法院根据案件具体情况并征得当事人同意，可以将法庭调查和法庭辩论合并进行。

**Article 230** The People's Court may, based on the specific circumstances of the case and with the consent of the parties, combine the court investigation and court debate into a single stage.

<a id="art-231"></a>第二百三十一条　当事人在法庭上提出新的证据的，人民法院应当依照[民事诉讼法第六十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-68)第二款规定和本解释相关规定处理。

**Article 231** Where a party presents new evidence during the court session, the People's Court shall handle the matter in accordance with Article 68, paragraph 2 of the Civil Procedure Law and relevant provisions of this Interpretation.

<a id="art-232"></a>第二百三十二条　在案件受理后，法庭辩论结束前，原告增加诉讼请求，被告提出反诉，第三人提出与本案有关的诉讼请求，可以合并审理的，人民法院应当合并审理。

**Article 232** After the acceptance of a case and before the conclusion of court debate, if the plaintiff adds claims, the defendant files a counterclaim, or a third party raises claims related to the case, and such claims can be tried together, the People's Court shall consolidate the trials.

<a id="art-233"></a>第二百三十三条　反诉的当事人应当限于本诉的当事人的范围。  
反诉与本诉的诉讼请求基于相同法律关系、诉讼请求之间具有因果关系，或者反诉与本诉的诉讼请求基于相同事实的，人民法院应当合并审理。  
反诉应由其他人民法院专属管辖，或者与本诉的诉讼标的及诉讼请求所依据的事实、理由无关联的，裁定不予受理，告知另行起诉。

**Article 233** The parties to a counterclaim shall be limited to the scope of the parties to the original claim.  
Where the claims of the counterclaim and the original claim are based on the same legal relationship, have a causal relationship between the claims, or are based on the same facts, the People's Court shall consolidate the trials.  
If the counterclaim is subject to exclusive jurisdiction by another People's Court, or has no connection with the subject matter of the original claim and the facts and reasons on which the claims are based, the court shall rule not to accept it and inform the party to file a separate lawsuit.

<a id="art-234"></a>第二百三十四条　无民事行为能力人的离婚诉讼，当事人的法定代理人应当到庭；法定代理人不能到庭的，人民法院应当在查清事实的基础上，依法作出判决。

**Article 234** In a divorce action involving a person without civil capacity, the legal representative of the party shall appear in court; if the legal representative cannot appear, the People's Court shall, on the basis of ascertaining the facts, enter a judgment according to law.

<a id="art-235"></a>第二百三十五条　无民事行为能力的当事人的法定代理人，经传票传唤无正当理由拒不到庭，属于原告方的，比照[民事诉讼法第一百四十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-146)的规定，按撤诉处理；属于被告方的，比照[民事诉讼法第一百四十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-147)的规定，缺席判决。必要时，人民法院可以拘传其到庭。

**Article 235** If the legal representative of a party without capacity for civil conduct, after being summoned by service of a summons, refuses to appear in court without justified grounds, where such party is the plaintiff, the case shall be treated as withdrawal of the prosecution by reference to the provisions of Article 146 of the Civil Procedure Law; where such party is the defendant, a judgment by default shall be entered by reference to the provisions of Article 147 of the Civil Procedure Law. When necessary, the People's Court may issue a compulsory summons for such representative to appear in court.

<a id="art-236"></a>第二百三十六条　有独立请求权的第三人经人民法院传票传唤，无正当理由拒不到庭的，或者未经法庭许可中途退庭的，比照[民事诉讼法第一百四十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-146)的规定，按撤诉处理。

**Article 236** If a third party with an independent claim, after being summoned by the People's Court by service of a summons, refuses to appear in court without justified grounds, or withdraws from the courtroom during the hearing without permission of the court, the case shall be treated as withdrawal of the prosecution by reference to the provisions of Article 146 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-237"></a>第二百三十七条　有独立请求权的第三人参加诉讼后，原告申请撤诉，人民法院在准许原告撤诉后，有独立请求权的第三人作为另案原告，原案原告、被告作为另案被告，诉讼继续进行。

**Article 237** After a third party with an independent claim has participated in the proceedings, if the plaintiff applies to withdraw the prosecution, after the People's Court permits the plaintiff to withdraw the prosecution, the third party with an independent claim shall act as the plaintiff in another case, and the original plaintiff and defendant shall act as the defendants in that other case, and the proceedings shall continue.

<a id="art-238"></a>第二百三十八条　当事人申请撤诉或者依法可以按撤诉处理的案件，如果当事人有违反法律的行为需要依法处理的，人民法院可以不准许撤诉或者不按撤诉处理。  
法庭辩论终结后原告申请撤诉，被告不同意的，人民法院可以不予准许。

**Article 238** In a case where a party applies to withdraw the prosecution or may be treated as withdrawal of the prosecution according to law, if the party has committed an act violating the law that needs to be dealt with according to law, the People's Court may refuse to permit the withdrawal of the prosecution or may not treat it as withdrawal of the prosecution.  
After the conclusion of court debate, if the plaintiff applies to withdraw the prosecution and the defendant does not consent, the People's Court may refuse to permit it.

<a id="art-239"></a>第二百三十九条　人民法院准许本诉原告撤诉的，应当对反诉继续审理；被告申请撤回反诉的，人民法院应予准许。

**Article 239** If the People's Court permits the plaintiff in the principal action to withdraw the prosecution, it shall continue to hear the counterclaim; if the defendant applies to withdraw the counterclaim, the People's Court shall permit it.

<a id="art-240"></a>第二百四十条　无独立请求权的第三人经人民法院传票传唤，无正当理由拒不到庭，或者未经法庭许可中途退庭的，不影响案件的审理。

**Article 240** If a third party without an independent claim, after being summoned by the People's Court by service of a summons, refuses to appear in court without justified grounds, or withdraws from the courtroom during the hearing without permission of the court, this shall not affect the hearing of the case.

<a id="art-241"></a>第二百四十一条　被告经传票传唤无正当理由拒不到庭，或者未经法庭许可中途退庭的，人民法院应当按期开庭或者继续开庭审理，对到庭的当事人诉讼请求、双方的诉辩理由以及已经提交的证据及其他诉讼材料进行审理后，可以依法缺席判决。

**Article 241** If the defendant, after being summoned by service of a summons, refuses to appear in court without justified grounds, or withdraws from the courtroom during the hearing without permission of the court, the People's Court shall hold the hearing on schedule or continue the hearing, and after hearing the claims of the appearing party, the arguments of both sides, and the evidence and other litigation materials already submitted, may enter a default judgment according to law.

<a id="art-242"></a>第二百四十二条　一审宣判后，原审人民法院发现判决有错误，当事人在上诉期内提出上诉的，原审人民法院可以提出原判决有错误的意见，报送第二审人民法院，由第二审人民法院按照第二审程序进行审理；当事人不上诉的，按照审判监督程序处理。

**Article 242** After the judgment of first instance is pronounced, if the original trial People's Court discovers an error in the judgment and a party files an appeal within the appeal period, the original trial People's Court may submit its opinion that the original judgment contains an error to the second instance People's Court, and the second instance People's Court shall hear the case in accordance with the procedure of second instance; if no party appeals, the case shall be handled in accordance with the trial supervision procedure.

<a id="art-243"></a>第二百四十三条　[民事诉讼法第一百五十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-152)规定的审限，是指从立案之日起至裁判宣告、调解书送达之日止的期间，但公告期间、鉴定期间、双方当事人和解期间、审理当事人提出的管辖异议以及处理人民法院之间的管辖争议期间不应计算在内。

**Article 243** The time limit for trial as provided in Article 152 of the Civil Procedure Law refers to the period from the date of filing the case to the date of pronouncement of the judgment or service of the mediation document, except that the period for public announcement, the period for expert evaluation, the period for conciliation between the parties, the period for hearing jurisdictional objections raised by the parties, and the period for handling jurisdictional disputes between People's Courts shall not be counted.

<a id="art-244"></a>第二百四十四条　可以上诉的判决书、裁定书不能同时送达双方当事人的，上诉期从各自收到判决书、裁定书之日计算。

**Article 244** If a judgment or ruling subject to appeal cannot be served on both parties simultaneously, the appeal period shall be calculated from the date each party receives the judgment or ruling respectively.

<a id="art-245"></a>第二百四十五条　[民事诉讼法第一百五十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-157)第一款第七项规定的笔误是指法律文书误写、误算，诉讼费用漏写、误算和其他笔误。

**Article 245** The clerical error referred to in subparagraph (7) of the first paragraph of Article 157 of the Civil Procedure Law means mistaken writing or miscalculation in legal documents, omission or miscalculation of litigation costs, and other clerical errors.

<a id="art-246"></a>第二百四十六条　裁定中止诉讼的原因消除，恢复诉讼程序时，不必撤销原裁定，从人民法院通知或者准许当事人双方继续进行诉讼时起，中止诉讼的裁定即失去效力。

**Article 246** When the cause for the ruling to suspend the proceedings is eliminated and the proceedings are resumed, the original ruling need not be revoked; from the time the People's Court notifies or permits both parties to continue the proceedings, the ruling to suspend the proceedings shall cease to have effect.

<a id="art-247"></a>第二百四十七条　当事人就已经提起诉讼的事项在诉讼过程中或者裁判生效后再次起诉，同时符合下列条件的，构成重复起诉：  
（一）后诉与前诉的当事人相同；  
（二）后诉与前诉的诉讼标的相同；  
（三）后诉与前诉的诉讼请求相同，或者后诉的诉讼请求实质上否定前诉裁判结果。  
当事人重复起诉的，裁定不予受理；已经受理的，裁定驳回起诉，但法律、司法解释另有规定的除外。

**Article 247** Where a party files another lawsuit regarding a matter for which a lawsuit has already been instituted, either during the course of the proceedings or after the judgment has taken effect, and the following conditions are concurrently satisfied, it shall constitute a duplicative prosecution:  
(1) The parties in the subsequent action are identical to those in the prior action;  
(2) The subject matter of the subsequent action is identical to that of the prior action;  
(3) The claims in the subsequent action are identical to those in the prior action, or the claims in the subsequent action substantially negate the result of the prior judgment.  
If a party institutes a duplicative prosecution, the People's Court shall rule not to accept the case; if already accepted, the People's Court shall rule to dismiss the prosecution, unless otherwise provided by law or judicial interpretation.

<a id="art-248"></a>第二百四十八条　裁判发生法律效力后，发生新的事实，当事人再次提起诉讼的，人民法院应当依法受理。

**Article 248** After a judgment has taken legal effect, if new facts arise and a party files another lawsuit, the People's Court shall accept the case according to law.

<a id="art-249"></a>第二百四十九条　在诉讼中，争议的民事权利义务转移的，不影响当事人的诉讼主体资格和诉讼地位。人民法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定对受让人具有拘束力。  
受让人申请以无独立请求权的第三人身份参加诉讼的，人民法院可予准许。受让人申请替代当事人承担诉讼的，人民法院可以根据案件的具体情况决定是否准许；不予准许的，可以追加其为无独立请求权的第三人。

**Article 249** During the proceedings, if the disputed civil rights and obligations are transferred, this shall not affect the party's status as a litigant and position in the proceedings. The judgments and rulings rendered by the People's Court that have taken legal effect shall be binding on the transferee.  
If the transferee applies to participate in the proceedings as a third party without an independent claim, the People's Court may permit it. If the transferee applies to substitute for the party to assume the proceedings, the People's Court may decide whether to permit it based on the specific circumstances of the case; if not permitted, the transferee may be added as a third party without an independent claim.

<a id="art-250"></a>第二百五十条　依照[本解释第二百四十九条](#art-249)规定，人民法院准许受让人替代当事人承担诉讼的，裁定变更当事人。  
变更当事人后，诉讼程序以受让人为当事人继续进行，原当事人应当退出诉讼。原当事人已经完成的诉讼行为对受让人具有拘束力。

**Article 250** Where, pursuant to Article 249 of this Interpretation, the People's Court permits the transferee to substitute for the party to assume the proceedings, it shall rule to change the party.  
After the change of party, the proceedings shall continue with the transferee as the party, and the original party shall withdraw from the proceedings. The litigation acts already completed by the original party shall be binding on the transferee.

<a id="art-251"></a>第二百五十一条　二审裁定撤销一审判决发回重审的案件，当事人申请变更、增加诉讼请求或者提出反诉，第三人提出与本案有关的诉讼请求的，依照[民事诉讼法第一百四十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-143)规定处理。

**Article 251** In a case where the second instance ruling sets aside the first instance judgment and remands the case for retrial, if a party applies to change or add claims or raises a counterclaim, or if a third party raises claims related to the case, the matter shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of Article 143 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-252"></a>第二百五十二条　再审裁定撤销原判决、裁定发回重审的案件，当事人申请变更、增加诉讼请求或者提出反诉，符合下列情形之一的，人民法院应当准许：  
（一）原审未合法传唤缺席判决，影响当事人行使诉讼权利的；  
（二）追加新的诉讼当事人的；  
（三）诉讼标的物灭失或者发生变化致使原诉讼请求无法实现的；  
（四）当事人申请变更、增加的诉讼请求或者提出的反诉，无法通过另诉解决的。

**Article 252** In a case where a retrial ruling sets aside the original judgment or ruling and remands the case for retrial, if a party applies to change or add claims or raises a counterclaim, and any of the following circumstances exists, the People's Court shall permit it:  
(1) The original trial, without lawful summons, entered a default judgment, affecting the party's exercise of litigation rights;  
(2) A new litigant is added;  
(3) The subject matter of the action is lost or has changed, rendering the original claims impossible to realize;  
(4) The claims changed or added by the party or the counterclaim raised by the party cannot be resolved through a separate lawsuit.

<a id="art-253"></a>第二百五十三条　当庭宣判的案件，除当事人当庭要求邮寄发送裁判文书的外，人民法院应当告知当事人或者诉讼代理人领取裁判文书的时间和地点以及逾期不领取的法律后果。上述情况，应当记入笔录。

**Article 253** In a case where judgment is pronounced in court, unless a party requests at the court session that the judgment document be sent by mail, the People's Court shall inform the party or the litigation agent of the time and place for collecting the judgment document and the legal consequences of failing to collect it within the prescribed time. The above circumstances shall be recorded in the record.

<a id="art-254"></a>第二百五十四条　公民、法人或者其他组织申请查阅发生法律效力的判决书、裁定书的，应当向作出该生效裁判的人民法院提出。申请应当以书面形式提出，并提供具体的案号或者当事人姓名、名称。

**Article 254** If a citizen, legal person, or other organization applies to inspect a judgment or ruling that has taken legal effect, the application shall be filed with the People's Court that rendered the effective judgment or ruling. The application shall be made in writing and shall provide the specific case number or the names of the parties.

<a id="art-255"></a>第二百五十五条　对于查阅判决书、裁定书的申请，人民法院根据下列情形分别处理：  
（一）判决书、裁定书已经通过信息网络向社会公开的，应当引导申请人自行查阅；  
（二）判决书、裁定书未通过信息网络向社会公开，且申请符合要求的，应当及时提供便捷的查阅服务；  
（三）判决书、裁定书尚未发生法律效力，或者已失去法律效力的，不提供查阅并告知申请人；  
（四）发生法律效力的判决书、裁定书不是本院作出的，应当告知申请人向作出生效裁判的人民法院申请查阅；  
（五）申请查阅的内容涉及国家秘密、商业秘密、个人隐私的，不予准许并告知申请人。

**Article 255** For an application to inspect a judgment or ruling, the People's Court shall handle it according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If the judgment or ruling has already been made public through the information network, the court shall guide the applicant to inspect it on their own;  
(2) If the judgment or ruling has not been made public through the information network, and the application meets the requirements, the court shall promptly provide convenient inspection services;  
(3) If the judgment or ruling has not yet taken legal effect, or has ceased to have legal effect, the court shall not provide inspection and shall inform the applicant;  
(4) If the judgment or ruling that has taken legal effect was not rendered by this court, the court shall inform the applicant to apply for inspection with the People's Court that rendered the effective judgment or ruling;  
(5) If the content sought to be inspected involves state secrets, trade secrets, or personal privacy, the court shall not permit the inspection and shall inform the applicant.

### 十一、简易程序

<a id="art-256"></a>第二百五十六条　[民事诉讼法第一百六十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-160)规定的简单民事案件中的事实清楚，是指当事人对争议的事实陈述基本一致，并能提供相应的证据，无须人民法院调查收集证据即可查明事实；权利义务关系明确是指能明确区分谁是责任的承担者，谁是权利的享有者；争议不大是指当事人对案件的是非、责任承担以及诉讼标的争执无原则分歧。

**Article 256** In simple civil cases as provided in Article 160 of the Civil Procedure Law, 'clearly ascertained facts' means that the parties' statements regarding the disputed facts are basically consistent and they can provide corresponding evidence, so that the facts can be ascertained without the People's Court conducting investigation and collecting evidence; 'clearly defined rights and obligations' means that it can be clearly distinguished who bears the liability and who enjoys the rights; 'minor disputes' means that there is no fundamental disagreement between the parties concerning the merits of the case, the allocation of liability, or the subject matter of the action.

<a id="art-257"></a>第二百五十七条　下列案件，不适用简易程序：  
（一）起诉时被告下落不明的；  
（二）发回重审的；  
（三）当事人一方人数众多的；  
（四）适用审判监督程序的；  
（五）涉及国家利益、社会公共利益的；  
（六）第三人起诉请求改变或者撤销生效判决、裁定、调解书的；  
（七）其他不宜适用简易程序的案件。

**Article 257** The summary procedure shall not apply to the following cases:  
(1) Where the defendant's whereabouts are unknown at the time of prosecution;  
(2) Where the case is remanded for retrial;  
(3) Where one party has a large number of persons;  
(4) Where the trial supervision procedure applies;  
(5) Where state interests or public interests are involved;  
(6) Where a third party initiates a lawsuit requesting the alteration or revocation of an effective judgment, ruling, or mediation document;  
(7) Other cases to which the summary procedure is not suitable to apply.

<a id="art-258"></a>第二百五十八条　适用简易程序审理的案件，审理期限到期后，有特殊情况需要延长的，经本院院长批准，可以延长审理期限。延长后的审理期限累计不得超过四个月。  
人民法院发现案件不宜适用简易程序，需要转为普通程序审理的，应当在审理期限届满前作出裁定并将审判人员及相关事项书面通知双方当事人。  
案件转为普通程序审理的，审理期限自人民法院立案之日计算。

**Article 258** For a case tried under the summary procedure, if after the expiration of the trial period, special circumstances require an extension, the trial period may be extended with the approval of the president of the court. The cumulative trial period after extension shall not exceed four months.  
If the People's Court finds that a case is not suitable for the summary procedure and needs to be converted to the ordinary procedure for trial, it shall make a ruling before the expiration of the trial period and notify both parties in writing of the trial personnel and relevant matters.  
If a case is converted to the ordinary procedure for trial, the trial period shall be calculated from the date of filing the case with the People's Court.

<a id="art-259"></a>第二百五十九条　当事人双方可就开庭方式向人民法院提出申请，由人民法院决定是否准许。经当事人双方同意，可以采用视听传输技术等方式开庭。

**Article 259** Both parties may file an application with the People's Court regarding the method of court session, and the People's Court shall decide whether to permit it. With the consent of both parties, the court session may be conducted by means of audiovisual transmission technology or other means.

<a id="art-260"></a>第二百六十条　已经按照普通程序审理的案件，在开庭后不得转为简易程序审理。

**Article 260** A case that has already been tried under the ordinary procedure shall not be converted to the summary procedure after the court session.

<a id="art-261"></a>第二百六十一条　适用简易程序审理案件，人民法院可以依照[民事诉讼法第九十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-90)、第一百六十二条的规定采取捎口信、电话、短信、传真、电子邮件等简便方式传唤双方当事人、通知证人和送达诉讼文书。  
以简便方式送达的开庭通知，未经当事人确认或者没有其他证据证明当事人已经收到的，人民法院不得缺席判决。  
适用简易程序审理案件，由审判员独任审判，书记员担任记录。

**Article 261** When trying a case under the summary procedure, the People's Court may, in accordance with the provisions of Articles 90 and 162 of the Civil Procedure Law, use simple methods such as oral messages, telephone, text messages, fax, or email to summon both parties, notify witnesses, and serve litigation documents.  
If a notice of court session is served by simple means and is not confirmed by the party or there is no other evidence proving that the party has received it, the People's Court shall not enter a default judgment.  
When trying a case under the summary procedure, a single judge shall conduct the trial, and a court clerk shall keep the record.

<a id="art-262"></a>第二百六十二条　人民法庭制作的判决书、裁定书、调解书，必须加盖基层人民法院印章，不得用人民法庭的印章代替基层人民法院的印章。

**Article 262** Judgments, rulings, and mediation documents prepared by a people's tribunal must be affixed with the seal of the Primary People's Court; the seal of the people's tribunal shall not be used in place of the seal of the Primary People's Court.

<a id="art-263"></a>第二百六十三条　适用简易程序审理案件，卷宗中应当具备以下材料：  
（一）起诉状或者口头起诉笔录；  
（二）答辩状或者口头答辩笔录；  
（三）当事人身份证明材料；  
（四）委托他人代理诉讼的授权委托书或者口头委托笔录；  
（五）证据；  
（六）询问当事人笔录；  
（七）审理（包括调解）笔录；  
（八）判决书、裁定书、调解书或者调解协议；  
（九）送达和宣判笔录；  
（十）执行情况；  
（十一）诉讼费收据；  
（十二）适用[民事诉讼法第一百六十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-165)规定审理的，有关程序适用的书面告知。

**Article 263** When trying a case under the summary procedure, the case file shall contain the following materials:  
(1) The statement of claim or the record of oral prosecution;  
(2) The statement of defense or the record of oral defense;  
(3) The identity certification documents of the parties;  
(4) The power of attorney for authorizing another person to act as an agent ad litem, or the record of oral authorization;  
(5) Evidence;  
(6) The record of questioning the parties;  
(7) The record of trial (including mediation);  
(8) The judgment, ruling, mediation document, or mediation agreement;  
(9) The record of service and pronouncement of judgment;  
(10) The status of enforcement;  
(11) The receipt for litigation costs;  
(12) If the case is tried in accordance with Article 165 of the Civil Procedure Law, the written notice concerning the application of the procedure.

<a id="art-264"></a>第二百六十四条　当事人双方根据[民事诉讼法第一百六十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-160)第二款规定约定适用简易程序的，应当在开庭前提出。口头提出的，记入笔录，由双方当事人签名或者捺印确认。  
[本解释第二百五十七条](#art-257)规定的案件，当事人约定适用简易程序的，人民法院不予准许。

**Article 264** If both parties agree to apply the summary procedure pursuant to the provisions of the second paragraph of Article 160 of the Civil Procedure Law, they shall raise the request before the court session. If the request is made orally, it shall be recorded in the record and confirmed by the signatures or fingerprints of both parties.  
For cases provided in Article 257 of this Interpretation, if the parties agree to apply the summary procedure, the People's Court shall not permit it.

<a id="art-265"></a>第二百六十五条　原告口头起诉的，人民法院应当将当事人的姓名、性别、工作单位、住所、联系方式等基本信息，诉讼请求，事实及理由等准确记入笔录，由原告核对无误后签名或者捺印。对当事人提交的证据材料，应当出具收据。
