**Article 265** If the plaintiff prosecutes orally, the People's Court shall accurately record in the record the basic information of the party such as name, gender, work unit, domicile, and contact information, the claims, the facts and reasons, etc., and the plaintiff shall check it for accuracy and then sign or affix a fingerprint. For the evidentiary materials submitted by the party, a receipt shall be issued.

<a id="art-266"></a>第二百六十六条　适用简易程序案件的举证期限由人民法院确定，也可以由当事人协商一致并经人民法院准许，但不得超过十五日。被告要求书面答辩的，人民法院可在征得其同意的基础上，合理确定答辩期间。  
人民法院应当将举证期限和开庭日期告知双方当事人，并向当事人说明逾期举证以及拒不到庭的法律后果，由双方当事人在笔录和开庭传票的送达回证上签名或者捺印。  
当事人双方均表示不需要举证期限、答辩期间的，人民法院可以立即开庭审理或者确定开庭日期。

**Article 266** The time limit for producing evidence in a case under the summary procedure shall be determined by the People's Court, or may be agreed upon by both parties and approved by the People's Court, but shall not exceed fifteen days. If the defendant requests a written defense, the People's Court may reasonably determine the defense period on the basis of obtaining the defendant's consent.  
The People's Court shall inform both parties of the time limit for producing evidence and the date of the court session, and shall explain to the parties the legal consequences of producing evidence beyond the time limit and refusing to appear in court. Both parties shall sign or affix their fingerprints on the record and the acknowledgement of service of the summons.  
If both parties indicate that they do not need a time limit for producing evidence or a defense period, the People's Court may immediately hold the court session or determine the date of the court session.

<a id="art-267"></a>第二百六十七条　适用简易程序审理案件，可以简便方式进行审理前的准备。

**Article 267** When trying a case under the summary procedure, preparations before trial may be conducted by simple means.

<a id="art-268"></a>第二百六十八条　对没有委托律师、基层法律服务工作者代理诉讼的当事人，人民法院在庭审过程中可以对回避、自认、举证证明责任等相关内容向其作必要的解释或者说明，并在庭审过程中适当提示当事人正确行使诉讼权利、履行诉讼义务。

**Article 268** For a party who has not retained a lawyer or a primary-level legal service worker to act as agent ad litem, the People's Court may, during the court proceedings, provide necessary explanations or clarifications to the party regarding avoidance, admission, burden of proof, and other related matters, and appropriately remind the party to properly exercise litigation rights and perform litigation obligations during the court proceedings.

<a id="art-269"></a>第二百六十九条　当事人就案件适用简易程序提出异议，人民法院经审查，异议成立的，裁定转为普通程序；异议不成立的，裁定驳回。裁定以口头方式作出的，应当记入笔录。  
转为普通程序的，人民法院应当将审判人员及相关事项以书面形式通知双方当事人。  
转为普通程序前，双方当事人已确认的事实，可以不再进行举证、质证。

**Article 269** If a party raises an objection to the application of the summary procedure to the case, and after examination by the People's Court, the objection is sustained, the court shall rule to convert the case to the ordinary procedure; if the objection is not sustained, the court shall rule to reject it. If the ruling is made orally, it shall be recorded in the record.  
If the case is converted to the ordinary procedure, the People's Court shall notify both parties in writing of the trial personnel and relevant matters.  
Facts that have been confirmed by both parties before the conversion to the ordinary procedure need not be subject to further production of evidence or cross-examination.

<a id="art-270"></a>第二百七十条　适用简易程序审理的案件，有下列情形之一的，人民法院在制作判决书、裁定书、调解书时，对认定事实或者裁判理由部分可以适当简化：  
（一）当事人达成调解协议并需要制作民事调解书的；  
（二）一方当事人明确表示承认对方全部或者部分诉讼请求的；  
（三）涉及商业秘密、个人隐私的案件，当事人一方要求简化裁判文书中的相关内容，人民法院认为理由正当的；  
（四）当事人双方同意简化的。

**Article 270** In a case tried under the summary procedure, if any of the following circumstances exists, the People's Court may appropriately simplify the part concerning the findings of facts or the reasons for the judgment when preparing the judgment, ruling, or mediation document:  
(1) The parties have reached a mediation agreement and a civil mediation document needs to be prepared;  
(2) One party expressly admits all or part of the claims of the other party;  
(3) In a case involving trade secrets or personal privacy, one party requests simplification of the relevant content in the judgment document, and the People's Court considers the reason to be justified;  
(4) Both parties agree to simplification.

### 十二、简易程序中的小额诉讼

<a id="art-271"></a>第二百七十一条　人民法院审理小额诉讼案件，适用[民事诉讼法第一百六十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-165)的规定，实行一审终审。

**Article 271** When a People's Court tries a small claims case, the provisions of Article 165 of the Civil Procedure Law shall apply, and the judgment shall be final and not subject to appeal.

<a id="art-272"></a>第二百七十二条　[民事诉讼法第一百六十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-165)规定的各省、自治区、直辖市上年度就业人员年平均工资，是指已经公布的各省、自治区、直辖市上一年度就业人员年平均工资。在上一年度就业人员年平均工资公布前，以已经公布的最近年度就业人员年平均工资为准。

**Article 272** The average annual wage of employed persons in the previous year in each province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government as provided in Article 165 of the Civil Procedure Law refers to the published average annual wage of employed persons in the previous year in each province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government. Before the average annual wage of employed persons in the previous year is published, the published average annual wage of employed persons in the most recent year shall prevail.

<a id="art-273"></a>第二百七十三条　海事法院可以适用小额诉讼的程序审理海事、海商案件。案件标的额应当以实际受理案件的海事法院或者其派出法庭所在的省、自治区、直辖市上年度就业人员年平均工资为基数计算。

**Article 273** A maritime court may apply the small claims procedure to try maritime and maritime commerce cases. The amount in controversy of the case shall be calculated based on the average annual wage of employed persons in the previous year in the province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government where the maritime court accepting the case or its dispatched tribunal is located.

<a id="art-274"></a>第二百七十四条　人民法院受理小额诉讼案件，应当向当事人告知该类案件的审判组织、一审终审、审理期限、诉讼费用交纳标准等相关事项。

**Article 274** When a People's Court accepts a small claims case, it shall inform the parties of the trial organization for such cases, the finality of the first instance judgment, the trial period, the standards for paying litigation costs, and other related matters.

<a id="art-275"></a>第二百七十五条　小额诉讼案件的举证期限由人民法院确定，也可以由当事人协商一致并经人民法院准许，但一般不超过七日。  
被告要求书面答辩的，人民法院可以在征得其同意的基础上合理确定答辩期间，但最长不得超过十五日。  
当事人到庭后表示不需要举证期限和答辩期间的，人民法院可立即开庭审理。

**Article 275** The time limit for producing evidence in a small claims case shall be determined by the People's Court, or may be agreed upon by both parties and approved by the People's Court, but generally shall not exceed seven days.  
If the defendant requests a written defense, the People's Court may reasonably determine the defense period on the basis of obtaining the defendant's consent, but the maximum shall not exceed fifteen days.  
If, after appearing in court, the parties indicate that they do not need a time limit for producing evidence or a defense period, the People's Court may immediately hold the court session.

<a id="art-276"></a>第二百七十六条　当事人对小额诉讼案件提出管辖异议的，人民法院应当作出裁定。裁定一经作出即生效。

**Article 276** If a party raises a jurisdictional objection in a small claims case, the People's Court shall render a ruling. The ruling shall take effect as soon as it is made.

<a id="art-277"></a>第二百七十七条　人民法院受理小额诉讼案件后，发现起诉不符合[民事诉讼法第一百二十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-122)规定的起诉条件的，裁定驳回起诉。裁定一经作出即生效。

**Article 277** After a People's Court accepts a small claims case, if it discovers that the prosecution does not meet the conditions for prosecution provided in Article 122 of the Civil Procedure Law, it shall rule to dismiss the prosecution. The ruling shall take effect as soon as it is made.

<a id="art-278"></a>第二百七十八条　因当事人申请增加或者变更诉讼请求、提出反诉、追加当事人等，致使案件不符合小额诉讼案件条件的，应当适用简易程序的其他规定审理。  
前款规定案件，应当适用普通程序审理的，裁定转为普通程序。  
适用简易程序的其他规定或者普通程序审理前，双方当事人已确认的事实，可以不再进行举证、质证。

**Article 278** If, due to a party's application to add or change claims, raise a counterclaim, add parties, etc., the case no longer meets the conditions for a small claims case, the other provisions of the summary procedure shall apply to the trial.  
If the case provided in the preceding paragraph shall be tried under the ordinary procedure, a ruling shall be made to convert it to the ordinary procedure.  
Before the trial under the other provisions of the summary procedure or the ordinary procedure, facts that have been confirmed by both parties need not be subject to further production of evidence or cross-examination.

<a id="art-279"></a>第二百七十九条　当事人对按照小额诉讼案件审理有异议的，应当在开庭前提出。人民法院经审查，异议成立的，适用简易程序的其他规定审理或者裁定转为普通程序；异议不成立的，裁定驳回。裁定以口头方式作出的，应当记入笔录。

**Article 279** If a party raises an objection to the trial of a case as a small claims case, the objection shall be raised before the court session. After examination by the People's Court, if the objection is sustained, the other provisions of the summary procedure shall apply to the trial, or a ruling shall be made to convert it to the ordinary procedure; if the objection is not sustained, the court shall rule to reject it. If the ruling is made orally, it shall be recorded in the record.

<a id="art-280"></a>第二百八十条　小额诉讼案件的裁判文书可以简化，主要记载当事人基本信息、诉讼请求、裁判主文等内容。

**Article 280** The judgment documents in a small claims case may be simplified, mainly recording the basic information of the parties, the claims, and the main text of the judgment.

<a id="art-281"></a>第二百八十一条　人民法院审理小额诉讼案件，本解释没有规定的，适用简易程序的其他规定。  
（待续）

**Article 281** When a People's Court tries a small claims case, matters not provided for in this Interpretation shall be governed by the other provisions of the summary procedure.  
(To be continued)

### 十三、公益诉讼

<a id="art-282"></a>第二百八十二条　环境保护法、消费者权益保护法等法律规定的机关和有关组织对污染环境、侵害众多消费者合法权益等损害社会公共利益的行为，根据[民事诉讼法第五十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-58)规定提起公益诉讼，符合下列条件的，人民法院应当受理：  
（一）有明确的被告；  
（二）有具体的诉讼请求；  
（三）有社会公共利益受到损害的初步证据；  
（四）属于人民法院受理民事诉讼的范围和受诉人民法院管辖。

**Article 282** Where an authority or relevant organization as provided for by laws such as the Environmental Protection Law and the Law on the Protection of Consumer Rights and Interests initiates a public interest action under Article 58 of the Civil Procedure Law against conduct that pollutes the environment, infringes upon the legitimate rights and interests of numerous consumers, or otherwise harms the social public interest, the People's Court shall accept the case if the following conditions are met:  
(1) there is a definite defendant;  
(2) there are specific claims;  
(3) there is preliminary evidence that the social public interest has been harmed; and  
(4) the case falls within the scope of civil actions accepted by the People's Court and under the jurisdiction of the court receiving the action.

<a id="art-283"></a>第二百八十三条　公益诉讼案件由侵权行为地或者被告住所地中级人民法院管辖，但法律、司法解释另有规定的除外。  
因污染海洋环境提起的公益诉讼，由污染发生地、损害结果地或者采取预防污染措施地海事法院管辖。  
对同一侵权行为分别向两个以上人民法院提起公益诉讼的，由最先立案的人民法院管辖，必要时由它们的共同上级人民法院指定管辖。

**Article 283** A public interest action case shall be under the jurisdiction of the Intermediate People's Court of the place where the tortious act was committed or where the defendant has its domicile, unless otherwise provided by law or judicial interpretation.  
A public interest action initiated due to pollution of the marine environment shall be under the jurisdiction of the maritime court of the place where the pollution occurred, where the damage resulted, or where measures to prevent pollution were taken.  
Where public interest actions are initiated separately against the same tortious act before two or more People's Courts, the People's Court that first dockets the case shall have jurisdiction; when necessary, their common higher People's Court shall designate a court to have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-284"></a>第二百八十四条　人民法院受理公益诉讼案件后，应当在十日内书面告知相关行政主管部门。

**Article 284** After accepting a public interest action case, the People's Court shall, within ten days, notify in writing the relevant administrative department.

<a id="art-285"></a>第二百八十五条　人民法院受理公益诉讼案件后，依法可以提起诉讼的其他机关和有关组织，可以在开庭前向人民法院申请参加诉讼。人民法院准许参加诉讼的，列为共同原告。

**Article 285** After the People's Court accepts a public interest action case, other authorities and relevant organizations that may initiate an action according to law may apply to the People's Court to participate in the action before the trial begins. If the People's Court permits their participation, they shall be listed as co-plaintiffs.

<a id="art-286"></a>第二百八十六条　人民法院受理公益诉讼案件，不影响同一侵权行为的受害人根据[民事诉讼法第一百二十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-122)规定提起诉讼。

**Article 286** The acceptance of a public interest action case by the People's Court shall not affect the victim of the same tortious act from initiating an action under Article 122 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-287"></a>第二百八十七条　对公益诉讼案件，当事人可以和解，人民法院可以调解。  
当事人达成和解或者调解协议后，人民法院应当将和解或者调解协议进行公告。公告期间不得少于三十日。  
公告期满后，人民法院经审查，和解或者调解协议不违反社会公共利益的，应当出具调解书；和解或者调解协议违反社会公共利益的，不予出具调解书，继续对案件进行审理并依法作出裁判。

**Article 287** In a public interest action case, the parties may reach a settlement, and the People's Court may conduct mediation.  
After the parties reach a settlement or mediation agreement, the People's Court shall make a public announcement of the settlement or mediation agreement. The period of announcement shall not be less than thirty days.  
After the announcement period expires, if upon examination the People's Court finds that the settlement or mediation agreement does not violate the social public interest, it shall issue a mediation document; if the settlement or mediation agreement violates the social public interest, it shall not issue a mediation document and shall continue to try the case and render a judgment according to law.

<a id="art-288"></a>第二百八十八条　公益诉讼案件的原告在法庭辩论终结后申请撤诉的，人民法院不予准许。

**Article 288** If the plaintiff in a public interest action case applies to withdraw the action after the conclusion of the court debate, the People's Court shall not grant permission.

<a id="art-289"></a>第二百八十九条　公益诉讼案件的裁判发生法律效力后，其他依法具有原告资格的机关和有关组织就同一侵权行为另行提起公益诉讼的，人民法院裁定不予受理，但法律、司法解释另有规定的除外。

**Article 289** After the judgment or ruling in a public interest action case becomes legally effective, if any other authority or relevant organization qualified as a plaintiff according to law brings a separate public interest action concerning the same tortious act, the People's Court shall rule that it shall not accept the case, unless otherwise provided by law or judicial interpretation.  
XIV. Action for Setting Aside by a Third Party

### 十四、第三人撤销之诉

<a id="art-290"></a>第二百九十条　第三人对已经发生法律效力的判决、裁定、调解书提起撤销之诉的，应当自知道或者应当知道其民事权益受到损害之日起六个月内，向作出生效判决、裁定、调解书的人民法院提出，并应当提供存在下列情形的证据材料：  
（一）因不能归责于本人的事由未参加诉讼；  
（二）发生法律效力的判决、裁定、调解书的全部或者部分内容错误；  
（三）发生法律效力的判决、裁定、调解书内容错误损害其民事权益。

**Article 290** Where a third party initiates an action to set aside a legally effective judgment, ruling, or mediation document, it shall, within six months from the date when it knew or should have known that its civil rights and interests were harmed, bring the action before the People's Court that rendered the effective judgment, ruling, or mediation document, and shall provide evidentiary materials showing the existence of the following circumstances:  
(1) it did not participate in the action due to reasons not attributable to itself;  
(2) all or part of the content of the legally effective judgment, ruling, or mediation document is erroneous; and  
(3) the erroneous content of the legally effective judgment, ruling, or mediation document harms its civil rights and interests.

<a id="art-291"></a>第二百九十一条　人民法院应当在收到起诉状和证据材料之日起五日内送交对方当事人，对方当事人可以自收到起诉状之日起十日内提出书面意见。  
人民法院应当对第三人提交的起诉状、证据材料以及对方当事人的书面意见进行审查。必要时，可以询问双方当事人。  
经审查，符合起诉条件的，人民法院应当在收到起诉状之日起三十日内立案。不符合起诉条件的，应当在收到起诉状之日起三十日内裁定不予受理。

**Article 291** The People's Court shall, within five days from the date of receiving the statement of complaint and evidentiary materials, serve them on the other party, and the other party may submit written opinions within ten days from the date of receiving the statement of complaint.  
The People's Court shall examine the statement of complaint and evidentiary materials submitted by the third party and the written opinions of the other party. When necessary, it may question both parties.  
Upon examination, if the conditions for prosecution are met, the People's Court shall docket the case within thirty days from the date of receiving the statement of complaint. If the conditions are not met, it shall, within thirty days from the date of receiving the statement of complaint, rule that the action shall not be accepted.

<a id="art-292"></a>第二百九十二条　人民法院对第三人撤销之诉案件，应当组成合议庭开庭审理。

**Article 292** In a case concerning an action for setting aside by a third party, the People's Court shall form a collegial panel and hear the case in open court.

<a id="art-293"></a>第二百九十三条　[民事诉讼法第五十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-59)第三款规定的因不能归责于本人的事由未参加诉讼，是指没有被列为生效判决、裁定、调解书当事人，且无过错或者无明显过错的情形。包括：  
（一）不知道诉讼而未参加的；  
（二）申请参加未获准许的；  
（三）知道诉讼，但因客观原因无法参加的；  
（四）因其他不能归责于本人的事由未参加诉讼的。

**Article 293** The phrase "did not participate in the action due to reasons not attributable to itself" as provided in Article 59, paragraph 3 of the Civil Procedure Law means the situation where the person was not listed as a party to the effective judgment, ruling, or mediation document and is not at fault or has no obvious fault. It includes:  
(1) not knowing of the action and thus not participating;  
(2) applying to participate but not being permitted;  
(3) knowing of the action but being unable to participate due to objective reasons; or  
(4) not participating due to other reasons not attributable to itself.

<a id="art-294"></a>第二百九十四条　[民事诉讼法第五十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-59)第三款规定的判决、裁定、调解书的部分或者全部内容，是指判决、裁定的主文，调解书中处理当事人民事权利义务的结果。

**Article 294** The phrase "all or part of the content" of a judgment, ruling, or mediation document as provided in Article 59, paragraph 3 of the Civil Procedure Law means the main text of the judgment or ruling, and the part of the mediation document that disposes of the parties' civil rights and obligations.

<a id="art-295"></a>第二百九十五条　对下列情形提起第三人撤销之诉的，人民法院不予受理：  
（一）适用特别程序、督促程序、公示催告程序、破产程序等非讼程序处理的案件；  
（二）婚姻无效、撤销或者解除婚姻关系等判决、裁定、调解书中涉及身份关系的内容；  
（三）[民事诉讼法第五十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-57)规定的未参加登记的权利人对代表人诉讼案件的生效裁判；  
（四）[民事诉讼法第五十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-58)规定的损害社会公共利益行为的受害人对公益诉讼案件的生效裁判。

**Article 295** The People's Court shall not accept an action for setting aside by a third party brought against any of the following circumstances:  
(1) a case handled under non-contentious procedures such as special procedures, supervision procedures, public summons procedures, or bankruptcy procedures;  
(2) the part of a judgment, ruling, or mediation document concerning invalidity of marriage, annulment of marriage, or dissolution of marital relationship, which involves status relations;  
(3) an effective judgment or ruling in a representative action case with respect to a right holder who did not register to participate as provided in Article 57 of the Civil Procedure Law; or  
(4) an effective judgment or ruling in a public interest action case with respect to a victim of conduct harming the social public interest as provided in Article 58 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-296"></a>第二百九十六条　第三人提起撤销之诉，人民法院应当将该第三人列为原告，生效判决、裁定、调解书的当事人列为被告，但生效判决、裁定、调解书中没有承担责任的无独立请求权的第三人列为第三人。

**Article 296** Where a third party initiates an action for setting aside, the People's Court shall list that third party as the plaintiff, the parties to the effective judgment, ruling, or mediation document as the defendants, except that a third party with no independent claim who bears no liability under the effective judgment, ruling, or mediation document shall be listed as a third party.

<a id="art-297"></a>第二百九十七条　受理第三人撤销之诉案件后，原告提供相应担保，请求中止执行的，人民法院可以准许。

**Article 297** After accepting a case concerning an action for setting aside by a third party, if the plaintiff provides appropriate security and requests suspension of enforcement, the People's Court may grant permission.

<a id="art-298"></a>第二百九十八条　对第三人撤销或者部分撤销发生法律效力的判决、裁定、调解书内容的请求，人民法院经审理，按下列情形分别处理：  
（一）请求成立且确认其民事权利的主张全部或部分成立的，改变原判决、裁定、调解书内容的错误部分；  
（二）请求成立，但确认其全部或部分民事权利的主张不成立，或者未提出确认其民事权利请求的，撤销原判决、裁定、调解书内容的错误部分；  
（三）请求不成立的，驳回诉讼请求。  
对前款规定裁判不服的，当事人可以上诉。  
原判决、裁定、调解书的内容未改变或者未撤销的部分继续有效。

**Article 298** With respect to a third party's request to set aside or partially set aside the content of a legally effective judgment, ruling, or mediation document, the People's Court shall, after trial, handle the matter according to the following circumstances separately:  
(1) if the request is established and the claim for confirmation of its civil rights is wholly or partially established, the erroneous part of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation document shall be altered;  
(2) if the request is established but the claim for confirmation of all or part of its civil rights is not established, or no request for confirmation of its civil rights is made, the erroneous part of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation document shall be set aside;  
(3) if the request is not established, the claim shall be dismissed.  
If a party is not satisfied with the judgment or ruling under the preceding paragraph, it may appeal.  
The part of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation document that is not altered or set aside shall remain effective.

<a id="art-299"></a>第二百九十九条　第三人撤销之诉案件审理期间，人民法院对生效判决、裁定、调解书裁定再审的，受理第三人撤销之诉的人民法院应当裁定将第三人的诉讼请求并入再审程序。但有证据证明原审当事人之间恶意串通损害第三人合法权益的，人民法院应当先行审理第三人撤销之诉案件，裁定中止再审诉讼。

**Article 299** During the trial of a case concerning an action for setting aside by a third party, if the People's Court rules to retry the effective judgment, ruling, or mediation document, the People's Court that accepted the action for setting aside shall rule to merge the third party's claims into the retrial procedure. However, if there is evidence showing that the parties in the original trial colluded in bad faith to harm the legitimate rights and interests of the third party, the People's Court shall first hear the action for setting aside case and rule to suspend the retrial.

<a id="art-300"></a>第三百条　第三人诉讼请求并入再审程序审理的，按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）按照第一审程序审理的，人民法院应当对第三人的诉讼请求一并审理，所作的判决可以上诉；  
（二）按照第二审程序审理的，人民法院可以调解，调解达不成协议的，应当裁定撤销原判决、裁定、调解书，发回一审法院重审，重审时应当列明第三人。

**Article 300** Where the third party's claims are merged into the retrial procedure for trial, they shall be handled according to the following circumstances separately:  
(1) if the retrial is conducted under the first instance procedure, the People's Court shall try the third party's claims together, and the judgment rendered may be appealed;  
(2) if the retrial is conducted under the second instance procedure, the People's Court may mediate; if mediation fails to reach an agreement, it shall rule to set aside the original judgment, ruling, or mediation document and remand the case to the first instance court for retrial, and the third party shall be listed in the retrial.

<a id="art-301"></a>第三百零一条　第三人提起撤销之诉后，未中止生效判决、裁定、调解书执行的，执行法院对第三人依照[民事诉讼法第二百三十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-234)规定提出的执行异议，应予审查。第三人不服驳回执行异议裁定，申请对原判决、裁定、调解书再审的，人民法院不予受理。  
案外人对人民法院驳回其执行异议裁定不服，认为原判决、裁定、调解书内容错误损害其合法权益的，应当根据[民事诉讼法第二百三十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-234)规定申请再审，提起第三人撤销之诉的，人民法院不予受理。

**Article 301** After a third party initiates an action for setting aside, if enforcement of the effective judgment, ruling, or mediation document is not suspended, the enforcing court shall examine a enforcement objection raised by the third party under Article 234 of the Civil Procedure Law. If the third party is not satisfied with the ruling dismissing the enforcement objection and applies for retrial of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation document, the People's Court shall not accept it.  
If a person not involved in the case is not satisfied with the ruling of the People's Court dismissing its enforcement objection and believes that the erroneous content of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation document harms its legitimate rights and interests, it shall apply for retrial under Article 234 of the Civil Procedure Law; if it initiates an action for setting aside by a third party, the People's Court shall not accept it.  
XV. Action on Enforcement Objection

### 十五、执行异议之诉

<a id="art-302"></a>第三百零二条　根据[民事诉讼法第二百三十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-234)规定，案外人、当事人对执行异议裁定不服，自裁定送达之日起十五日内向人民法院提起执行异议之诉的，由执行法院管辖。

**Article 302** Where, under Article 234 of the Civil Procedure Law, a person not involved in the case or a party is not satisfied with a ruling on an enforcement objection and initiates an action on enforcement objection before the People's Court within fifteen days from the date the ruling is served, the enforcing court shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-303"></a>第三百零三条　案外人提起执行异议之诉，除符合[民事诉讼法第一百二十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-122)规定外，还应当具备下列条件：  
（一）案外人的执行异议申请已经被人民法院裁定驳回；  
（二）有明确的排除对执行标的执行的诉讼请求，且诉讼请求与原判决、裁定无关；  
（三）自执行异议裁定送达之日起十五日内提起。  
人民法院应当在收到起诉状之日起十五日内决定是否立案。

**Article 303** In addition to meeting the requirements of Article 122 of the Civil Procedure Law, a person not involved in the case who initiates an action on enforcement objection shall also satisfy the following conditions:  
(1) the person's application for an enforcement objection has been dismissed by a ruling of the People's Court;  
(2) there is a definite claim to exclude enforcement of the subject matter of enforcement, and the claim is unrelated to the original judgment or ruling; and  
(3) the action is brought within fifteen days from the date the ruling on the enforcement objection is served.  
The People's Court shall decide whether to docket the case within fifteen days from the date of receiving the statement of complaint.

<a id="art-304"></a>第三百零四条　申请执行人提起执行异议之诉，除符合[民事诉讼法第一百二十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-122)规定外，还应当具备下列条件：  
（一）依案外人执行异议申请，人民法院裁定中止执行；  
（二）有明确的对执行标的继续执行的诉讼请求，且诉讼请求与原判决、裁定无关；  
（三）自执行异议裁定送达之日起十五日内提起。  
人民法院应当在收到起诉状之日起十五日内决定是否立案。

**Article 304** In addition to meeting the requirements of Article 122 of the Civil Procedure Law, an applicant for enforcement who initiates an action on enforcement objection shall also satisfy the following conditions:  
(1) the People's Court has ruled to suspend enforcement upon the application of the person not involved in the case for an enforcement objection;  
(2) there is a definite claim to continue enforcement of the subject matter of enforcement, and the claim is unrelated to the original judgment or ruling; and  
(3) the action is brought within fifteen days from the date the ruling on the enforcement objection is served.  
The People's Court shall decide whether to docket the case within fifteen days from the date of receiving the statement of complaint.

<a id="art-305"></a>第三百零五条　案外人提起执行异议之诉的，以申请执行人为被告。被执行人反对案外人异议的，被执行人为共同被告；被执行人不反对案外人异议的，可以列被执行人为第三人。

**Article 305** If a person not involved in the case initiates an action on enforcement objection, the applicant for enforcement shall be the defendant. If the person subject to enforcement opposes the objection of the person not involved in the case, the person subject to enforcement shall be a co-defendant; if the person subject to enforcement does not oppose the objection, the person subject to enforcement may be listed as a third party.

<a id="art-306"></a>第三百零六条　申请执行人提起执行异议之诉的，以案外人为被告。被执行人反对申请执行人主张的，以案外人和被执行人为共同被告；被执行人不反对申请执行人主张的，可以列被执行人为第三人。

**Article 306** If the applicant for enforcement initiates an action on enforcement objection, the person not involved in the case shall be the defendant. If the person subject to enforcement opposes the claim of the applicant for enforcement, the person not involved in the case and the person subject to enforcement shall be co-defendants; if the person subject to enforcement does not oppose the claim, the person subject to enforcement may be listed as a third party.

<a id="art-307"></a>第三百零七条　申请执行人对中止执行裁定未提起执行异议之诉，被执行人提起执行异议之诉的，人民法院告知其另行起诉。

**Article 307** If the applicant for enforcement does not initiate an action on enforcement objection against the ruling suspending enforcement, and the person subject to enforcement initiates an action on enforcement objection, the People's Court shall inform them to file a separate action.

<a id="art-308"></a>第三百零八条　人民法院审理执行异议之诉案件，适用普通程序。

**Article 308** The People's Court shall apply ordinary procedure to hear cases concerning actions on enforcement objection.

<a id="art-309"></a>第三百零九条　案外人或者申请执行人提起执行异议之诉的，案外人应当就其对执行标的享有足以排除强制执行的民事权益承担举证证明责任。

**Article 309** Where a person not involved in the case or the applicant for enforcement initiates an action on enforcement objection, the person not involved in the case shall bear the burden of proof to establish that it has a civil right or interest in the subject matter of enforcement sufficient to preclude compulsory enforcement.

<a id="art-310"></a>第三百一十条　对案外人提起的执行异议之诉，人民法院经审理，按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）案外人就执行标的享有足以排除强制执行的民事权益的，判决不得执行该执行标的；  
（二）案外人就执行标的不享有足以排除强制执行的民事权益的，判决驳回诉讼请求。  
案外人同时提出确认其权利的诉讼请求的，人民法院可以在判决中一并作出裁判。

**Article 310** With respect to an action on enforcement objection initiated by a person not involved in the case, the People's Court shall, after trial, handle the matter according to the following circumstances separately:  
(1) if the person not involved in the case has a civil right or interest in the subject matter of enforcement sufficient to preclude compulsory enforcement, it shall be adjudged that the subject matter of enforcement shall not be enforced;  
(2) if the person not involved in the case does not have such a right or interest, the claim shall be dismissed.  
If the person not involved in the case also makes a claim for confirmation of its rights, the People's Court may make a judgment on that claim in the same judgment.

<a id="art-311"></a>第三百一十一条　对申请执行人提起的执行异议之诉，人民法院经审理，按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）案外人就执行标的不享有足以排除强制执行的民事权益的，判决准许执行该执行标的；  
（二）案外人就执行标的享有足以排除强制执行的民事权益的，判决驳回诉讼请求。

**Article 311** With respect to an action on enforcement objection initiated by the applicant for enforcement, the People's Court shall, after trial, handle the matter according to the following circumstances separately:  
(1) if the person not involved in the case does not have a civil right or interest in the subject matter of enforcement sufficient to preclude compulsory enforcement, it shall be adjudged that enforcement of the subject matter of enforcement is permitted;  
(2) if the person not involved in the case has such a right or interest, the claim shall be dismissed.

<a id="art-312"></a>第三百一十二条　对案外人执行异议之诉，人民法院判决不得对执行标的执行的，执行异议裁定失效。  
对申请执行人执行异议之诉，人民法院判决准许对该执行标的执行的，执行异议裁定失效，执行法院可以根据申请执行人的申请或者依职权恢复执行。

**Article 312** If, in an action on enforcement objection by a person not involved in the case, the People's Court adjudges that the subject matter of enforcement shall not be enforced, the ruling on the enforcement objection shall cease to have effect.  
If, in an action on enforcement objection by the applicant for enforcement, the People's Court adjudges that enforcement of the subject matter of enforcement is permitted, the ruling on the enforcement objection shall cease to have effect, and the enforcing court may resume enforcement upon the application of the applicant for enforcement or ex officio.

<a id="art-313"></a>第三百一十三条　案外人执行异议之诉审理期间，人民法院不得对执行标的进行处分。申请执行人请求人民法院继续执行并提供相应担保的，人民法院可以准许。  
被执行人与案外人恶意串通，通过执行异议、执行异议之诉妨害执行的，人民法院应当依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-116)规定处理。申请执行人因此受到损害的，可以提起诉讼要求被执行人、案外人赔偿。

**Article 313** During the trial of an action on enforcement objection by a person not involved in the case, the People's Court shall not dispose of the subject matter of enforcement. If the applicant for enforcement requests the People's Court to continue enforcement and provides appropriate security, the People's Court may grant permission.  
If the person subject to enforcement and the person not involved in the case collude in bad faith to obstruct enforcement through an enforcement objection or an action on enforcement objection, the People's Court shall handle the matter in accordance with Article 116 of the Civil Procedure Law. If the applicant for enforcement suffers damage as a result, it may initiate an action to claim compensation from the person subject to enforcement and the person not involved in the case.

<a id="art-314"></a>第三百一十四条　人民法院对执行标的裁定中止执行后，申请执行人在法律规定的期间内未提起执行异议之诉的，人民法院应当自起诉期限届满之日起七日内解除对该执行标的采取的执行措施。

**Article 314** After the People's Court rules to suspend enforcement of the subject matter of enforcement, if the applicant for enforcement does not initiate an action on enforcement objection within the period prescribed by law, the People's Court shall lift the enforcement measures taken against the subject matter of enforcement within seven days from the date the period for prosecution expires.  
XVI. Second Instance Procedure

### 十六、第二审程序

<a id="art-315"></a>第三百一十五条　双方当事人和第三人都提起上诉的，均列为上诉人。人民法院可以依职权确定第二审程序中当事人的诉讼地位。

**Article 315** If both parties and a third party all file an appeal, they shall all be listed as appellants. The People's Court may ex officio determine the procedural status of the parties in the second instance procedure.

<a id="art-316"></a>第三百一十六条　[民事诉讼法第一百七十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-173)、第一百七十四条规定的对方当事人包括被上诉人和原审其他当事人。

**Article 316** The "other party" as provided in Articles 173 and 174 of the Civil Procedure Law includes the respondent and other parties in the original trial.

<a id="art-317"></a>第三百一十七条　必要共同诉讼人的一人或者部分人提起上诉的，按下列情形分别处理：  
（一）上诉仅对与对方当事人之间权利义务分担有意见，不涉及其他共同诉讼人利益的，对方当事人为被上诉人，未上诉的同一方当事人依原审诉讼地位列明；  
（二）上诉仅对共同诉讼人之间权利义务分担有意见，不涉及对方当事人利益的，未上诉的同一方当事人为被上诉人，对方当事人依原审诉讼地位列明；  
（三）上诉对双方当事人之间以及共同诉讼人之间权利义务承担有意见的，未提起上诉的其他当事人均为被上诉人。

**Article 317** Where one or some of the necessary co-litigants file an appeal, the matter shall be handled according to the following circumstances separately:  
(1) if the appeal only concerns the apportionment of rights and obligations between the appellant and the other party, and does not involve the interests of other co-litigants, the other party shall be the respondent, and the same-side parties who did not appeal shall be listed according to their procedural status in the original trial;  
(2) if the appeal only concerns the apportionment of rights and obligations among co-litigants, and does not involve the interests of the other party, the same-side parties who did not appeal shall be the respondents, and the other party shall be listed according to its procedural status in the original trial;  
(3) if the appeal concerns the apportionment of rights and obligations between both parties and among co-litigants, all other parties who did not file an appeal shall be respondents.

<a id="art-318"></a>第三百一十八条　一审宣判时或者判决书、裁定书送达时，当事人口头表示上诉的，人民法院应告知其必须在法定上诉期间内递交上诉状。未在法定上诉期间内递交上诉状的，视为未提起上诉。虽递交上诉状，但未在指定的期限内交纳上诉费的，按自动撤回上诉处理。

**Article 318** If a party orally expresses an intention to appeal at the time of pronouncement of the first instance judgment or at the time of service of the judgment or ruling, the People's Court shall inform the party that it must submit a written notice of appeal within the statutory period for appeal. If no written notice of appeal is submitted within the statutory period, it shall be deemed that no appeal was filed. If a written notice of appeal is submitted but the appeal fee is not paid within the specified period, the appeal shall be treated as having been withdrawn automatically.

<a id="art-319"></a>第三百一十九条　无民事行为能力人、限制民事行为能力人的法定代理人，可以代理当事人提起上诉。

**Article 319** The legal representative of a person with no capacity for civil conduct or with limited capacity for civil conduct may represent the party in filing an appeal.

<a id="art-320"></a>第三百二十条　上诉案件的当事人死亡或者终止的，人民法院依法通知其权利义务承继者参加诉讼。  
需要终结诉讼的，适用[民事诉讼法第一百五十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-154)规定。

**Article 320** If a party to an appeal case dies or terminates, the People's Court shall, according to law, notify the successor of its rights and obligations to participate in the action.  
If it is necessary to terminate the action, the provisions of Article 154 of the Civil Procedure Law shall apply.

<a id="art-321"></a>第三百二十一条　第二审人民法院应当围绕当事人的上诉请求进行审理。  
当事人没有提出请求的，不予审理，但一审判决违反法律禁止性规定，或者损害国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益的除外。

**Article 321** The People's Court of the second instance shall conduct the trial centering on the claims of the parties on appeal.  
Matters not raised by the parties shall not be tried, except where the first instance judgment violates a prohibitive provision of law or harms the national interest, the social public interest, or the legitimate rights and interests of others.

<a id="art-322"></a>第三百二十二条　开庭审理的上诉案件，第二审人民法院可以依照[民事诉讼法第一百三十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-136)第四项规定进行审理前的准备。

**Article 322** For an appeal case to be heard in open court, the People's Court of the second instance may, in accordance with Article 136, item (4) of the Civil Procedure Law, make preparations before trial.

<a id="art-323"></a>第三百二十三条　下列情形，可以认定为[民事诉讼法第一百七十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-177)第一款第四项规定的严重违反法定程序：  
（一）审判组织的组成不合法的；  
（二）应当回避的审判人员未回避的；  
（三）无诉讼行为能力人未经法定代理人代为诉讼的；  
（四）违法剥夺当事人辩论权利的。

**Article 323** The following circumstances may be identified as a serious violation of statutory procedure as provided in Article 177, paragraph 1, item (4) of the Civil Procedure Law:  
(1) the composition of the adjudicatory body is unlawful;  
(2) a judicial officer who should have withdrawn did not withdraw;  
(3) a person with no capacity for litigation was not represented by a legal representative in the action; or  
(4) a party's right to debate was illegally deprived.

<a id="art-324"></a>第三百二十四条　对当事人在第一审程序中已经提出的诉讼请求，原审人民法院未作审理、判决的，第二审人民法院可以根据当事人自愿的原则进行调解；调解不成的，发回重审。

**Article 324** If a claim that has been raised by a party in the first instance procedure was not tried or adjudged by the People's Court of the original trial, the People's Court of the second instance may conduct mediation on the basis of the voluntary principle; if mediation fails, the case shall be remanded for retrial.

<a id="art-325"></a>第三百二十五条　必须参加诉讼的当事人或者有独立请求权的第三人，在第一审程序中未参加诉讼，第二审人民法院可以根据当事人自愿的原则予以调解；调解不成的，发回重审。

**Article 325** If a party that must participate in the action or a third party with independent claims did not participate in the action in the first instance procedure, the People's Court of the second instance may conduct mediation on the basis of the voluntary principle; if mediation fails, the case shall be remanded for retrial.

<a id="art-326"></a>第三百二十六条　在第二审程序中，原审原告增加独立的诉讼请求或者原审被告提出反诉的，第二审人民法院可以根据当事人自愿的原则就新增加的诉讼请求或者反诉进行调解；调解不成的，告知当事人另行起诉。  
双方当事人同意由第二审人民法院一并审理的，第二审人民法院可以一并裁判。

**Article 326** In the second instance procedure, if the original plaintiff adds an independent claim or the original defendant raises a counterclaim, the People's Court of the second instance may conduct mediation on the newly added claim or counterclaim on the basis of the voluntary principle; if mediation fails, the parties shall be informed to file a separate action.  
If both parties agree to have the People's Court of the second instance try the matter together, the People's Court of the second instance may adjudicate the matter together.

<a id="art-327"></a>第三百二十七条　一审判决不准离婚的案件，上诉后，第二审人民法院认为应当判决离婚的，可以根据当事人自愿的原则，与子女抚养、财产问题一并调解；调解不成的，发回重审。  
双方当事人同意由第二审人民法院一并审理的，第二审人民法院可以一并裁判。

**Article 327** In a case where the first instance judgment did not grant divorce, if after appeal, the People's Court of the second instance believes that divorce should be granted, it may conduct mediation on the basis of the voluntary principle regarding child custody and property issues together with the divorce; if mediation fails, the case shall be remanded for retrial.  
If both parties agree to have the People's Court of the second instance try the matter together, the People's Court of the second instance may adjudicate the matter together.

<a id="art-328"></a>第三百二十八条　人民法院依照第二审程序审理案件，认为依法不应由人民法院受理的，可以由第二审人民法院直接裁定撤销原裁判，驳回起诉。

**Article 328** Where a People's Court trying a case under the second instance procedure believes that the case should not have been accepted by the People's Court according to law, the People's Court of the second instance may directly rule to set aside the original judgment or ruling and dismiss the action.

<a id="art-329"></a>第三百二十九条　人民法院依照第二审程序审理案件，认为第一审人民法院受理案件违反专属管辖规定的，应当裁定撤销原裁判并移送有管辖权的人民法院。

**Article 329** Where a People's Court trying a case under the second instance procedure believes that the acceptance of the case by the People's Court of the first instance violates the provisions on exclusive jurisdiction, it shall rule to set aside the original judgment or ruling and transfer the case to the People's Court with jurisdiction.

<a id="art-330"></a>第三百三十条　第二审人民法院查明第一审人民法院作出的不予受理裁定有错误的，应当在撤销原裁定的同时，指令第一审人民法院立案受理；查明第一审人民法院作出的驳回起诉裁定有错误的，应当在撤销原裁定的同时，指令第一审人民法院审理。

**Article 330** If the People's Court of the second instance finds that the ruling on non-acceptance made by the People's Court of the first instance is erroneous, it shall, while setting aside the original ruling, instruct the People's Court of the first instance to docket and accept the case; if it finds that the ruling on dismissal of the action made by the People's Court of the first instance is erroneous, it shall, while setting aside the original ruling, instruct the People's Court of the first instance to try the case.

<a id="art-331"></a>第三百三十一条　第二审人民法院对下列上诉案件，依照[民事诉讼法第一百七十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-176)规定可以不开庭审理：  
（一）不服不予受理、管辖权异议和驳回起诉裁定的；  
（二）当事人提出的上诉请求明显不能成立的；  
（三）原判决、裁定认定事实清楚，但适用法律错误的；  
（四）原判决严重违反法定程序，需要发回重审的。

**Article 331** The People's Court of the second instance may, in accordance with Article 176 of the Civil Procedure Law, hear the following appeal cases without holding a hearing:  
(1) appeals against rulings on non-acceptance, jurisdictional objection, or dismissal of the action;  
(2) the appeal claims raised by the parties are obviously unsustainable;  
(3) the facts found in the original judgment or ruling are clear, but the application of law is erroneous; or  
(4) the original judgment seriously violates statutory procedure and needs to be remanded for retrial.

<a id="art-332"></a>第三百三十二条　原判决、裁定认定事实或者适用法律虽有瑕疵，但裁判结果正确的，第二审人民法院可以在判决、裁定中纠正瑕疵后，依照[民事诉讼法第一百七十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-177)第一款第一项规定予以维持。

**Article 332** If there is a defect in the findings of fact or application of law in the original judgment or ruling, but the result of the judgment or ruling is correct, the People's Court of the second instance may, after correcting the defect in its judgment or ruling, affirm it in accordance with Article 177, paragraph 1, item (1) of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-333"></a>第三百三十三条　[民事诉讼法第一百七十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-177)第一款第三项规定的基本事实，是指用以确定当事人主体资格、案件性质、民事权利义务等对原判决、裁定的结果有实质性影响的事实。

**Article 333** The basic facts as provided in Article 177, paragraph 1, item (3) of the Civil Procedure Law refer to facts used to determine the parties' qualifications, the nature of the case, civil rights and obligations, and other facts that have a substantial impact on the result of the original judgment or ruling.

<a id="art-334"></a>第三百三十四条　在第二审程序中，作为当事人的法人或者其他组织分立的，人民法院可以直接将分立后的法人或者其他组织列为共同诉讼人；合并的，将合并后的法人或者其他组织列为当事人。

**Article 334** In the second instance procedure, if a legal person or other organization that is a party is divided, the People's Court may directly list the divided legal persons or other organizations as co-litigants; if merged, the merged legal person or other organization shall be listed as the party.

<a id="art-335"></a>第三百三十五条　在第二审程序中，当事人申请撤回上诉，人民法院经审查认为一审判决确有错误，或者当事人之间恶意串通损害国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益的，不应准许。

**Article 335** In the second instance procedure, if a party applies to withdraw the appeal, and the People's Court, upon examination, finds that the first instance judgment is indeed erroneous, or that the parties have colluded in bad faith harming the national interest, the social public interest, or the legitimate rights and interests of others, permission shall not be granted.

<a id="art-336"></a>第三百三十六条　在第二审程序中，原审原告申请撤回起诉，经其他当事人同意，且不损害国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益的，人民法院可以准许。准许撤诉的，应当一并裁定撤销一审裁判。  
原审原告在第二审程序中撤回起诉后重复起诉的，人民法院不予受理。

**Article 336** In the second instance procedure, if the original plaintiff applies to withdraw the action, and with the consent of the other parties and without harming the national interest, the social public interest, or the legitimate rights and interests of others, the People's Court may grant permission. If withdrawal is permitted, the People's Court shall also rule to set aside the first instance judgment or ruling.  
If the original plaintiff, after withdrawing the action in the second instance procedure, brings a duplicate action, the People's Court shall not accept it.

<a id="art-337"></a>第三百三十七条　当事人在第二审程序中达成和解协议的，人民法院可以根据当事人的请求，对双方达成的和解协议进行审查并制作调解书送达当事人；因和解而申请撤诉，经审查符合撤诉条件的，人民法院应予准许。

**Article 337** If the parties reach a settlement agreement in the second instance proceedings, the People's Court may, upon the request of the parties, review the settlement agreement and prepare a mediation statement to serve on the parties; if a party applies for withdrawal of the action due to settlement, and upon review the conditions for withdrawal are satisfied, the People's Court shall grant permission.

<a id="art-338"></a>第三百三十八条　第二审人民法院宣告判决可以自行宣判，也可以委托原审人民法院或者当事人所在地人民法院代行宣判。

**Article 338** The second instance People's Court may pronounce judgment by itself, or may entrust the original trial People's Court or the People's Court of the place where the party is located to pronounce judgment on its behalf.

<a id="art-339"></a>第三百三十九条　人民法院审理对裁定的上诉案件，应当在第二审立案之日起三十日内作出终审裁定。有特殊情况需要延长审限的，由本院院长批准。

**Article 339** When the People's Court hears an appeal against a ruling, it shall make a final ruling within thirty days from the date of filing the appeal for second instance. If there are special circumstances requiring an extension of the trial period, approval shall be obtained from the president of the court.

<a id="art-340"></a>第三百四十条　当事人在第一审程序中实施的诉讼行为，在第二审程序中对该当事人仍具有拘束力。  
当事人推翻其在第一审程序中实施的诉讼行为时，人民法院应当责令其说明理由。理由不成立的，不予支持。

**Article 340** Litigation acts performed by a party in the first instance proceedings shall remain binding on that party in the second instance proceedings.  
When a party repudiates the litigation acts it performed in the first instance proceedings, the People's Court shall order it to explain the reasons. If the reasons are not established, the repudiation shall not be supported.  
XVII. Special Procedure

### 十七、特别程序

<a id="art-341"></a>第三百四十一条　宣告失踪或者宣告死亡案件，人民法院可以根据申请人的请求，清理下落不明人的财产，并指定案件审理期间的财产管理人。公告期满后，人民法院判决宣告失踪的，应当同时依照[民法典第四十二条](../../民事与商事/民法典/中华人民共和国民法典.md#art-42)的规定指定失踪人的财产代管人。

**Article 341** In a case concerning declaration of disappearance or declaration of death, the People's Court may, upon the request of the applicant, liquidate the property of the missing person and appoint a property administrator during the case hearing period. After the expiration of the public notice period, if the People's Court renders a judgment declaring the person missing, it shall simultaneously appoint a property custodian for the missing person in accordance with Article 42 of the Civil Code of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-342"></a>第三百四十二条　失踪人的财产代管人经人民法院指定后，代管人申请变更代管的，比照民事诉讼法特别程序的有关规定进行审理。申请理由成立的，裁定撤销申请人的代管人身份，同时另行指定财产代管人；申请理由不成立的，裁定驳回申请。  
失踪人的其他利害关系人申请变更代管的，人民法院应当告知其以原指定的代管人为被告起诉，并按普通程序进行审理。

**Article 342** After a property custodian for a missing person is appointed by the People's Court, if the custodian applies for a change of custodian, the case shall be heard by reference to the relevant provisions of the special procedure of the Civil Procedure Law. If the reasons for the application are established, a ruling shall be made to revoke the custodian status of the applicant and at the same time appoint another property custodian; if the reasons for the application are not established, a ruling shall be made to dismiss the application.  
If any other interested person of the missing person applies for a change of custodian, the People's Court shall inform such person to file an action against the originally appointed custodian as the defendant and hear the case under the ordinary procedure.

<a id="art-343"></a>第三百四十三条　人民法院判决宣告公民失踪后，利害关系人向人民法院申请宣告失踪人死亡，自失踪之日起满四年的，人民法院应当受理，宣告失踪的判决即是该公民失踪的证明，审理中仍应依照[民事诉讼法第一百九十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-192)规定进行公告。

**Article 343** After the People's Court renders a judgment declaring a citizen missing, if an interested person applies to the People's Court for a declaration of death of the missing person, and four years have elapsed since the date of disappearance, the People's Court shall accept the case, and the judgment declaring the person missing shall serve as proof of the person's disappearance; during the hearing, a public notice shall still be issued in accordance with Article 192 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-344"></a>第三百四十四条　符合法律规定的多个利害关系人提出宣告失踪、宣告死亡申请的，列为共同申请人。

**Article 344** If multiple interested persons who meet the legal requirements file applications for declaration of disappearance or declaration of death, they shall be listed as joint applicants.

<a id="art-345"></a>第三百四十五条　寻找下落不明人的公告应当记载下列内容：  
（一）被申请人应当在规定期间内向受理法院申报其具体地址及其联系方式。否则，被申请人将被宣告失踪、宣告死亡；  
（二）凡知悉被申请人生存现状的人，应当在公告期间内将其所知道情况向受理法院报告。

**Article 345** The public notice for locating the missing person shall include the following:  
(1) The respondent shall report his or her specific address and contact information to the accepting court within the prescribed period. Otherwise, the respondent shall be declared missing or declared dead;  
(2) Any person who knows the current living status of the respondent shall report the information known to the accepting court within the period of the public notice.

<a id="art-346"></a>第三百四十六条　人民法院受理宣告失踪、宣告死亡案件后，作出判决前，申请人撤回申请的，人民法院应当裁定终结案件，但其他符合法律规定的利害关系人加入程序要求继续审理的除外。

**Article 346** After the People's Court accepts a case of declaration of disappearance or declaration of death, and before judgment is rendered, if the applicant withdraws the application, the People's Court shall make a ruling to terminate the case, unless other interested persons who meet the legal requirements join the procedure and request to continue the hearing.

<a id="art-347"></a>第三百四十七条　在诉讼中，当事人的利害关系人或者有关组织提出该当事人不能辨认或者不能完全辨认自己的行为，要求宣告该当事人无民事行为能力或者限制民事行为能力的，应由利害关系人或者有关组织向人民法院提出申请，由受诉人民法院按照特别程序立案审理，原诉讼中止。

**Article 347** During litigation, if an interested person of a party or a relevant organization claims that the party cannot or cannot fully recognize his or her own conduct and requests a declaration that the party has no or limited capacity for civil conduct, the interested person or the relevant organization shall file an application with the People's Court, and the court hearing the original action shall file the case and hear it under the special procedure, and the original action shall be suspended.

<a id="art-348"></a>第三百四十八条　认定财产无主案件，公告期间有人对财产提出请求的，人民法院应当裁定终结特别程序，告知申请人另行起诉，适用普通程序审理。

**Article 348** In a case for confirmation of ownerless property, if during the period of public notice a person makes a claim to the property, the People's Court shall make a ruling to terminate the special procedure, inform the applicant to file a separate action, and hear the case under the ordinary procedure.

<a id="art-349"></a>第三百四十九条　被指定的监护人不服居民委员会、村民委员会或者民政部门指定，应当自接到通知之日起三十日内向人民法院提出异议。经审理，认为指定并无不当的，裁定驳回异议；指定不当的，判决撤销指定，同时另行指定监护人。判决书应当送达异议人、原指定单位及判决指定的监护人。  
有关当事人依照[民法典第三十一条](../../民事与商事/民法典/中华人民共和国民法典.md#art-31)第一款规定直接向人民法院申请指定监护人的，适用特别程序审理，判决指定监护人。判决书应当送达申请人、判决指定的监护人。

**Article 349** If the appointed guardian disagrees with the appointment by the residents' committee, villagers' committee, or civil affairs department, the guardian shall raise an objection with the People's Court within thirty days from receipt of the notice. Upon hearing, if the appointment is found to be proper, a ruling shall be made to dismiss the objection; if the appointment is improper, a judgment shall be made to revoke the appointment and at the same time appoint another guardian. The judgment shall be served on the objector, the original appointing entity, and the guardian appointed by the judgment.  
If a relevant party directly applies to the People's Court for appointment of a guardian in accordance with Article 31(1) of the Civil Code of the People's Republic of China, the case shall be heard under the special procedure, and a judgment shall be rendered appointing a guardian. The judgment shall be served on the applicant and the guardian appointed by the judgment.

<a id="art-350"></a>第三百五十条　申请认定公民无民事行为能力或者限制民事行为能力的案件，被申请人没有近亲属的，人民法院可以指定经被申请人住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会或者民政部门同意，且愿意担任代理人的个人或者组织为代理人。  
没有前款规定的代理人的，由被申请人住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会或者民政部门担任代理人。  
代理人可以是一人，也可以是同一顺序中的两人。

**Article 350** In a case where an application is made to declare a citizen to have no or limited capacity for civil conduct, if the respondent has no close relatives, the People's Court may appoint an individual or organization that has obtained the consent of the residents' committee, villagers' committee, or civil affairs department of the respondent's domicile and is willing to act as agent as the agent.  
If there is no agent as prescribed in the preceding paragraph, the residents' committee, villagers' committee, or civil affairs department of the respondent's domicile shall act as agent.  
The agent may be one person, or two persons in the same order.

<a id="art-351"></a>第三百五十一条　申请司法确认调解协议的，双方当事人应当本人或者由符合[民事诉讼法第六十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-61)规定的代理人依照[民事诉讼法第二百零一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-201)的规定提出申请。

**Article 351** Where an application is made for judicial confirmation of a mediation agreement, both parties shall file the application in person or through an agent who meets the requirements of Article 61 of the Civil Procedure Law, in accordance with Article 201 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-352"></a>第三百五十二条　调解组织自行开展的调解，有两个以上调解组织参与的，符合[民事诉讼法第二百零一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-201)规定的各调解组织所在地人民法院均有管辖权。  
双方当事人可以共同向符合[民事诉讼法第二百零一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-201)规定的其中一个有管辖权的人民法院提出申请；双方当事人共同向两个以上有管辖权的人民法院提出申请的，由最先立案的人民法院管辖。

**Article 352** If mediation is conducted by mediation organizations on their own and more than two mediation organizations participate, the People's Court at the place where each mediation organization that meets the requirements of Article 201 of the Civil Procedure Law is located shall have jurisdiction.  
Both parties may jointly file an application with any one of the People's Courts that have jurisdiction and meet the requirements of Article 201 of the Civil Procedure Law; if both parties jointly file applications with two or more People's Courts that have jurisdiction, the People's Court that first files the case shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-353"></a>第三百五十三条　当事人申请司法确认调解协议，可以采用书面形式或者口头形式。当事人口头申请的，人民法院应当记入笔录，并由当事人签名、捺印或者盖章。

**Article 353** A party's application for judicial confirmation of a mediation agreement may be made in writing or orally. If a party makes an oral application, the People's Court shall record it in the record, and the party shall sign, affix fingerprint, or seal it.

<a id="art-354"></a>第三百五十四条　当事人申请司法确认调解协议，应当向人民法院提交调解协议、调解组织主持调解的证明，以及与调解协议相关的财产权利证明等材料，并提供双方当事人的身份、住所、联系方式等基本信息。  
当事人未提交上述材料的，人民法院应当要求当事人限期补交。

**Article 354** A party applying for judicial confirmation of a mediation agreement shall submit to the People's Court the mediation agreement, proof that the mediation was conducted under the auspices of a mediation organization, and materials such as property rights certificates related to the mediation agreement, and shall provide basic information including the identity, domicile, and contact information of both parties.  
If the party fails to submit the above materials, the People's Court shall order the party to submit them within a specified period.

<a id="art-355"></a>第三百五十五条　当事人申请司法确认调解协议，有下列情形之一的，人民法院裁定不予受理：  
（一）不属于人民法院受理范围的；  
（二）不属于收到申请的人民法院管辖的；  
（三）申请确认婚姻关系、亲子关系、收养关系等身份关系无效、有效或者解除的；  
（四）涉及适用其他特别程序、公示催告程序、破产程序审理的；  
（五）调解协议内容涉及物权、知识产权确权的。  
人民法院受理申请后，发现有上述不予受理情形的，应当裁定驳回当事人的申请。

**Article 355** Where a party applies for judicial confirmation of a mediation agreement, if any of the following circumstances exists, the People's Court shall make a ruling not to accept the application:  
(1) It does not fall within the scope of acceptance by the People's Court;  
(2) It does not fall within the jurisdiction of the People's Court receiving the application;  
(3) The application seeks confirmation of invalidity, validity, or dissolution of status relationships such as marital relationship, parent-child relationship, or adoptive relationship;  
(4) It involves cases to be heard under other special procedures, public summons procedure, or bankruptcy procedure;  
(5) The content of the mediation agreement involves confirmation of real rights or intellectual property rights.  
After accepting the application, the People's Court finds that there is any of the above circumstances for non-acceptance, it shall make a ruling to dismiss the party's application.

<a id="art-356"></a>第三百五十六条　人民法院审查相关情况时，应当通知双方当事人共同到场对案件进行核实。  
人民法院经审查，认为当事人的陈述或者提供的证明材料不充分、不完备或者有疑义的，可以要求当事人限期补充陈述或者补充证明材料。必要时，人民法院可以向调解组织核实有关情况。

**Article 356** When the People's Court examines the relevant circumstances, it shall notify both parties to appear together to verify the case.  
If upon examination, the People's Court considers that the statements of the parties or the evidentiary materials provided are insufficient, incomplete, or doubtful, it may require the parties to supplement statements or supplementary evidentiary materials within a prescribed period. When necessary, the People's Court may verify the relevant circumstances with the mediation organization.

<a id="art-357"></a>第三百五十七条　确认调解协议的裁定作出前，当事人撤回申请的，人民法院可以裁定准许。  
当事人无正当理由未在限期内补充陈述、补充证明材料或者拒不接受询问的，人民法院可以按撤回申请处理。

**Article 357** Before a ruling confirming a mediation agreement is made, if a party withdraws the application, the People's Court may make a ruling to grant permission.  
If a party fails, without justifiable reasons, to supplement statements or evidentiary materials within the prescribed period, or refuses to accept questioning, the People's Court may treat the application as withdrawn.

<a id="art-358"></a>第三百五十八条　经审查，调解协议有下列情形之一的，人民法院应当裁定驳回申请：  
（一）违反法律强制性规定的；  
（二）损害国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益的；  
（三）违背公序良俗的；  
（四）违反自愿原则的；  
（五）内容不明确的；  
（六）其他不能进行司法确认的情形。

**Article 358** Upon examination, if the mediation agreement has any of the following circumstances, the People's Court shall make a ruling to dismiss the application:  
(1) It violates mandatory provisions of laws;  
(2) It harms national interests, public interests, or the lawful rights and interests of others;  
(3) It violates public order and good customs;  
(4) It violates the principle of voluntariness;  
(5) Its content is unclear;  
(6) Other circumstances where judicial confirmation cannot be made.

<a id="art-359"></a>第三百五十九条　[民事诉讼法第二百零三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-203)规定的担保物权人，包括抵押权人、质权人、留置权人；其他有权请求实现担保物权的人，包括抵押人、出质人、财产被留置的债务人或者所有权人等。

**Article 359** The security interest holders as referred to in Article 203 of the Civil Procedure Law include mortgagees, pledgees, and lien holders; other persons entitled to request the realization of security interests include mortgagors, pledgors, debtors whose property is subject to lien, or owners, among others.

<a id="art-360"></a>第三百六十条　实现票据、仓单、提单等有权利凭证的权利质权案件，可以由权利凭证持有人住所地人民法院管辖；无权利凭证的权利质权，由出质登记地人民法院管辖。

**Article 360** Cases concerning the realization of a pledge of rights that have certificates of rights, such as negotiable instruments, warehouse receipts, or bills of lading, may be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the domicile of the holder of the certificate of rights; for a pledge of rights without a certificate of rights, the People's Court at the place of registration of the pledge shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-361"></a>第三百六十一条　实现担保物权案件属于海事法院等专门人民法院管辖的，由专门人民法院管辖。

**Article 361** If a case concerning the realization of a security interest falls within the jurisdiction of a special People's Court such as a maritime court, it shall be under the jurisdiction of the special People's Court.

<a id="art-362"></a>第三百六十二条　同一债权的担保物有多个且所在地不同，申请人分别向有管辖权的人民法院申请实现担保物权的，人民法院应当依法受理。

**Article 362** If there are multiple security properties for the same claim and their locations are different, and the applicant separately files applications for realization of security interests with the People's Courts that have jurisdiction, the People's Courts shall accept them according to law.

<a id="art-363"></a>第三百六十三条　依照[民法典第三百九十二条](../../民事与商事/民法典/中华人民共和国民法典.md#art-392)的规定，被担保的债权既有物的担保又有人的担保，当事人对实现担保物权的顺序有约定，实现担保物权的申请违反该约定的，人民法院裁定不予受理；没有约定或者约定不明的，人民法院应当受理。

**Article 363** In accordance with Article 392 of the Civil Code of the People's Republic of China, if the secured claim is secured by both property guarantee and personal guarantee, and the parties have agreed on the order of realizing the security interests, if the application for realization of security interests violates such agreement, the People's Court shall make a ruling not to accept it; if there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, the People's Court shall accept the application.

<a id="art-364"></a>第三百六十四条　同一财产上设立多个担保物权，登记在先的担保物权尚未实现的，不影响后顺位的担保物权人向人民法院申请实现担保物权。

**Article 364** If multiple security interests are established on the same property, and the security interest registered first has not yet been realized, this shall not affect the security interest holder in a later rank from applying to the People's Court for realization of the security interest.

<a id="art-365"></a>第三百六十五条　申请实现担保物权，应当提交下列材料：  
（一）申请书。申请书应当记明申请人、被申请人的姓名或者名称、联系方式等基本信息，具体的请求和事实、理由；  
（二）证明担保物权存在的材料，包括主合同、担保合同、抵押登记证明或者他项权利证书，权利质权的权利凭证或者质权出质登记证明等；  
（三）证明实现担保物权条件成就的材料；  
（四）担保财产现状的说明；  
（五）人民法院认为需要提交的其他材料。

**Article 365** An application for realization of a security interest shall be accompanied by the following materials:  
(1) A written application, which shall state the basic information such as the name or title and contact information of the applicant and the respondent, the specific request, and the facts and reasons;  
(2) Materials proving the existence of the security interest, including the principal contract, the security contract, the mortgage registration certificate or certificate of other rights, the certificate of rights for a pledge of rights or the registration certificate of the pledge, etc.;  
(3) Materials proving that the conditions for realization of the security interest have been satisfied;  
(4) A statement on the current status of the secured property;  
(5) Other materials that the People's Court considers necessary to submit.

<a id="art-366"></a>第三百六十六条　人民法院受理申请后，应当在五日内向被申请人送达申请书副本、异议权利告知书等文书。  
被申请人有异议的，应当在收到人民法院通知后的五日内向人民法院提出，同时说明理由并提供相应的证据材料。

**Article 366** After accepting the application, the People's Court shall, within five days, serve on the respondent the copy of the application, the notice of the right to object, and other documents.  
If the respondent has any objection, it shall raise it with the People's Court within five days after receiving the notice from the People's Court, and shall at the same time state the reasons and provide corresponding evidentiary materials.

<a id="art-367"></a>第三百六十七条　实现担保物权案件可以由审判员一人独任审查。担保财产标的额超过基层人民法院管辖范围的，应当组成合议庭进行审查。

**Article 367** A case concerning the realization of a security interest may be examined by a single judge. If the subject matter amount of the secured property exceeds the jurisdictional limit of the Primary People's Court, a collegial panel shall be formed for examination.

<a id="art-368"></a>第三百六十八条　人民法院审查实现担保物权案件，可以询问申请人、被申请人、利害关系人，必要时可以依职权调查相关事实。

**Article 368** When examining a case concerning the realization of a security interest, the People's Court may question the applicant, the respondent, and interested persons, and when necessary, may ex officio investigate relevant facts.

<a id="art-369"></a>第三百六十九条　人民法院应当就主合同的效力、期限、履行情况，担保物权是否有效设立、担保财产的范围、被担保的债权范围、被担保的债权是否已届清偿期等担保物权实现的条件，以及是否损害他人合法权益等内容进行审查。  
被申请人或者利害关系人提出异议的，人民法院应当一并审查。

**Article 369** The People's Court shall examine matters including the validity, term, and performance of the principal contract; whether the security interest has been validly established; the scope of the secured property; the scope of the secured claim; whether the secured claim has fallen due; and other conditions for realization of the security interest, as well as whether it harms the lawful rights and interests of others.  
If the respondent or an interested person raises an objection, the People's Court shall examine it together.

<a id="art-370"></a>第三百七十条　人民法院审查后，按下列情形分别处理：  
（一）当事人对实现担保物权无实质性争议且实现担保物权条件成就的，裁定准许拍卖、变卖担保财产；  
（二）当事人对实现担保物权有部分实质性争议的，可以就无争议部分裁定准许拍卖、变卖担保财产；  
（三）当事人对实现担保物权有实质性争议的，裁定驳回申请，并告知申请人向人民法院提起诉讼。

**Article 370** Upon examination, the People's Court shall handle the matter according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If the parties have no substantial dispute over the realization of the security interest and the conditions for realizing the security interest are satisfied, a ruling shall be made to permit the auction or sale of the secured property;  
(2) If the parties have a partial substantial dispute over the realization of the security interest, a ruling may be made to permit the auction or sale of the secured property with respect to the undisputed part;  
(3) If the parties have a substantial dispute over the realization of the security interest, a ruling shall be made to dismiss the application, and the applicant shall be informed to file an action with the People's Court.

<a id="art-371"></a>第三百七十一条　人民法院受理申请后，申请人对担保财产提出保全申请的，可以按照民事诉讼法关于诉讼保全的规定办理。

**Article 371** After the People's Court accepts the application, if the applicant makes an application for preservation of the secured property, it may be handled in accordance with the provisions of the Civil Procedure Law on litigation preservation.

<a id="art-372"></a>第三百七十二条　适用特别程序作出的判决、裁定，当事人、利害关系人认为有错误的，可以向作出该判决、裁定的人民法院提出异议。人民法院经审查，异议成立或者部分成立的，作出新的判决、裁定撤销或者改变原判决、裁定；异议不成立的，裁定驳回。  
对人民法院作出的确认调解协议、准许实现担保物权的裁定，当事人有异议的，应当自收到裁定之日起十五日内提出；利害关系人有异议的，自知道或者应当知道其民事权益受到侵害之日起六个月内提出。

**Article 372** If a party or an interested person believes that a judgment or ruling rendered under the special procedure is erroneous, it may raise an objection with the People's Court that rendered the judgment or ruling. Upon examination by the People's Court, if the objection is established or partially established, a new judgment or ruling shall be made to revoke or modify the original judgment or ruling; if the objection is not established, a ruling shall be made to dismiss it.  
If a party has an objection to a ruling made by the People's Court confirming a mediation agreement or granting permission to realize a security interest, it shall raise the objection within fifteen days from receipt of the ruling; if an interested person has an objection, it shall raise it within six months from the date when it knows or should have known that its civil rights and interests have been infringed.  
XVIII. Procedure for Trial Supervision

### 十八、审判监督程序

<a id="art-373"></a>第三百七十三条　当事人死亡或者终止的，其权利义务承继者可以根据[民事诉讼法第二百零六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-206)、第二百零八条的规定申请再审。  
判决、调解书生效后，当事人将判决、调解书确认的债权转让，债权受让人对该判决、调解书不服申请再审的，人民法院不予受理。

**Article 373** If a party dies or terminates, the successor to its rights and obligations may apply for retrial in accordance with Articles 206 and 208 of the Civil Procedure Law.  
After a judgment or mediation statement takes effect, if a party assigns the claim confirmed by the judgment or mediation statement, and the assignee of the claim is dissatisfied with the judgment or mediation statement and applies for retrial, the People's Court shall not accept the application.

<a id="art-374"></a>第三百七十四条　[民事诉讼法第二百零六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-206)规定的人数众多的一方当事人，包括公民、法人和其他组织。  
[民事诉讼法第二百零六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-206)规定的当事人双方为公民的案件，是指原告和被告均为公民的案件。

**Article 374** The party where there is a large number of persons as referred to in Article 206 of the Civil Procedure Law includes citizens, legal persons, and other organizations.  
A case where both parties are citizens as referred to in Article 206 of the Civil Procedure Law means a case in which both the plaintiff and the defendant are citizens.

<a id="art-375"></a>第三百七十五条　当事人申请再审，应当提交下列材料：  
（一）再审申请书，并按照被申请人和原审其他当事人的人数提交副本；  
（二）再审申请人是自然人的，应当提交身份证明；再审申请人是法人或者其他组织的，应当提交营业执照、组织机构代码证书、法定代表人或者主要负责人身份证明书。委托他人代为申请的，应当提交授权委托书和代理人身份证明；  
（三）原审判决书、裁定书、调解书；  
（四）反映案件基本事实的主要证据及其他材料。  
前款第二项、第三项、第四项规定的材料可以是与原件核对无异的复印件。

**Article 375** A party applying for retrial shall submit the following materials:  
(1) A written application for retrial, along with copies equal to the number of the respondent and other parties in the original trial;  
(2) If the applicant for retrial is a natural person, a certificate of identity shall be submitted; if the applicant for retrial is a legal person or other organization, the business license, the organization code certificate, and the certificate of the legal representative or the principal responsible person's identity shall be submitted. If another person is entrusted to file the application, a power of attorney and the agent's identity certificate shall be submitted;  
(3) The judgment, ruling, or mediation statement of the original trial;  
(4) The main evidence reflecting the basic facts of the case and other materials.  
The materials specified in items (2), (3), and (4) of the preceding paragraph may be copies verified as identical to the originals.

<a id="art-376"></a>第三百七十六条　再审申请书应当记明下列事项：  
（一）再审申请人与被申请人及原审其他当事人的基本信息；  
（二）原审人民法院的名称，原审裁判文书案号；  
（三）具体的再审请求；  
（四）申请再审的法定情形及具体事实、理由。  
再审申请书应当明确申请再审的人民法院，并由再审申请人签名、捺印或者盖章。

**Article 376** The written application for retrial shall state the following:  
(1) The basic information of the applicant for retrial, the respondent, and other parties in the original trial;  
(2) The name of the People's Court of the original trial and the case number of the original adjudicative document;  
(3) The specific request for retrial;  
(4) The statutory grounds for applying for retrial and the specific facts and reasons.  
The written application for retrial shall clearly indicate the People's Court to which the application is made, and shall be signed, fingerprinted, or sealed by the applicant for retrial.

<a id="art-377"></a>第三百七十七条　当事人一方人数众多或者当事人双方为公民的案件，当事人分别向原审人民法院和上一级人民法院申请再审且不能协商一致的，由原审人民法院受理。

**Article 377** In a case where one party consists of a large number of persons or both parties are citizens, if the parties separately apply for retrial to the original trial People's Court and the court at the next higher level and cannot reach a consensus, the original trial People's Court shall accept the application.

<a id="art-378"></a>第三百七十八条　适用特别程序、督促程序、公示催告程序、破产程序等非讼程序审理的案件，当事人不得申请再审。

**Article 378** A party may not apply for retrial in a case heard under a non-contentious procedure such as the special procedure, summary procedure, public summons procedure, or bankruptcy procedure.

<a id="art-379"></a>第三百七十九条　当事人认为发生法律效力的不予受理、驳回起诉的裁定错误的，可以申请再审。

**Article 379** If a party believes that a legally effective ruling on non-acceptance or dismissal of the action is erroneous, it may apply for retrial.

<a id="art-380"></a>第三百八十条　当事人就离婚案件中的财产分割问题申请再审，如涉及判决中已分割的财产，人民法院应当依照[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)的规定进行审查，符合再审条件的，应当裁定再审；如涉及判决中未作处理的夫妻共同财产，应当告知当事人另行起诉。

**Article 380** If a party applies for retrial concerning the division of property in a divorce case, if it involves property that has been divided in the judgment, the People's Court shall examine it in accordance with Article 207 of the Civil Procedure Law, and if the conditions for retrial are satisfied, it shall make a ruling to retry the case; if it involves marital joint property that was not dealt with in the judgment, the party shall be informed to file a separate action.

<a id="art-381"></a>第三百八十一条　当事人申请再审，有下列情形之一的，人民法院不予受理：  
（一）再审申请被驳回后再次提出申请的；  
（二）对再审判决、裁定提出申请的；  
（三）在人民检察院对当事人的申请作出不予提出再审检察建议或者抗诉决定后又提出申请的。  
前款第一项、第二项规定情形，人民法院应当告知当事人可以向人民检察院申请再审检察建议或者抗诉，但因人民检察院提出再审检察建议或者抗诉而再审作出的判决、裁定除外。

**Article 381** If a party applies for retrial under any of the following circumstances, the People's Court shall not accept the application:  
(1) The application is made again after the application for retrial has been dismissed;  
(2) The application is made against a judgment or ruling rendered in the retrial;  
(3) The application is made after the People's Procuratorate has made a decision not to file a retrial procuratorial recommendation or protest against the party's application.  
For the circumstances specified in items (1) and (2) of the preceding paragraph, the People's Court shall inform the party that it may apply to the People's Procuratorate for a retrial procuratorial recommendation or protest, except where the judgment or ruling was rendered in a retrial that resulted from the People's Procuratorate's issuance of a retrial procuratorial recommendation or protest.

<a id="art-382"></a>第三百八十二条　当事人对已经发生法律效力的调解书申请再审，应当在调解书发生法律效力后六个月内提出。

**Article 382** If a party applies for retrial against a mediation statement that has already taken legal effect, it shall file the application within six months after the mediation statement takes legal effect.

<a id="art-383"></a>第三百八十三条　人民法院应当自收到符合条件的再审申请书等材料之日起五日内向再审申请人发送受理通知书，并向被申请人及原审其他当事人发送应诉通知书、再审申请书副本等材料。

**Article 383** The People's Court shall, within five days from receipt of the qualified written application for retrial and other materials, send a notice of acceptance to the applicant for retrial, and send a notice of response, a copy of the written application for retrial, and other materials to the respondent and other parties in the original trial.

<a id="art-384"></a>第三百八十四条　人民法院受理申请再审案件后，应当依照[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)、第二百零八条、第二百一十一条等规定，对当事人主张的再审事由进行审查。

**Article 384** After accepting a case of an application for retrial, the People's Court shall examine the grounds for retrial asserted by the party in accordance with Articles 207, 208, 211, etc. of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-385"></a>第三百八十五条　再审申请人提供的新的证据，能够证明原判决、裁定认定基本事实或者裁判结果错误的，应当认定为[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)第一项规定的情形。  
对于符合前款规定的证据，人民法院应当责令再审申请人说明其逾期提供该证据的理由；拒不说明理由或者理由不成立的，依照[民事诉讼法第六十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-68)第二款和[本解释第一百零二条](#art-102)的规定处理。

**Article 385** If the new evidence provided by the applicant for retrial can prove that the basic facts found in the original judgment or ruling or the adjudicative result is erroneous, it shall be deemed as the circumstance specified in Article 207(1) of the Civil Procedure Law.  
For evidence that meets the requirements of the preceding paragraph, the People's Court shall order the applicant for retrial to explain the reasons for the late submission of such evidence; if the applicant refuses to explain the reasons or the reasons are not established, the matter shall be handled in accordance with Article 68(2) of the Civil Procedure Law and Article 102 of this Interpretation.

<a id="art-386"></a>第三百八十六条　再审申请人证明其提交的新的证据符合下列情形之一的，可以认定逾期提供证据的理由成立：  
（一）在原审庭审结束前已经存在，因客观原因于庭审结束后才发现的；  
（二）在原审庭审结束前已经发现，但因客观原因无法取得或者在规定的期限内不能提供的；  
（三）在原审庭审结束后形成，无法据此另行提起诉讼的。  
再审申请人提交的证据在原审中已经提供，原审人民法院未组织质证且未作为裁判根据的，视为逾期提供证据的理由成立，但原审人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第六十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-68)规定不予采纳的除外。

**Article 386** If the applicant for retrial proves that the new evidence it submits meets any of the following circumstances, the reasons for late submission of evidence may be deemed to be established:  
(1) It existed before the conclusion of the original trial hearing, but was discovered after the hearing due to objective reasons;  
(2) It was discovered before the conclusion of the original trial hearing, but could not be obtained due to objective reasons or could not be provided within the prescribed time limit;  
(3) It was formed after the conclusion of the original trial hearing, and a separate action cannot be filed based on it.  
If the evidence submitted by the applicant for retrial was already provided in the original trial, and the original trial People's Court did not organize cross-examination and did not use it as a basis for adjudication, the reasons for late submission of evidence shall be deemed to be established, except where the original trial People's Court decided not to admit the evidence in accordance with Article 68 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-387"></a>第三百八十七条　当事人对原判决、裁定认定事实的主要证据在原审中拒绝发表质证意见或者质证中未对证据发表质证意见的，不属于[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)第四项规定的未经质证的情形。

**Article 387** If a party, during the original trial, refuses to express cross-examination opinions on the main evidence that the original judgment or ruling relied on to find facts, or fails to express cross-examination opinions on the evidence during cross-examination, this shall not constitute the circumstance of failing to conduct cross-examination as specified in Article 207(4) of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-388"></a>第三百八十八条　有下列情形之一，导致判决、裁定结果错误的，应当认定为[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)第六项规定的原判决、裁定适用法律确有错误：  
（一）适用的法律与案件性质明显不符的；  
（二）确定民事责任明显违背当事人约定或者法律规定的；  
（三）适用已经失效或者尚未施行的法律的；  
（四）违反法律溯及力规定的；  
（五）违反法律适用规则的；  
（六）明显违背立法原意的。

**Article 388** Any of the following circumstances, which leads to an erroneous result in the judgment or ruling, shall be deemed as the original judgment or ruling having erroneously applied the law as specified in Article 207(6) of the Civil Procedure Law:  
(1) The applied law is clearly inconsistent with the nature of the case;  
(2) The determination of civil liability clearly violates the agreement of the parties or the provisions of the law;  
(3) A law that has ceased to have effect or has not yet come into force is applied;  
(4) Provisions on the retroactive effect of law are violated;  
(5) Rules on the application of law are violated;  
(6) The legislative intent is clearly violated.

<a id="art-389"></a>第三百八十九条　原审开庭过程中有下列情形之一的，应当认定为[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)第九项规定的剥夺当事人辩论权利：  
（一）不允许当事人发表辩论意见的；  
（二）应当开庭审理而未开庭审理的；  
（三）违反法律规定送达起诉状副本或者上诉状副本，致使当事人无法行使辩论权利的；  
（四）违法剥夺当事人辩论权利的其他情形。

**Article 389** Any of the following circumstances during the original trial hearing shall be deemed as depriving the party of the right to debate as specified in Article 207(9) of the Civil Procedure Law:  
(1) The party is not allowed to express its arguments;  
(2) The case should have been heard in an open hearing but was not;  
(3) The service of a copy of the statement of claim or a copy of the appeal brief violates the legal provisions, thereby causing the party unable to exercise its right to debate;  
(4) Other circumstances where the party's right to debate is illegally deprived.

<a id="art-390"></a>第三百九十条　[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)第十一项规定的诉讼请求，包括一审诉讼请求、二审上诉请求，但当事人未对一审判决、裁定遗漏或者超出诉讼请求提起上诉的除外。

**Article 390** The claims referred to in Item (11) of Article 207 of the Civil Procedure Law include claims at trial of first instance and claims on appeal at trial of second instance, except where the party fails to appeal on the ground that the judgment or ruling of first instance omits or exceeds the claims.
