<a id="art-391"></a>第三百九十一条　[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)第十二项规定的法律文书包括：  
（一）发生法律效力的判决书、裁定书、调解书；  
（二）发生法律效力的仲裁裁决书；  
（三）具有强制执行效力的公证债权文书。

**Article 391** The legal instruments referred to in Item (12) of Article 207 of the Civil Procedure Law include:  
(1) judgments, rulings, and mediation documents that have become legally effective;  
(2) arbitration awards that have become legally effective;  
(3) notarized instruments of creditor's rights that are effective for compulsory enforcement.

<a id="art-392"></a>第三百九十二条　[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)第十三项规定的审判人员审理该案件时有贪污受贿、徇私舞弊、枉法裁判行为，是指已经由生效刑事法律文书或者纪律处分决定所确认的行为。

**Article 392** The act of corruption, bribery, malpractice for personal gain, or perversion of law in adjudicating the case by the adjudicators as referred to in Item (13) of Article 207 of the Civil Procedure Law means an act that has been confirmed by an effective criminal legal instrument or a disciplinary sanction decision.

<a id="art-393"></a>第三百九十三条　当事人主张的再审事由成立，且符合民事诉讼法和本解释规定的申请再审条件的，人民法院应当裁定再审。  
当事人主张的再审事由不成立，或者当事人申请再审超过法定申请再审期限、超出法定再审事由范围等不符合民事诉讼法和本解释规定的申请再审条件的，人民法院应当裁定驳回再审申请。

**Article 393** Where the ground for retrial asserted by a party is established and the conditions for applying for retrial as prescribed by the Civil Procedure Law and this Interpretation are satisfied, the People's Court shall rule to retry the case.  
Where the ground for retrial asserted by a party is not established, or the party's application for retrial exceeds the statutory time limit for applying for retrial, exceeds the statutory scope of grounds for retrial, or otherwise fails to meet the conditions for applying for retrial as prescribed by the Civil Procedure Law and this Interpretation, the People's Court shall rule to dismiss the application for retrial.

<a id="art-394"></a>第三百九十四条　人民法院对已经发生法律效力的判决、裁定、调解书依法决定再审，依照[民事诉讼法第二百一十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-213)规定，需要中止执行的，应当在再审裁定中同时写明中止原判决、裁定、调解书的执行；情况紧急的，可以将中止执行裁定口头通知负责执行的人民法院，并在通知后十日内发出裁定书。

**Article 394** Where the People's Court decides to retry a legally effective judgment, ruling, or mediation document according to law, and it is necessary to suspend enforcement in accordance with Article 213 of the Civil Procedure Law, it shall simultaneously state in the retrial ruling the suspension of enforcement of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation document; in an emergency, it may orally notify the People's Court responsible for enforcement of the ruling to suspend enforcement, and shall issue the written ruling within ten days after the notification.

<a id="art-395"></a>第三百九十五条　人民法院根据审查案件的需要决定是否询问当事人。新的证据可能推翻原判决、裁定的，人民法院应当询问当事人。

**Article 395** The People's Court shall decide whether to examine the parties based on the needs of case review. Where new evidence may overturn the original judgment or ruling, the People's Court shall examine the parties.

<a id="art-396"></a>第三百九十六条　审查再审申请期间，被申请人及原审其他当事人依法提出再审申请的，人民法院应当将其列为再审申请人，对其再审事由一并审查，审查期限重新计算。经审查，其中一方再审申请人主张的再审事由成立的，应当裁定再审。各方再审申请人主张的再审事由均不成立的，一并裁定驳回再审申请。

**Article 396** During the review of an application for retrial, if the respondent or other parties in the original trial file an application for retrial according to law, the People's Court shall list them as applicants for retrial, review their grounds for retrial together, and the review period shall be recalculated. Upon review, if the ground for retrial asserted by any one of the applicants for retrial is established, it shall rule to retry the case. If none of the grounds for retrial asserted by all applicants for retrial are established, it shall rule to dismiss all the applications for retrial together.

<a id="art-397"></a>第三百九十七条　审查再审申请期间，再审申请人申请人民法院委托鉴定、勘验的，人民法院不予准许。

**Article 397** During the review of an application for retrial, if the applicant for retrial requests the People's Court to entrust an expert evaluation or inspection, the People's Court shall not grant the request.

<a id="art-398"></a>第三百九十八条　审查再审申请期间，再审申请人撤回再审申请的，是否准许，由人民法院裁定。  
再审申请人经传票传唤，无正当理由拒不接受询问的，可以按撤回再审申请处理。

**Article 398** During the review of an application for retrial, if the applicant for retrial withdraws the application for retrial, whether to grant the withdrawal shall be ruled by the People's Court.  
If the applicant for retrial, after being summoned by service of summons, refuses without justifiable cause to submit to examination, it may be treated as withdrawal of the application for retrial.

<a id="art-399"></a>第三百九十九条　人民法院准许撤回再审申请或者按撤回再审申请处理后，再审申请人再次申请再审的，不予受理，但有[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)第一项、第三项、第十二项、第十三项规定情形，自知道或者应当知道之日起六个月内提出的除外。

**Article 399** After the People's Court grants the withdrawal of an application for retrial or treats it as withdrawal, if the applicant for retrial applies for retrial again, no acceptance shall be allowed, except where the circumstances fall under Items (1), (3), (12), or (13) of Article 207 of the Civil Procedure Law and the application is filed within six months from the date of knowing or should have known thereof.

<a id="art-400"></a>第四百条　再审申请审查期间，有下列情形之一的，裁定终结审查：  
（一）再审申请人死亡或者终止，无权利义务承继者或者权利义务承继者声明放弃再审申请的；  
（二）在给付之诉中，负有给付义务的被申请人死亡或者终止，无可供执行的财产，也没有应当承担义务的人的；  
（三）当事人达成和解协议且已履行完毕的，但当事人在和解协议中声明不放弃申请再审权利的除外；  
（四）他人未经授权以当事人名义申请再审的；  
（五）原审或者上一级人民法院已经裁定再审的；  
（六）有[本解释第三百八十一条](#art-381)第一款规定情形的。

**Article 400** During the review of an application for retrial, if any of the following circumstances exists, a ruling shall be made to terminate the review:  
(1) the applicant for retrial dies or is terminated, and there is no successor to rights and obligations, or the successor renounces the application for retrial;  
(2) in an action for performance, the respondent obligated to perform dies or is terminated, and there is no property available for enforcement and no person who shall assume the obligation;  
(3) the parties reach a settlement agreement and have performed it in full, except where the parties declare in the settlement agreement that they do not waive the right to apply for retrial;  
(4) a person without authorization applies for retrial in the name of a party;  
(5) the court of original trial or the higher People's Court has already ruled to retry the case;  
(6) the circumstance provided in paragraph 1 of Article 381 of this Interpretation exists.

<a id="art-401"></a>第四百零一条　人民法院审理再审案件应当组成合议庭开庭审理，但按照第二审程序审理，有特殊情况或者双方当事人已经通过其他方式充分表达意见，且书面同意不开庭审理的除外。  
符合缺席判决条件的，可以缺席判决。

**Article 401** The People's Court shall form a collegial panel to hear a retrial case in open court, except where the case is tried under the procedure of second instance and there are special circumstances, or both parties have already fully expressed their opinions through other means and have agreed in writing not to hold a hearing.  
Where the conditions for a default judgment are met, a default judgment may be rendered.

<a id="art-402"></a>第四百零二条　人民法院开庭审理再审案件，应当按照下列情形分别进行：  
（一）因当事人申请再审的，先由再审申请人陈述再审请求及理由，后由被申请人答辩、其他原审当事人发表意见；  
（二）因抗诉再审的，先由抗诉机关宣读抗诉书，再由申请抗诉的当事人陈述，后由被申请人答辩、其他原审当事人发表意见；  
（三）人民法院依职权再审，有申诉人的，先由申诉人陈述再审请求及理由，后由被申诉人答辩、其他原审当事人发表意见；  
（四）人民法院依职权再审，没有申诉人的，先由原审原告或者原审上诉人陈述，后由原审其他当事人发表意见。  
对前款第一项至第三项规定的情形，人民法院应当要求当事人明确其再审请求。

**Article 402** When conducting a hearing in a retrial case, the People's Court shall proceed in the following order:  
(1) For retrial initiated by a party's application, the applicant for retrial shall first state the requests and grounds for retrial, followed by the respondent's defense and the opinions of other parties in the original trial;  
(2) For retrial initiated by a protest, the protesting authority shall first read the protest letter, then the party that applied for the protest shall make a statement, followed by the respondent's defense and the opinions of other parties in the original trial;  
(3) For retrial initiated ex officio by the People's Court where there is a petitioner, the petitioner shall first state the requests and grounds for retrial, followed by the respondent's defense and the opinions of other parties in the original trial;  
(4) For retrial initiated ex officio by the People's Court where there is no petitioner, the original plaintiff or the original appellant shall first make a statement, followed by the opinions of other parties in the original trial.  
For the circumstances set forth in items (1) to (3) of the preceding paragraph, the People's Court shall require the parties to clarify their requests for retrial.

<a id="art-403"></a>第四百零三条　人民法院审理再审案件应当围绕再审请求进行。当事人的再审请求超出原审诉讼请求的，不予审理；符合另案诉讼条件的，告知当事人可以另行起诉。  
被申请人及原审其他当事人在庭审辩论结束前提出的再审请求，符合[民事诉讼法第二百一十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-212)规定的，人民法院应当一并审理。  
人民法院经再审，发现已经发生法律效力的判决、裁定损害国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益的，应当一并审理。

**Article 403** The People's Court shall hear a retrial case centering on the requests for retrial. Where a party's request for retrial exceeds the claims in the original trial, it shall not be heard; if it meets the conditions for a separate action, the party shall be informed that it may file a separate lawsuit.  
If the respondent or other parties in the original trial raise a request for retrial before the conclusion of court debate, and such request meets the requirements of Article 212 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court shall hear it together with the original case.  
If, during the retrial, the People's Court discovers that the legally effective judgment or ruling harms the national interest, social public interest, or the lawful rights and interests of others, it shall hear the matter together.

<a id="art-404"></a>第四百零四条　再审审理期间，有下列情形之一的，可以裁定终结再审程序：  
（一）再审申请人在再审期间撤回再审请求，人民法院准许的；  
（二）再审申请人经传票传唤，无正当理由拒不到庭的，或者未经法庭许可中途退庭，按撤回再审请求处理的；  
（三）人民检察院撤回抗诉的；  
（四）有[本解释第四百条](#art-400)第一项至第四项规定情形的。  
因人民检察院提出抗诉裁定再审的案件，申请抗诉的当事人有前款规定的情形，且不损害国家利益、社会公共利益或者他人合法权益的，人民法院应当裁定终结再审程序。  
再审程序终结后，人民法院裁定中止执行的原生效判决自动恢复执行。

**Article 404** During the retrial proceedings, if any of the following circumstances exists, a ruling may be made to terminate the retrial procedure:  
(1) the applicant for retrial withdraws the request for retrial during the retrial and the People's Court grants the withdrawal;  
(2) the applicant for retrial, after being summoned by service of summons, refuses to appear in court without justifiable cause, or leaves the courtroom without permission during the hearing, and is treated as having withdrawn the request for retrial;  
(3) the People's Procuratorate withdraws its protest;  
(4) the circumstances provided in items (1) to (4) of Article 400 of this Interpretation exist.  
For a case where a retrial is ordered due to a protest filed by the People's Procuratorate, if the party that applied for the protest is in any of the circumstances set forth in the preceding paragraph and the national interest, social public interest, or lawful rights and interests of others are not harmed, the People's Court shall rule to terminate the retrial procedure.  
After the termination of the retrial procedure, the enforcement of the original effective judgment that was suspended by the People's Court's ruling shall be automatically resumed.

<a id="art-405"></a>第四百零五条　人民法院经再审审理认为，原判决、裁定认定事实清楚、适用法律正确的，应予维持；原判决、裁定认定事实、适用法律虽有瑕疵，但裁判结果正确的，应当在再审判决、裁定中纠正瑕疵后予以维持。  
原判决、裁定认定事实、适用法律错误，导致裁判结果错误的，应当依法改判、撤销或者变更。

**Article 405** If, after retrial, the People's Court finds that the facts found and the law applied in the original judgment or ruling are clear and correct, it shall affirm the original judgment or ruling; if the original judgment or ruling has defects in fact-finding or application of law but the adjudicative result is correct, the People's Court shall correct the defects in the retrial judgment or ruling and then affirm the result.  
If the original judgment or ruling errs in fact-finding or application of law, resulting in an incorrect adjudicative result, the People's Court shall amend, revoke, or alter the original judgment or ruling according to law.

<a id="art-406"></a>第四百零六条　按照第二审程序再审的案件，人民法院经审理认为不符合民事诉讼法规定的起诉条件或者符合[民事诉讼法第一百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-127)规定不予受理情形的，应当裁定撤销一、二审判决，驳回起诉。

**Article 406** For a case retried under the procedure of second instance, if upon trial the People's Court finds that the case does not meet the conditions for filing an action as prescribed by the Civil Procedure Law, or falls under the circumstances for non-acceptance as provided in Article 127 of the Civil Procedure Law, it shall rule to revoke the judgments of first and second instance and dismiss the action.

<a id="art-407"></a>第四百零七条　人民法院对调解书裁定再审后，按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）当事人提出的调解违反自愿原则的事由不成立，且调解书的内容不违反法律强制性规定的，裁定驳回再审申请；  
（二）人民检察院抗诉或者再审检察建议所主张的损害国家利益、社会公共利益的理由不成立的，裁定终结再审程序。  
前款规定情形，人民法院裁定中止执行的调解书需要继续执行的，自动恢复执行。

**Article 407** After the People's Court rules to retry a mediation document, it shall handle the case according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If the ground raised by a party that the mediation violated the principle of voluntariness is not established and the content of the mediation document does not violate mandatory provisions of law, the People's Court shall rule to dismiss the application for retrial;  
(2) If the ground asserted by the People's Procuratorate in its protest or retrial procuratorial recommendation that the mediation document harms the national interest or social public interest is not established, the People's Court shall rule to terminate the retrial procedure.  
In the circumstances set forth in the preceding paragraph, if the mediation document whose enforcement was suspended by the People's Court's ruling needs to continue enforcement, the enforcement shall be automatically resumed.

<a id="art-408"></a>第四百零八条　一审原告在再审审理程序中申请撤回起诉，经其他当事人同意，且不损害国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益的，人民法院可以准许。裁定准许撤诉的，应当一并撤销原判决。  
一审原告在再审审理程序中撤回起诉后重复起诉的，人民法院不予受理。

**Article 408** If the plaintiff of first instance applies to withdraw the action during the retrial procedure, and the other parties consent and the withdrawal does not harm the national interest, social public interest, or the lawful rights and interests of others, the People's Court may grant the request. If a ruling granting the withdrawal is made, the original judgment shall be revoked together.  
If the plaintiff of first instance, after withdrawing the action in the retrial procedure, files another action on the same matter, the People's Court shall not accept the case.

<a id="art-409"></a>第四百零九条　当事人提交新的证据致使再审改判，因再审申请人或者申请检察监督当事人的过错未能在原审程序中及时举证，被申请人等当事人请求补偿其增加的交通、住宿、就餐、误工等必要费用的，人民法院应予支持。

**Article 409** If a party submits new evidence leading to a change of the original judgment in retrial, and due to the fault of the applicant for retrial or the party applying for procuratorial supervision, the evidence was not timely produced in the original trial, the respondent or other parties requesting compensation for the increased necessary expenses such as transportation, accommodation, meals, and lost work time shall be supported by the People's Court.

<a id="art-410"></a>第四百一十条　部分当事人到庭并达成调解协议，其他当事人未作出书面表示的，人民法院应当在判决中对该事实作出表述；调解协议内容不违反法律规定，且不损害其他当事人合法权益的，可以在判决主文中予以确认。

**Article 410** If some parties appear in court and reach a mediation agreement, while other parties have not made a written statement, the People's Court shall state this fact in its judgment; if the content of the mediation agreement does not violate the law and does not harm the lawful rights and interests of the other parties, it may be affirmed in the main text of the judgment.

<a id="art-411"></a>第四百一十一条　人民检察院依法对损害国家利益、社会公共利益的发生法律效力的判决、裁定、调解书提出抗诉，或者经人民检察院检察委员会讨论决定提出再审检察建议的，人民法院应予受理。

**Article 411** If the People's Procuratorate files a protest against a legally effective judgment, ruling, or mediation document that harms the national interest or social public interest according to law, or, after discussion and decision by the Procuratorial Committee of the People's Procuratorate, files a retrial procuratorial recommendation, the People's Court shall accept the case.

<a id="art-412"></a>第四百一十二条　人民检察院对已经发生法律效力的判决以及不予受理、驳回起诉的裁定依法提出抗诉的，人民法院应予受理，但适用特别程序、督促程序、公示催告程序、破产程序以及解除婚姻关系的判决、裁定等不适用审判监督程序的判决、裁定除外。

**Article 412** If the People's Procuratorate files a protest according to law against a legally effective judgment or a ruling of non-acceptance or dismissal of action, the People's Court shall accept the case, except for judgments or rulings that are not subject to the trial supervision procedure, such as those rendered under the special procedure, procedure for hastening debt recovery, procedure for public summons for exhortation, bankruptcy procedure, or judgments and rulings on dissolution of marriage.

<a id="art-413"></a>第四百一十三条　人民检察院依照[民事诉讼法第二百一十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-216)第一款第三项规定对有明显错误的再审判决、裁定提出抗诉或者再审检察建议的，人民法院应予受理。

**Article 413** If the People's Procuratorate, in accordance with Item (3) of Paragraph 1 of Article 216 of the Civil Procedure Law, files a protest or a retrial procuratorial recommendation against a retrial judgment or ruling that has obvious errors, the People's Court shall accept the case.

<a id="art-414"></a>第四百一十四条　地方各级人民检察院依当事人的申请对生效判决、裁定向同级人民法院提出再审检察建议，符合下列条件的，应予受理：  
（一）再审检察建议书和原审当事人申请书及相关证据材料已经提交；  
（二）建议再审的对象为依照民事诉讼法和本解释规定可以进行再审的判决、裁定；  
（三）再审检察建议书列明该判决、裁定有[民事诉讼法第二百一十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-215)第二款规定情形；  
（四）符合[民事诉讼法第二百一十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-216)第一款第一项、第二项规定情形；  
（五）再审检察建议经该人民检察院检察委员会讨论决定。  
不符合前款规定的，人民法院可以建议人民检察院予以补正或者撤回；不予补正或者撤回的，应当函告人民检察院不予受理。

**Article 414** Where a local People's Procuratorate, at the request of a party, files a retrial procuratorial recommendation with the People's Court at the same level against an effective judgment or ruling, the People's Court shall accept it if the following conditions are satisfied:  
(1) the retrial procuratorial recommendation, the application of the party in the original trial, and relevant evidentiary materials have been submitted;  
(2) the object of the recommended retrial is a judgment or ruling that may be retried under the Civil Procedure Law and this Interpretation;  
(3) the retrial procuratorial recommendation specifies that the judgment or ruling falls under the circumstances provided in Paragraph 2 of Article 215 of the Civil Procedure Law;  
(4) the circumstances provided in Items (1) and (2) of Paragraph 1 of Article 216 of the Civil Procedure Law are satisfied;  
(5) the retrial procuratorial recommendation has been discussed and decided by the Procuratorial Committee of that People's Procuratorate.  
If the conditions in the preceding paragraph are not met, the People's Court may suggest that the People's Procuratorate make corrections or withdraw the recommendation; if it fails to do so, the People's Court shall notify the People's Procuratorate in writing that the recommendation is not accepted.

<a id="art-415"></a>第四百一十五条　人民检察院依当事人的申请对生效判决、裁定提出抗诉，符合下列条件的，人民法院应当在三十日内裁定再审：  
（一）抗诉书和原审当事人申请书及相关证据材料已经提交；  
（二）抗诉对象为依照民事诉讼法和本解释规定可以进行再审的判决、裁定；  
（三）抗诉书列明该判决、裁定有[民事诉讼法第二百一十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-215)第一款规定情形；  
（四）符合[民事诉讼法第二百一十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-216)第一款第一项、第二项规定情形。  
不符合前款规定的，人民法院可以建议人民检察院予以补正或者撤回；不予补正或者撤回的，人民法院可以裁定不予受理。

**Article 415** Where the People's Procuratorate, at the request of a party, files a protest against an effective judgment or ruling, the People's Court shall, within thirty days, rule to retry the case if the following conditions are satisfied:  
(1) the protest letter, the application of the party in the original trial, and relevant evidentiary materials have been submitted;  
(2) the object of the protest is a judgment or ruling that may be retried under the Civil Procedure Law and this Interpretation;  
(3) the protest letter specifies that the judgment or ruling falls under the circumstances provided in Paragraph 1 of Article 215 of the Civil Procedure Law;  
(4) the circumstances provided in Items (1) and (2) of Paragraph 1 of Article 216 of the Civil Procedure Law are satisfied.  
If the conditions in the preceding paragraph are not met, the People's Court may suggest that the People's Procuratorate make corrections or withdraw the protest; if it fails to do so, the People's Court may rule not to accept the protest.

<a id="art-416"></a>第四百一十六条　当事人的再审申请被上级人民法院裁定驳回后，人民检察院对原判决、裁定、调解书提出抗诉，抗诉事由符合[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)第一项至第五项规定情形之一的，受理抗诉的人民法院可以交由下一级人民法院再审。

**Article 416** After a party's application for retrial has been dismissed by a ruling of the higher People's Court, if the People's Procuratorate files a protest against the original judgment, ruling, or mediation document, and the grounds for protest fall under any of the circumstances provided in Items (1) to (5) of Article 207 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court that accepts the protest may refer the case to the next lower People's Court for retrial.

<a id="art-417"></a>第四百一十七条　人民法院收到再审检察建议后，应当组成合议庭，在三个月内进行审查，发现原判决、裁定、调解书确有错误，需要再审的，依照[民事诉讼法第二百零五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-205)规定裁定再审，并通知当事人；经审查，决定不予再审的，应当书面回复人民检察院。

**Article 417** After receiving a retrial procuratorial recommendation, the People's Court shall form a collegial panel and conduct review within three months. If it finds that the original judgment, ruling, or mediation document indeed contains errors and requires retrial, it shall rule to retry the case in accordance with Article 205 of the Civil Procedure Law and notify the parties; if it decides not to retry after review, it shall reply to the People's Procuratorate in writing.

<a id="art-418"></a>第四百一十八条　人民法院审理因人民检察院抗诉或者检察建议裁定再审的案件，不受此前已经作出的驳回当事人再审申请裁定的影响。

**Article 418** Where the People's Court hears a case that has been ordered to be retried due to a protest or procuratorial recommendation from the People's Procuratorate, it shall not be affected by any previously made ruling dismissing the party's application for retrial.

<a id="art-419"></a>第四百一十九条　人民法院开庭审理抗诉案件，应当在开庭三日前通知人民检察院、当事人和其他诉讼参与人。同级人民检察院或者提出抗诉的人民检察院应当派员出庭。  
人民检察院因履行法律监督职责向当事人或者案外人调查核实的情况，应当向法庭提交并予以说明，由双方当事人进行质证。

**Article 419** Where the People's Court opens a hearing for a protest case, it shall notify the People's Procuratorate, the parties, and other participants in the proceedings at least three days before the hearing. The People's Procuratorate at the same level or the People's Procuratorate that filed the protest shall send personnel to appear in court.  
If the People's Procuratorate investigates and verifies information from parties or persons not involved in the case in the performance of its legal supervision duties, it shall submit such information to the court and explain it, and the information shall be subject to cross-examination by both parties.

<a id="art-420"></a>第四百二十条　必须共同进行诉讼的当事人因不能归责于本人或者其诉讼代理人的事由未参加诉讼的，可以根据[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)第八项规定，自知道或者应当知道之日起六个月内申请再审，但符合[本解释第四百二十一条](#art-421)规定情形的除外。  
人民法院因前款规定的当事人申请而裁定再审，按照第一审程序再审的，应当追加其为当事人，作出新的判决、裁定；按照第二审程序再审，经调解不能达成协议的，应当撤销原判决、裁定，发回重审，重审时应追加其为当事人。

**Article 420** If a party who must participate in the joint action fails to participate in the proceedings for reasons not attributable to himself or his litigation agent, such party may, in accordance with Item (8) of Article 207 of the Civil Procedure Law, apply for retrial within six months from the date of knowing or should have known thereof, except where the circumstances provided in Article 421 of this Interpretation exist.  
If the People's Court rules to retry the case due to the application of the party specified in the preceding paragraph, and the retrial is conducted under the procedure of first instance, it shall add such party as a party and render a new judgment or ruling; if the retrial is conducted under the procedure of second instance and mediation fails to reach an agreement, it shall revoke the original judgment and ruling and remand the case for retrial, and such party shall be added as a party in the retrial.

<a id="art-421"></a>第四百二十一条　根据[民事诉讼法第二百三十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-234)规定，案外人对驳回其执行异议的裁定不服，认为原判决、裁定、调解书内容错误损害其民事权益的，可以自执行异议裁定送达之日起六个月内，向作出原判决、裁定、调解书的人民法院申请再审。

**Article 421** In accordance with Article 234 of the Civil Procedure Law, if a person other than a party is dissatisfied with a ruling dismissing his objection to enforcement, and believes that the content of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation document is erroneous and harms his civil rights and interests, he may, within six months from the date of service of the ruling on the enforcement objection, apply for retrial to the People's Court that rendered the original judgment, ruling, or mediation document.

<a id="art-422"></a>第四百二十二条　根据[民事诉讼法第二百三十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-234)规定，人民法院裁定再审后，案外人属于必要的共同诉讼当事人的，依照[本解释第四百二十条](#art-420)第二款规定处理。  
案外人不是必要的共同诉讼当事人的，人民法院仅审理原判决、裁定、调解书对其民事权益造成损害的内容。经审理，再审请求成立的，撤销或者改变原判决、裁定、调解书；再审请求不成立的，维持原判决、裁定、调解书。

**Article 422** In accordance with Article 234 of the Civil Procedure Law, after the People's Court rules to retry the case, if the person other than a party is a necessary joint party in the action, the case shall be handled in accordance with Paragraph 2 of Article 420 of this Interpretation.  
If the person other than a party is not a necessary joint party, the People's Court shall only hear the part of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation document that harms his civil rights and interests. Upon trial, if the claim for retrial is established, the original judgment, ruling, or mediation document shall be revoked or modified; if the claim for retrial is not established, the original judgment, ruling, or mediation document shall be affirmed.

<a id="art-423"></a>第四百二十三条　[本解释第三百三十八条](#art-338)规定适用于审判监督程序。

**Article 423** The provisions of Article 338 of this Interpretation shall apply to the trial supervision procedure.

<a id="art-424"></a>第四百二十四条　对小额诉讼案件的判决、裁定，当事人以[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)规定的事由向原审人民法院申请再审的，人民法院应当受理。申请再审事由成立的，应当裁定再审，组成合议庭进行审理。作出的再审判决、裁定，当事人不得上诉。  
当事人以不应按小额诉讼案件审理为由向原审人民法院申请再审的，人民法院应当受理。理由成立的，应当裁定再审，组成合议庭审理。作出的再审判决、裁定，当事人可以上诉。

**Article 424** For a judgment or ruling in a small-claims case, if a party applies for retrial to the court of original trial on the grounds set forth in Article 207 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court shall accept the application. If the ground for retrial is established, it shall rule to retry the case and form a collegial panel for trial. The parties shall not appeal against the retrial judgment or ruling rendered.  
If a party applies for retrial to the court of original trial on the ground that the case should not have been tried as a small-claims case, the People's Court shall accept the application. If the ground is established, it shall rule to retry the case and form a collegial panel for trial. The parties may appeal against the retrial judgment or ruling rendered.  
19. Procedure for Hastening Debt Recovery

### 十九、督促程序

<a id="art-425"></a>第四百二十五条　两个以上人民法院都有管辖权的，债权人可以向其中一个基层人民法院申请支付令。  
债权人向两个以上有管辖权的基层人民法院申请支付令的，由最先立案的人民法院管辖。

**Article 425** Where two or more People's Courts have jurisdiction, the creditor may apply for an order for payment to any one of the Primary People's Courts.  
If the creditor applies for an order for payment to two or more Primary People's Courts that have jurisdiction, the court that first opens the case shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-426"></a>第四百二十六条　人民法院收到债权人的支付令申请书后，认为申请书不符合要求的，可以通知债权人限期补正。人民法院应当自收到补正材料之日起五日内通知债权人是否受理。

**Article 426** After receiving the creditor's application for an order for payment, if the People's Court finds that the application does not meet the requirements, it may notify the creditor to make corrections within a time limit. The People's Court shall, within five days from the date of receiving the corrected materials, notify the creditor whether the application is accepted.

<a id="art-427"></a>第四百二十七条　债权人申请支付令，符合下列条件的，基层人民法院应当受理，并在收到支付令申请书后五日内通知债权人：  
（一）请求给付金钱或者汇票、本票、支票、股票、债券、国库券、可转让的存款单等有价证券；  
（二）请求给付的金钱或者有价证券已到期且数额确定，并写明了请求所根据的事实、证据；  
（三）债权人没有对待给付义务；  
（四）债务人在我国境内且未下落不明；  
（五）支付令能够送达债务人；  
（六）收到申请书的人民法院有管辖权；  
（七）债权人未向人民法院申请诉前保全。  
不符合前款规定的，人民法院应当在收到支付令申请书后五日内通知债权人不予受理。  
基层人民法院受理申请支付令案件，不受债权金额的限制。

**Article 427** Where a creditor applies for an order for payment and meets the following conditions, the Primary People's Court shall accept the application and notify the creditor within five days from the date of receiving the application:  
(1) the claim is for payment of money or negotiable instruments such as bills of exchange, promissory notes, checks, stocks, bonds, treasury bonds, transferable certificates of deposit, etc.;  
(2) the money or negotiable instruments claimed have matured and the amount is definite, and the facts and evidence on which the claim is based are stated;  
(3) the creditor has no counter-obligation of performance;  
(4) the debtor is within the territory of China and has not disappeared or his whereabouts are unknown;  
(5) the order for payment can be served on the debtor;  
(6) the People's Court that receives the application has jurisdiction;  
(7) the creditor has not applied to the People's Court for pre-action preservation.  
If the conditions in the preceding paragraph are not met, the People's Court shall, within five days from the date of receiving the application for an order for payment, notify the creditor that the application is not accepted.  
The acceptance of an application for an order for payment by a Primary People's Court shall not be limited by the amount of the creditor's claim.

<a id="art-428"></a>第四百二十八条　人民法院受理申请后，由审判员一人进行审查。经审查，有下列情形之一的，裁定驳回申请：  
（一）申请人不具备当事人资格的；  
（二）给付金钱或者有价证券的证明文件没有约定逾期给付利息或者违约金、赔偿金，债权人坚持要求给付利息或者违约金、赔偿金的；  
（三）要求给付的金钱或者有价证券属于违法所得的；  
（四）要求给付的金钱或者有价证券尚未到期或者数额不确定的。  
人民法院受理支付令申请后，发现不符合本解释规定的受理条件的，应当在受理之日起十五日内裁定驳回申请。

**Article 428** After accepting the application, the People's Court shall have one judge review the case. Upon review, if any of the following circumstances exists, a ruling shall be made to dismiss the application:  
(1) the applicant does not have the status of a party;  
(2) the documentary evidence for payment of money or negotiable instruments does not provide for interest on overdue payment or liquidated damages or compensation, but the creditor insists on claiming such interest, liquidated damages, or compensation;  
(3) the money or negotiable instruments claimed are illegal gains;  
(4) the money or negotiable instruments claimed have not yet matured or the amount is not definite.  
If, after accepting the application for an order for payment, the People's Court finds that the conditions for acceptance as prescribed by this Interpretation are not met, it shall rule to dismiss the application within fifteen days from the date of acceptance.

<a id="art-429"></a>第四百二十九条　向债务人本人送达支付令，债务人拒绝接收的，人民法院可以留置送达。

**Article 429** If the order for payment is served on the debtor personally but the debtor refuses to receive it, the People's Court may serve it by leaving it at the debtor's domicile.

<a id="art-430"></a>第四百三十条　有下列情形之一的，人民法院应当裁定终结督促程序，已发出支付令的，支付令自行失效：  
（一）人民法院受理支付令申请后，债权人就同一债权债务关系又提起诉讼的；  
（二）人民法院发出支付令之日起三十日内无法送达债务人的；  
（三）债务人收到支付令前，债权人撤回申请的。

**Article 430** Where any of the following circumstances exists, the People's Court shall rule to terminate the procedure for hastening debt recovery, and if an order for payment has been issued, the order for payment shall automatically become invalid:  
(1) after the People's Court accepts the application for an order for payment, the creditor files a lawsuit on the same creditor-debtor relationship;  
(2) the order for payment cannot be served on the debtor within thirty days from the date of issuance;  
(3) the creditor withdraws the application before the debtor receives the order for payment.

<a id="art-431"></a>第四百三十一条　债务人在收到支付令后，未在法定期间提出书面异议，而向其他人民法院起诉的，不影响支付令的效力。  
债务人超过法定期间提出异议的，视为未提出异议。

**Article 431** If, after receiving the order for payment, the debtor fails to raise a written objection within the statutory period but files a lawsuit with another People's Court, the validity of the order for payment shall not be affected.  
If the debtor raises an objection beyond the statutory period, it shall be deemed that no objection has been raised.

<a id="art-432"></a>第四百三十二条　债权人基于同一债权债务关系，在同一支付令申请中向债务人提出多项支付请求，债务人仅就其中一项或者几项请求提出异议的，不影响其他各项请求的效力。

**Article 432** If the creditor, based on the same creditor-debtor relationship, makes multiple payment claims against the debtor in the same application for an order for payment, and the debtor raises an objection to only one or several of the claims, the validity of the other claims shall not be affected.

<a id="art-433"></a>第四百三十三条　债权人基于同一债权债务关系，就可分之债向多个债务人提出支付请求，多个债务人中的一人或者几人提出异议的，不影响其他请求的效力。

**Article 433** If the creditor, based on the same creditor-debtor relationship, makes payment claims to multiple debtors for a divisible debt, and one or more of the debtors raise an objection, the validity of the other claims shall not be affected.

<a id="art-434"></a>第四百三十四条　对设有担保的债务的主债务人发出的支付令，对担保人没有拘束力。  
债权人就担保关系单独提起诉讼的，支付令自人民法院受理案件之日起失效。

**Article 434** An order for payment issued against the principal debtor for a debt secured by a security interest shall not be binding on the guarantor.  
If the creditor separately files a lawsuit on the security relationship, the order for payment shall become invalid from the date on which the People's Court accepts the case.

<a id="art-435"></a>第四百三十五条　经形式审查，债务人提出的书面异议有下列情形之一的，应当认定异议成立，裁定终结督促程序，支付令自行失效：  
（一）本解释规定的不予受理申请情形的；  
（二）本解释规定的裁定驳回申请情形的；  
（三）本解释规定的应当裁定终结督促程序情形的；  
（四）人民法院对是否符合发出支付令条件产生合理怀疑的。

**Article 435** Upon formal examination, if a written objection raised by the debtor falls under any of the following circumstances, the objection shall be deemed established, a ruling shall be made to terminate the procedure for hastening debt recovery, and the order for payment shall automatically become invalid:  
(1) the circumstances for non-acceptance of the application as provided in this Interpretation;  
(2) the circumstances for dismissal of the application by ruling as provided in this Interpretation;  
(3) the circumstances where a ruling to terminate the procedure for hastening debt recovery shall be made as provided in this Interpretation;  
(4) the People's Court has reasonable doubt as to whether the conditions for issuing an order for payment are satisfied.

<a id="art-436"></a>第四百三十六条　债务人对债务本身没有异议，只是提出缺乏清偿能力、延缓债务清偿期限、变更债务清偿方式等异议的，不影响支付令的效力。  
人民法院经审查认为异议不成立的，裁定驳回。  
债务人的口头异议无效。

**Article 436** Where the debtor has no objection to the debt itself but merely raises objections such as lack of solvency, extension of the debt repayment term, or change of the debt repayment method, the validity of the payment order shall not be affected.  
If the People's Court, upon examination, finds that the objection is not established, it shall issue an order to dismiss the objection.  
An oral objection by the debtor shall be void.

<a id="art-437"></a>第四百三十七条　人民法院作出终结督促程序或者驳回异议裁定前，债务人请求撤回异议的，应当裁定准许。  
债务人对撤回异议反悔的，人民法院不予支持。

**Article 437** Where the debtor requests to withdraw the objection before the People's Court renders an order to terminate the督促 procedure or to dismiss the objection, the People's Court shall grant the request by order.  
If the debtor retracts the withdrawal of objection, the People's Court shall not uphold it.

<a id="art-438"></a>第四百三十八条　支付令失效后，申请支付令的一方当事人不同意提起诉讼的，应当自收到终结督促程序裁定之日起七日内向受理申请的人民法院提出。  
申请支付令的一方当事人不同意提起诉讼的，不影响其向其他有管辖权的人民法院提起诉讼。

**Article 438** After the payment order loses effect, the party that applied for the payment order who does not agree to initiate an action shall make a statement to the People's Court that accepted the application within seven days from the date of receipt of the order to terminate the督促 procedure.  
The fact that the party applying for the payment order does not agree to initiate an action shall not affect its right to initiate an action in another People's Court with jurisdiction.

<a id="art-439"></a>第四百三十九条　支付令失效后，申请支付令的一方当事人自收到终结督促程序裁定之日起七日内未向受理申请的人民法院表明不同意提起诉讼的，视为向受理申请的人民法院起诉。  
债权人提出支付令申请的时间，即为向人民法院起诉的时间。

**Article 439** After the payment order loses effect, if the party applying for the payment order fails to indicate to the People's Court that accepted the application that it does not agree to initiate an action within seven days from the date of receipt of the order to terminate the督促 procedure, it shall be deemed to have initiated an action in the People's Court that accepted the application.  
The time when the creditor filed the application for the payment order shall be the time when the action was initiated in the People's Court.

<a id="art-440"></a>第四百四十条　债权人向人民法院申请执行支付令的期间，适用[民事诉讼法第二百四十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-246)的规定。

**Article 440** The period for a creditor to apply to the People's Court for enforcement of a payment order shall be governed by Article 246 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-441"></a>第四百四十一条　人民法院院长发现本院已经发生法律效力的支付令确有错误，认为需要撤销的，应当提交本院审判委员会讨论决定后，裁定撤销支付令，驳回债权人的申请。

**Article 441** If the president of a People's Court discovers that a payment order that has already taken legal effect rendered by that court is indeed erroneous and considers that it needs to be revoked, the president shall submit the matter to the judicial committee of that court for discussion and decision, and thereafter issue an order to revoke the payment order and dismiss the creditor's application.  
20. Procedure for Public Notice for Assertion of Claims

### 二十、公示催告程序

<a id="art-442"></a>第四百四十二条　[民事诉讼法第二百二十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-225)规定的票据持有人，是指票据被盗、遗失或者灭失前的最后持有人。

**Article 442** The holder of an instrument referred to in Article 225 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China means the last holder before the instrument was stolen, lost, or destroyed.

<a id="art-443"></a>第四百四十三条　人民法院收到公示催告的申请后，应当立即审查，并决定是否受理。经审查认为符合受理条件的，通知予以受理，并同时通知支付人停止支付；认为不符合受理条件的，七日内裁定驳回申请。

**Article 443** After receiving an application for public notice for assertion of claims, the People's Court shall promptly examine it and decide whether to accept it. If upon examination it finds that the conditions for acceptance are met, it shall notify the applicant of the acceptance and simultaneously notify the payer to suspend payment; if it finds that the conditions for acceptance are not met, it shall issue an order to dismiss the application within seven days.

<a id="art-444"></a>第四百四十四条　因票据丧失，申请公示催告的，人民法院应结合票据存根、丧失票据的复印件、出票人关于签发票据的证明、申请人合法取得票据的证明、银行挂失止付通知书、报案证明等证据，决定是否受理。

**Article 444** Where an application for public notice for assertion of claims is filed due to loss of an instrument, the People's Court shall decide whether to accept the application by taking into consideration evidence such as the instrument stub, a copy of the lost instrument, the drawer's certification of issuing the instrument, the applicant's certification of lawful acquisition of the instrument, the bank's notice of report of loss and suspension of payment, and the report certificate.

<a id="art-445"></a>第四百四十五条　人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第二百二十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-226)规定发出的受理申请的公告，应当写明下列内容：  
（一）公示催告申请人的姓名或者名称；  
（二）票据的种类、号码、票面金额、出票人、背书人、持票人、付款期限等事项以及其他可以申请公示催告的权利凭证的种类、号码、权利范围、权利人、义务人、行权日期等事项；  
（三）申报权利的期间；  
（四）在公示催告期间转让票据等权利凭证，利害关系人不申报的法律后果。

**Article 445** The public notice of acceptance of an application issued by the People's Court in accordance with Article 226 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China shall specify the following:  
(1) the name of the applicant for public notice for assertion of claims;  
(2) the type, number, face amount, drawer, endorser, holder, payment term and other particulars of the instrument, as well as the type, number, scope of rights, right holder, obligor, exercise date and other particulars of other right instruments for which a public notice for assertion of claims may be applied;  
(3) the period for asserting rights;  
(4) the legal consequences of transferring the instrument or other right instruments during the period of public notice for assertion of claims if the interested person fails to assert rights.

<a id="art-446"></a>第四百四十六条　公告应当在有关报纸或者其他媒体上刊登，并于同日公布于人民法院公告栏内。人民法院所在地有证券交易所的，还应当同日在该交易所公布。

**Article 446** The public notice shall be published in relevant newspapers or other media, and on the same day, be posted on the bulletin board of the People's Court. If there is a stock exchange in the locality where the People's Court is located, it shall also be published on the same day at that exchange.

<a id="art-447"></a>第四百四十七条　公告期间不得少于六十日，且公示催告期间届满日不得早于票据付款日后十五日。

**Article 447** The period of the public notice shall not be less than sixty days, and the expiry date of the period for public notice for assertion of claims shall not be earlier than fifteen days after the payment date of the instrument.

<a id="art-448"></a>第四百四十八条　在申报期届满后、判决作出之前，利害关系人申报权利的，应当适用[民事诉讼法第二百二十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-228)第二款、第三款规定处理。

**Article 448** If an interested person asserts rights after the expiry of the declaration period but before a judgment is rendered, the provisions of Article 228, paragraphs 2 and 3 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China shall apply.

<a id="art-449"></a>第四百四十九条　利害关系人申报权利，人民法院应当通知其向法院出示票据，并通知公示催告申请人在指定的期间查看该票据。公示催告申请人申请公示催告的票据与利害关系人出示的票据不一致的，应当裁定驳回利害关系人的申报。

**Article 449** Where an interested person asserts rights, the People's Court shall notify the interested person to present the instrument to the court and notify the applicant for public notice for assertion of claims to inspect the instrument within a specified period. If the instrument in respect of which the applicant applied for public notice is inconsistent with the instrument presented by the interested person, the People's Court shall issue an order to dismiss the assertion of the interested person.

<a id="art-450"></a>第四百五十条　在申报权利的期间无人申报权利，或者申报被驳回的，申请人应当自公示催告期间届满之日起一个月内申请作出判决。逾期不申请判决的，终结公示催告程序。  
裁定终结公示催告程序的，应当通知申请人和支付人。

**Article 450** If no person asserts rights within the period for asserting rights or if the assertion is dismissed, the applicant shall apply for a judgment within one month from the date of expiry of the period for public notice for assertion of claims. If the applicant fails to apply for a judgment within the time limit, the procedure for public notice for assertion of claims shall be terminated.  
If an order is issued to terminate the procedure for public notice for assertion of claims, the applicant and the payer shall be notified.

<a id="art-451"></a>第四百五十一条　判决公告之日起，公示催告申请人有权依据判决向付款人请求付款。  
付款人拒绝付款，申请人向人民法院起诉，符合[民事诉讼法第一百二十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-122)规定的起诉条件的，人民法院应予受理。

**Article 451** From the date of publication of the judgment, the applicant for public notice for assertion of claims shall have the right to demand payment from the payer in accordance with the judgment.  
If the payer refuses to make payment and the applicant initiates an action in the People's Court that meets the conditions for filing an action prescribed in Article 122 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, the People's Court shall accept the case.

<a id="art-452"></a>第四百五十二条　适用公示催告程序审理案件，可由审判员一人独任审理；判决宣告票据无效的，应当组成合议庭审理。

**Article 452** Cases tried under the procedure for public notice for assertion of claims may be tried by a single judge; if the judgment declares the instrument null and void, a collegial panel shall be formed to try the case.

<a id="art-453"></a>第四百五十三条　公示催告申请人撤回申请，应在公示催告前提出；公示催告期间申请撤回的，人民法院可以径行裁定终结公示催告程序。

**Article 453** An applicant for public notice for assertion of claims who withdraws the application shall do so before the public notice is issued; if the application for withdrawal is made during the period of public notice, the People's Court may directly issue an order to terminate the procedure for public notice for assertion of claims.

<a id="art-454"></a>第四百五十四条　人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第二百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-227)规定通知支付人停止支付，应当符合有关财产保全的规定。支付人收到停止支付通知后拒不止付的，除可依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-114)、第一百一十七条规定采取强制措施外，在判决后，支付人仍应承担付款义务。

**Article 454** Where the People's Court notifies the payer to suspend payment in accordance with Article 227 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, it shall comply with the relevant provisions on property preservation. If the payer refuses to suspend payment after receiving the notice of suspension of payment, in addition to the compulsory measures that may be taken in accordance with Articles 114 and 117 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, the payer shall still bear the obligation to make payment after the judgment.

<a id="art-455"></a>第四百五十五条　人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第二百二十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-228)规定终结公示催告程序后，公示催告申请人或者申报人向人民法院提起诉讼，因票据权利纠纷提起的，由票据支付地或者被告住所地人民法院管辖；因非票据权利纠纷提起的，由被告住所地人民法院管辖。

**Article 455** After the People's Court terminates the procedure for public notice for assertion of claims in accordance with Article 228 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, if the applicant for public notice or the declarant initiates an action in the People's Court, an action arising from a dispute over rights on an instrument shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the place of payment of the instrument or at the domicile of the defendant; an action arising from a dispute not concerning rights on an instrument shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the domicile of the defendant.

<a id="art-456"></a>第四百五十六条　依照[民事诉讼法第二百二十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-228)规定制作的终结公示催告程序的裁定书，由审判员、书记员署名，加盖人民法院印章。

**Article 456** An order terminating the procedure for public notice for assertion of claims made in accordance with Article 228 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China shall be signed by the judge and the court clerk, and affixed with the seal of the People's Court.

<a id="art-457"></a>第四百五十七条　依照[民事诉讼法第二百三十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-230)的规定，利害关系人向人民法院起诉的，人民法院可按票据纠纷适用普通程序审理。

**Article 457** Where an interested person initiates an action in the People's Court in accordance with Article 230 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, the People's Court may try the case as an instrument dispute under ordinary procedure.

<a id="art-458"></a>第四百五十八条　[民事诉讼法第二百三十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-230)规定的正当理由，包括：  
（一）因发生意外事件或者不可抗力致使利害关系人无法知道公告事实的；  
（二）利害关系人因被限制人身自由而无法知道公告事实，或者虽然知道公告事实，但无法自己或者委托他人代为申报权利的；  
（三）不属于法定申请公示催告情形的；  
（四）未予公告或者未按法定方式公告的；  
（五）其他导致利害关系人在判决作出前未能向人民法院申报权利的客观事由。

**Article 458** The justifiable reasons referred to in Article 230 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China include:  
(1) the interested person was unable to know the facts of the public notice due to an accident or force majeure;  
(2) the interested person was unable to know the facts of the public notice because his/her personal freedom was restricted, or although he/she knew the facts of the public notice, was unable to assert rights personally or by an authorized agent;  
(3) the circumstances do not fall within the statutory conditions for applying for public notice for assertion of claims;  
(4) the public notice was not issued or was not issued in the statutory manner;  
(5) other objective reasons that prevented the interested person from asserting rights to the People's Court before the judgment was rendered.

<a id="art-459"></a>第四百五十九条　根据[民事诉讼法第二百三十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-230)的规定，利害关系人请求人民法院撤销除权判决的，应当将申请人列为被告。  
利害关系人仅诉请确认其为合法持票人的，人民法院应当在裁判文书中写明，确认利害关系人为票据权利人的判决作出后，除权判决即被撤销。

**Article 459** Where an interested person requests the People's Court to revoke a judgment on exclusion of rights in accordance with Article 230 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, the applicant shall be listed as the defendant.  
If the interested person only claims that he/she is the lawful holder of the instrument, the People's Court shall state in its judicial document that after a judgment confirming the interested person as the holder of the rights on the instrument is rendered, the judgment on exclusion of rights shall be revoked.  
21. Enforcement Procedure

### 二十一、执行程序

<a id="art-460"></a>第四百六十条　发生法律效力的实现担保物权裁定、确认调解协议裁定、支付令，由作出裁定、支付令的人民法院或者与其同级的被执行财产所在地的人民法院执行。  
认定财产无主的判决，由作出判决的人民法院将无主财产收归国家或者集体所有。

**Article 460** An order on realizing a security interest, an order on confirming a mediation agreement, or a payment order that has taken legal effect shall be enforced by the People's Court that rendered the order or payment order, or by the People's Court at the same level at the place where the property to be enforced is located.  
A judgment declaring property to be ownerless shall be enforced by the People's Court that rendered the judgment by taking the ownerless property into State or collective ownership.

<a id="art-461"></a>第四百六十一条　当事人申请人民法院执行的生效法律文书应当具备下列条件：  
（一）权利义务主体明确；  
（二）给付内容明确。  
法律文书确定继续履行合同的，应当明确继续履行的具体内容。

**Article 461** A legally effective legal document submitted by a party for enforcement by the People's Court shall meet the following conditions:  
(1) the subject of rights and obligations is clear;  
(2) the content of performance is clear.  
If the legal document determines that a contract shall continue to be performed, the specific content of continued performance shall be specified.

<a id="art-462"></a>第四百六十二条　根据[民事诉讼法第二百三十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-234)规定，案外人对执行标的提出异议的，应当在该执行标的执行程序终结前提出。

**Article 462** According to Article 234 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, if a person not involved in the case raises an objection to the subject matter of enforcement, such objection shall be raised before the conclusion of the enforcement procedure for such subject matter.

<a id="art-463"></a>第四百六十三条　案外人对执行标的提出的异议，经审查，按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）案外人对执行标的不享有足以排除强制执行的权益的，裁定驳回其异议；  
（二）案外人对执行标的享有足以排除强制执行的权益的，裁定中止执行。  
驳回案外人执行异议裁定送达案外人之日起十五日内，人民法院不得对执行标的进行处分。

**Article 463** The objection raised by a person not involved in the case to the subject matter of enforcement shall, upon examination, be handled separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) if the person not involved in the case does not have an interest in the subject matter of enforcement sufficient to exclude compulsory enforcement, the court shall issue an order to dismiss the objection;  
(2) if the person not involved in the case has an interest in the subject matter of enforcement sufficient to exclude compulsory enforcement, the court shall issue an order to suspend enforcement.  
Within fifteen days from the date of service of the order dismissing the objection raised by the person not involved in the case, the People's Court shall not dispose of the subject matter of enforcement.

<a id="art-464"></a>第四百六十四条　申请执行人与被执行人达成和解协议后请求中止执行或者撤回执行申请的，人民法院可以裁定中止执行或者终结执行。

**Article 464** Where the applicant for enforcement and the judgment debtor reach a settlement agreement and thereafter request suspension of enforcement or withdrawal of the enforcement application, the People's Court may issue an order to suspend enforcement or terminate enforcement.

<a id="art-465"></a>第四百六十五条　一方当事人不履行或者不完全履行在执行中双方自愿达成的和解协议，对方当事人申请执行原生效法律文书的，人民法院应当恢复执行，但和解协议已履行的部分应当扣除。和解协议已经履行完毕的，人民法院不予恢复执行。

**Article 465** If one party fails to perform or fails to fully perform a settlement agreement voluntarily reached by both parties during enforcement, and the other party applies for enforcement of the original legally effective legal document, the People's Court shall resume enforcement, but the portion that has been performed under the settlement agreement shall be deducted. If the settlement agreement has been fully performed, the People's Court shall not resume enforcement.

<a id="art-466"></a>第四百六十六条　申请恢复执行原生效法律文书，适用[民事诉讼法第二百四十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-246)申请执行期间的规定。申请执行期间因达成执行中的和解协议而中断，其期间自和解协议约定履行期限的最后一日起重新计算。

**Article 466** An application for resumption of enforcement of the original legally effective legal document shall be governed by the provisions on the period for applying for enforcement under Article 246 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China. The period for applying for enforcement is interrupted by the conclusion of a settlement agreement during enforcement, and the period shall be recalculated from the last day of the performance period stipulated in the settlement agreement.

<a id="art-467"></a>第四百六十七条　人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第二百三十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-238)规定决定暂缓执行的，如果担保是有期限的，暂缓执行的期限应当与担保期限一致，但最长不得超过一年。被执行人或者担保人对担保的财产在暂缓执行期间有转移、隐藏、变卖、毁损等行为的，人民法院可以恢复强制执行。

**Article 467** Where the People's Court decides to suspend enforcement in accordance with Article 238 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, if the guarantee has a term, the period of suspension of enforcement shall be consistent with the term of the guarantee, but shall not exceed one year at most. If the judgment debtor or the guarantor engages in any act such as transferring, concealing, selling, or damaging the guaranteed property during the period of suspension of enforcement, the People's Court may resume compulsory enforcement.

<a id="art-468"></a>第四百六十八条　根据[民事诉讼法第二百三十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-238)规定向人民法院提供执行担保的，可以由被执行人或者他人提供财产担保，也可以由他人提供保证。担保人应当具有代为履行或者代为承担赔偿责任的能力。  
他人提供执行保证的，应当向执行法院出具保证书，并将保证书副本送交申请执行人。被执行人或者他人提供财产担保的，应当参照民法典的有关规定办理相应手续。

**Article 468** Where an enforcement guarantee is provided to the People's Court in accordance with Article 238 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, the judgment debtor or another person may provide property as guarantee, or another person may provide a suretyship. The guarantor shall have the ability to perform on behalf of or bear liability for compensation on behalf of the judgment debtor.  
If another person provides an enforcement suretyship, a letter of guarantee shall be issued to the enforcement court, and a copy of the letter of guarantee shall be delivered to the applicant for enforcement. If the judgment debtor or another person provides property as guarantee, the relevant procedures shall be handled with reference to the relevant provisions of the Civil Code of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-469"></a>第四百六十九条　被执行人在人民法院决定暂缓执行的期限届满后仍不履行义务的，人民法院可以直接执行担保财产，或者裁定执行担保人的财产，但执行担保人的财产以担保人应当履行义务部分的财产为限。

**Article 469** If the judgment debtor still fails to perform its obligations after the expiry of the period of suspension of enforcement decided by the People's Court, the People's Court may directly enforce the guaranteed property, or issue an order to enforce the property of the guarantor, provided that the property of the guarantor to be enforced shall be limited to the portion of property corresponding to the obligations that the guarantor shall perform.

<a id="art-470"></a>第四百七十条　依照[民事诉讼法第二百三十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-239)规定，执行中作为被执行人的法人或者其他组织分立、合并的，人民法院可以裁定变更后的法人或者其他组织为被执行人；被注销的，如果依照有关实体法的规定有权利义务承受人的，可以裁定该权利义务承受人为被执行人。

**Article 470** In accordance with Article 239 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, if the legal person or other organization serving as the judgment debtor is divided or merged during enforcement, the People's Court may issue an order to substitute the legal person or other organization after the division or merger as the judgment debtor; if it is dissolved, and if there is a person that assumes the rights and obligations under the relevant substantive law, the People's Court may issue an order to make that person the judgment debtor.

<a id="art-471"></a>第四百七十一条　其他组织在执行中不能履行法律文书确定的义务的，人民法院可以裁定执行对该其他组织依法承担义务的法人或者公民个人的财产。

**Article 471** If another organization fails to perform the obligations determined by a legal document during enforcement, the People's Court may issue an order to enforce the property of the legal person or natural person that bears obligations for such other organization according to law.

<a id="art-472"></a>第四百七十二条　在执行中，作为被执行人的法人或者其他组织名称变更的，人民法院可以裁定变更后的法人或者其他组织为被执行人。

**Article 472** During enforcement, if the name of the legal person or other organization serving as the judgment debtor is changed, the People's Court may issue an order to make the legal person or other organization after the name change the judgment debtor.

<a id="art-473"></a>第四百七十三条　作为被执行人的公民死亡，其遗产继承人没有放弃继承的，人民法院可以裁定变更被执行人，由该继承人在遗产的范围内偿还债务。继承人放弃继承的，人民法院可以直接执行被执行人的遗产。

**Article 473** If the natural person serving as the judgment debtor dies and his/her heir to the estate has not renounced the inheritance, the People's Court may issue an order to substitute the judgment debtor, and the heir shall repay the debts within the scope of the estate. If the heir renounces the inheritance, the People's Court may directly enforce the estate of the judgment debtor.

<a id="art-474"></a>第四百七十四条　法律规定由人民法院执行的其他法律文书执行完毕后，该法律文书被有关机关或者组织依法撤销的，经当事人申请，适用[民事诉讼法第二百四十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-240)规定。

**Article 474** After enforcement of other legal documents that are required by law to be enforced by the People's Court is completed, if such legal document is revoked by the relevant authority or organization according to law, the provisions of Article 240 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China shall apply upon application by the party.

<a id="art-475"></a>第四百七十五条　仲裁机构裁决的事项，部分有[民事诉讼法第二百四十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-244)第二款、第三款规定情形的，人民法院应当裁定对该部分不予执行。  
应当不予执行部分与其他部分不可分的，人民法院应当裁定不予执行仲裁裁决。

**Article 475** If part of the matters adjudicated by an arbitration institution falls under the circumstances specified in Article 244, paragraphs 2 and 3 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, the People's Court shall issue an order not to enforce that part.  
If the part that shall not be enforced is inseparable from the other parts, the People's Court shall issue an order not to enforce the arbitration award.

<a id="art-476"></a>第四百七十六条　依照[民事诉讼法第二百四十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-244)第二款、第三款规定，人民法院裁定不予执行仲裁裁决后，当事人对该裁定提出执行异议或者复议的，人民法院不予受理。当事人可以就该民事纠纷重新达成书面仲裁协议申请仲裁，也可以向人民法院起诉。

**Article 476** After the People's Court issues an order not to enforce an arbitration award in accordance with Article 244, paragraphs 2 and 3 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, if a party raises an enforcement objection or applies for reconsideration of such order, the People's Court shall not accept it. The party may reach a new written arbitration agreement on the civil dispute and apply for arbitration, or may initiate an action in the People's Court.

<a id="art-477"></a>第四百七十七条　在执行中，被执行人通过仲裁程序将人民法院查封、扣押、冻结的财产确权或者分割给案外人的，不影响人民法院执行程序的进行。  
案外人不服的，可以根据[民事诉讼法第二百三十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-234)规定提出异议。

**Article 477** During enforcement, if the judgment debtor, through arbitration proceedings, has the property sealed up, seized, or frozen by the People's Court confirmed as rights or divided to a person not involved in the case, this shall not affect the progress of the People's Court's enforcement procedure.  
If the person not involved in the case disagrees, the person may raise an objection in accordance with Article 234 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-478"></a>第四百七十八条　有下列情形之一的，可以认定为[民事诉讼法第二百四十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-245)第二款规定的公证债权文书确有错误：  
（一）公证债权文书属于不得赋予强制执行效力的债权文书的；  
（二）被执行人一方未亲自或者未委托代理人到场公证等严重违反法律规定的公证程序的；  
（三）公证债权文书的内容与事实不符或者违反法律强制性规定的；  
（四）公证债权文书未载明被执行人不履行义务或者不完全履行义务时同意接受强制执行的。  
人民法院认定执行该公证债权文书违背社会公共利益的，裁定不予执行。  
公证债权文书被裁定不予执行后，当事人、公证事项的利害关系人可以就债权争议提起诉讼。

**Article 478** A notarized document of a creditor's right as referred to in Article 245, paragraph 2 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China may be determined to be indeed erroneous under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) the notarized document of a creditor's right is a document of a creditor's right that shall not be given the effect of compulsory enforcement;  
(2) the judgment debtor party did not appear in person or authorize an agent to appear for notarization, or there are other serious violations of the statutory notarization procedures;  
(3) the content of the notarized document of a creditor's right is inconsistent with the facts or violates mandatory provisions of law;  
(4) the notarized document of a creditor's right does not state that the judgment debtor agrees to accept compulsory enforcement if it fails to perform or fails to fully perform its obligations.  
If the People's Court determines that enforcement of such notarized document of a creditor's right is contrary to social public interests, it shall issue an order not to enforce it.  
After the notarized document of a creditor's right is ordered not to be enforced, the parties and interested persons of the notarization matter may initiate an action regarding the dispute over the creditor's right.

<a id="art-479"></a>第四百七十九条　当事人请求不予执行仲裁裁决或者公证债权文书的，应当在执行终结前向执行法院提出。

**Article 479** A party requesting not to enforce an arbitration award or a notarized document of a creditor's right shall make such request to the enforcement court before the conclusion of enforcement.

<a id="art-480"></a>第四百八十条　人民法院应当在收到申请执行书或者移交执行书后十日内发出执行通知。  
执行通知中除应责令被执行人履行法律文书确定的义务外，还应通知其承担[民事诉讼法第二百六十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-260)规定的迟延履行利息或者迟延履行金。

**Article 480** The People's Court shall issue an enforcement notice within ten days after receiving the application for enforcement or the transfer of enforcement.  
In addition to ordering the judgment debtor to perform the obligations determined by the legal document, the enforcement notice shall also notify the judgment debtor to pay the interest for delayed performance or the damages for delayed performance as specified in Article 260 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-481"></a>第四百八十一条　申请执行人超过申请执行时效期间向人民法院申请强制执行的，人民法院应予受理。被执行人对申请执行时效期间提出异议，人民法院经审查异议成立的，裁定不予执行。  
被执行人履行全部或者部分义务后，又以不知道申请执行时效期间届满为由请求执行回转的，人民法院不予支持。

**Article 481** If an applicant for enforcement applies to the People's Court for compulsory enforcement beyond the limitation period for applying for enforcement, the People's Court shall accept the application. If the judgment debtor raises an objection to the limitation period for applying for enforcement, and the People's Court finds upon examination that the objection is established, it shall issue an order not to enforce.  
After the judgment debtor performs all or part of its obligations, if it thereafter requests a recovery of enforcement on the ground that it did not know the limitation period for applying for enforcement had expired, the People's Court shall not uphold the request.

<a id="art-482"></a>第四百八十二条　对必须接受调查询问的被执行人、被执行人的法定代表人、负责人或者实际控制人，经依法传唤无正当理由拒不到场的，人民法院可以拘传其到场。  
人民法院应当及时对被拘传人进行调查询问，调查询问的时间不得超过八小时；情况复杂，依法可能采取拘留措施的，调查询问的时间不得超过二十四小时。  
人民法院在本辖区以外采取拘传措施时，可以将被拘传人拘传到当地人民法院，当地人民法院应予协助。

**Article 482** Where a judgment debtor, the legal representative, responsible person, or actual controller of the judgment debtor who must accept investigation and interrogation refuses to appear without justifiable reasons after being summoned according to law, the People's Court may issue a warrant to compel appearance.  
The People's Court shall promptly investigate and interrogate the person compelled to appear. The time for investigation and interrogation shall not exceed eight hours; if the circumstances are complex and detention measures may be taken according to law, the time for investigation and interrogation shall not exceed twenty-four hours.  
When the People's Court takes measures to compel appearance outside its jurisdiction, it may compel the person to appear at the local People's Court, and the local People's Court shall provide assistance.

<a id="art-483"></a>第四百八十三条　人民法院有权查询被执行人的身份信息与财产信息，掌握相关信息的单位和个人必须按照协助执行通知书办理。

**Article 483** The People's Court shall have the right to inquire into the identity information and property information of the judgment debtor. The entities and individuals in possession of the relevant information must act in accordance with the notice of assistance in enforcement.

<a id="art-484"></a>第四百八十四条　对被执行的财产，人民法院非经查封、扣押、冻结不得处分。对银行存款等各类可以直接扣划的财产，人民法院的扣划裁定同时具有冻结的法律效力。

**Article 484** The People's Court shall not dispose of the property to be enforced without sealing up, seizing, or freezing it. For bank deposits and other types of property that may be directly deducted, the order of the People's Court for deduction shall simultaneously have the legal effect of freezing.

<a id="art-485"></a>第四百八十五条　人民法院冻结被执行人的银行存款的期限不得超过一年，查封、扣押动产的期限不得超过两年，查封不动产、冻结其他财产权的期限不得超过三年。  
申请执行人申请延长期限的，人民法院应当在查封、扣押、冻结期限届满前办理续行查封、扣押、冻结手续，续行期限不得超过前款规定的期限。  
人民法院也可以依职权办理续行查封、扣押、冻结手续。

**Article 485** The period for the People's Court to freeze the bank deposits of the judgment debtor shall not exceed one year; the period for sealing up or seizing chattels shall not exceed two years; the period for sealing up immovable property or freezing other property rights shall not exceed three years.  
If the applicant for enforcement applies for an extension of the period, the People's Court shall handle the renewal of sealing up, seizure, or freezing before the expiry of the original period. The renewed period shall not exceed the period specified in the preceding paragraph.  
The People's Court may also handle the renewal of sealing up, seizure, or freezing by its own authority.

<a id="art-486"></a>第四百八十六条　依照[民事诉讼法第二百五十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-254)规定，人民法院在执行中需要拍卖被执行人财产的，可以由人民法院自行组织拍卖，也可以交由具备相应资质的拍卖机构拍卖。  
交拍卖机构拍卖的，人民法院应当对拍卖活动进行监督。

**Article 486** Where the People's Court needs to auction the property of the judgment debtor during enforcement in accordance with Article 254 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, the People's Court may organize the auction by itself, or may assign it to an auction institution with corresponding qualifications for auction.  
If the auction is assigned to an auction institution, the People's Court shall supervise the auction activities.

<a id="art-487"></a>第四百八十七条　拍卖评估需要对现场进行检查、勘验的，人民法院应当责令被执行人、协助义务人予以配合。被执行人、协助义务人不予配合的，人民法院可以强制进行。

**Article 487** If the auction evaluation requires an inspection or examination of the site, the People's Court shall order the judgment debtor and the person with an obligation to assist to cooperate. If the judgment debtor or the person with an obligation to assist fails to cooperate, the People's Court may forcibly conduct the inspection or examination.

<a id="art-488"></a>第四百八十八条　人民法院在执行中需要变卖被执行人财产的，可以交有关单位变卖，也可以由人民法院直接变卖。  
对变卖的财产，人民法院或者其工作人员不得买受。

**Article 488** Where the People's Court needs to sell off the property of the judgment debtor during enforcement, it may assign the sale to a relevant entity or may sell off the property directly by itself.  
The People's Court or its staff members shall not purchase the property being sold off.

<a id="art-489"></a>第四百八十九条　经申请执行人和被执行人同意，且不损害其他债权人合法权益和社会公共利益的，人民法院可以不经拍卖、变卖，直接将被执行人的财产作价交申请执行人抵偿债务。对剩余债务，被执行人应当继续清偿。

**Article 489** With the consent of both the applicant for enforcement and the judgment debtor, and provided that the lawful rights and interests of other creditors and the social public interest are not harmed, the People's Court may, without auction or sale, directly evaluate the property of the judgment debtor and deliver it to the applicant for enforcement to offset the debt. For the remaining debt, the judgment debtor shall continue to satisfy it.

<a id="art-490"></a>第四百九十条　被执行人的财产无法拍卖或者变卖的，经申请执行人同意，且不损害其他债权人合法权益和社会公共利益的，人民法院可以将该项财产作价后交付申请执行人抵偿债务，或者交付申请执行人管理；申请执行人拒绝接收或者管理的，退回被执行人。

**Article 490** If the property of the judgment debtor cannot be auctioned or sold off, with the consent of the applicant for enforcement and provided that the lawful rights and interests of other creditors and the social public interest are not harmed, the People's Court may, after evaluating the property, deliver it to the applicant for enforcement to offset the debt, or deliver it to the applicant for enforcement for management; if the applicant for enforcement refuses to accept or manage the property, it shall be returned to the judgment debtor.

<a id="art-491"></a>第四百九十一条　拍卖成交或者依法定程序裁定以物抵债的，标的物所有权自拍卖成交裁定或者抵债裁定送达买受人或者接受抵债物的债权人时转移。

**Article 491** Where an auction is concluded or an order is made according to statutory procedures to offset debt with property, the ownership of the subject matter shall be transferred at the time when the order on conclusion of auction or the order on offsetting debt with property is served on the buyer or the creditor who accepts the property used to offset the debt.

<a id="art-492"></a>第四百九十二条　执行标的物为特定物的，应当执行原物。原物确已毁损或者灭失的，经双方当事人同意，可以折价赔偿。  
双方当事人对折价赔偿不能协商一致的，人民法院应当终结执行程序。申请执行人可以另行起诉。

**Article 492** If the subject matter of enforcement is a specific thing, the original thing shall be enforced. If the original thing has indeed been damaged or destroyed, with the consent of both parties, compensation may be made at a converted price.  
If the two parties cannot reach an agreement on compensation at a converted price, the People's Court shall terminate the enforcement procedure. The applicant for enforcement may bring a separate action.

<a id="art-493"></a>第四百九十三条　他人持有法律文书指定交付的财物或者票证，人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第二百五十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-256)第二款、第三款规定发出协助执行通知后，拒不转交的，可以强制执行，并可依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-117)、第一百一十八条规定处理。  
他人持有期间财物或者票证毁损、灭失的，参照[本解释第四百九十二条](#art-492)规定处理。  
他人主张合法持有财物或者票证的，可以根据[民事诉讼法第二百三十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-234)规定提出执行异议。

**Article 493** Where another person holds the property or negotiable instrument designated for delivery in the legal document, and after the People's Court issues a notice of assistance in enforcement in accordance with Article 256, paragraphs 2 and 3 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, refuses to transfer it, compulsory enforcement may be carried out, and the matter may be handled in accordance with Articles 117 and 118 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China.  
If the property or negotiable instrument is damaged or destroyed while held by another person, the provisions of Article 492 of this Interpretation shall apply.  
If another person claims lawful possession of the property or negotiable instrument, an enforcement objection may be raised in accordance with Article 234 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-494"></a>第四百九十四条　在执行中，被执行人隐匿财产、会计账簿等资料的，人民法院除可依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-114)第一款第六项规定对其处理外，还应责令被执行人交出隐匿的财产、会计账簿等资料。被执行人拒不交出的，人民法院可以采取搜查措施。

**Article 494** During enforcement, if the judgment debtor conceals property, accounting books, or other materials, the People's Court, in addition to handling the matter in accordance with Article 114, paragraph 1, item 6 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, shall order the judgment debtor to produce the concealed property, accounting books, or other materials. If the judgment debtor refuses to produce them, the People's Court may take search measures.

<a id="art-495"></a>第四百九十五条　搜查人员应当按规定着装并出示搜查令和工作证件。

**Article 495** The search personnel shall be properly dressed as required and shall present the search warrant and their work identification.

<a id="art-496"></a>第四百九十六条　人民法院搜查时禁止无关人员进入搜查现场；搜查对象是公民的，应当通知被执行人或者他的成年家属以及基层组织派员到场；搜查对象是法人或者其他组织的，应当通知法定代表人或者主要负责人到场。拒不到场的，不影响搜查。  
搜查妇女身体，应当由女执行人员进行。

**Article 496** When a People's Court conducts a search, irrelevant persons shall be prohibited from entering the search site; if the subject of the search is a citizen, the person subjected to enforcement or his adult family members and the basic-level organization shall be notified to send representatives to be present; if the subject of the search is a legal person or any other organization, its legal representative or principal responsible person shall be notified to be present. Failure to be present shall not affect the search.  
A search of a woman's body shall be conducted by female enforcement personnel.

<a id="art-497"></a>第四百九十七条　搜查中发现应当依法采取查封、扣押措施的财产，依照[民事诉讼法第二百五十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-252)第二款和第二百五十四条规定办理。

**Article 497** Where any property that shall be sealed up or distrained according to law is discovered during a search, the matter shall be handled in accordance with Article 252, paragraph 2, and Article 254 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-498"></a>第四百九十八条　搜查应当制作搜查笔录，由搜查人员、被搜查人及其他在场人签名、捺印或者盖章。拒绝签名、捺印或者盖章的，应当记入搜查笔录。

**Article 498** A search record shall be made for a search, and shall be signed, fingerprinted or sealed by the searchers, the searched person and any other persons present. If any person refuses to sign, fingerprint or seal, such refusal shall be recorded in the search record.

<a id="art-499"></a>第四百九十九条　人民法院执行被执行人对他人的到期债权，可以作出冻结债权的裁定，并通知该他人向申请执行人履行。  
该他人对到期债权有异议，申请执行人请求对异议部分强制执行的，人民法院不予支持。利害关系人对到期债权有异议的，人民法院应当按照[民事诉讼法第二百三十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-234)规定处理。  
对生效法律文书确定的到期债权，该他人予以否认的，人民法院不予支持。

**Article 499** When a People's Court enforces a matured claim that the person subjected to enforcement holds against a third party, it may render a ruling to freeze the claim and notify the third party to perform the obligation to the applicant for enforcement.  
If the third party raises an objection to the matured claim, and the applicant for enforcement requests compulsory execution of the objected part, the People's Court shall not support it. If an interested party raises an objection to the matured claim, the People's Court shall handle the matter in accordance with Article 234 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China.  
If the third party denies a matured claim confirmed by an effective legal instrument, the People's Court shall not support it.

<a id="art-500"></a>第五百条　人民法院在执行中需要办理房产证、土地证、林权证、专利证书、商标证书、车船执照等有关财产权证照转移手续的，可以依照[民事诉讼法第二百五十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-258)规定办理。

**Article 500** Where a People's Court needs to handle the transfer of property rights certificates such as real estate certificates, land certificates, forest rights certificates, patent certificates, trademark certificates, vehicle and vessel licenses during enforcement, it may handle the matter in accordance with Article 258 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-501"></a>第五百零一条　被执行人不履行生效法律文书确定的行为义务，该义务可由他人完成的，人民法院可以选定代履行人；法律、行政法规对履行该行为义务有资格限制的，应当从有资格的人中选定。必要时，可以通过招标的方式确定代履行人。  
申请执行人可以在符合条件的人中推荐代履行人，也可以申请自己代为履行，是否准许，由人民法院决定。

**Article 501** Where the person subjected to enforcement fails to perform a behavioral obligation confirmed by an effective legal instrument, and such obligation may be performed by another person, the People's Court may select a substitute performer; if there are qualification restrictions on performance of such behavioral obligation under laws or administrative regulations, the substitute performer shall be selected from qualified persons. If necessary, a substitute performer may be determined through bidding.  
The applicant for enforcement may recommend a substitute performer among eligible persons, or apply to perform the obligation by itself. Whether to permit shall be decided by the People's Court.

<a id="art-502"></a>第五百零二条　代履行费用的数额由人民法院根据案件具体情况确定，并由被执行人在指定期限内预先支付。被执行人未预付的，人民法院可以对该费用强制执行。  
代履行结束后，被执行人可以查阅、复制费用清单以及主要凭证。

**Article 502** The amount of substitute performance fees shall be determined by the People's Court based on the specific circumstances of the case, and shall be prepaid by the person subjected to enforcement within a specified period. If the person subjected to enforcement fails to prepay, the People's Court may enforce such fees.  
After the substitute performance is completed, the person subjected to enforcement may inspect and copy the fee list and main vouchers.

<a id="art-503"></a>第五百零三条　被执行人不履行法律文书指定的行为，且该项行为只能由被执行人完成的，人民法院可以依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-114)第一款第六项规定处理。  
被执行人在人民法院确定的履行期间内仍不履行的，人民法院可以依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-114)第一款第六项规定再次处理。

**Article 503** Where the person subjected to enforcement fails to perform an act specified in a legal instrument, and such act can only be completed by the person subjected to enforcement, the People's Court may handle the matter in accordance with Article 114, paragraph 1, subparagraph 6 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China.  
If the person subjected to enforcement still fails to perform within the performance period determined by the People's Court, the People's Court may handle the matter again in accordance with Article 114, paragraph 1, subparagraph 6 of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-504"></a>第五百零四条　被执行人迟延履行的，迟延履行期间的利息或者迟延履行金自判决、裁定和其他法律文书指定的履行期间届满之日起计算。

**Article 504** If the person subjected to enforcement delays performance, the interest on delayed performance or the liquidated damages for delayed performance shall be calculated from the day following the expiration of the performance period specified in the judgment, ruling or other legal instrument.

<a id="art-505"></a>第五百零五条　被执行人未按判决、裁定和其他法律文书指定的期间履行非金钱给付义务的，无论是否已给申请执行人造成损失，都应当支付迟延履行金。已经造成损失的，双倍补偿申请执行人已经受到的损失；没有造成损失的，迟延履行金可以由人民法院根据具体案件情况决定。

**Article 505** If the person subjected to enforcement fails to perform a non-monetary payment obligation within the period specified in the judgment, ruling or other legal instrument, regardless of whether any loss has been caused to the applicant for enforcement, the person subjected to enforcement shall pay liquidated damages for delayed performance. If loss has been caused, double compensation shall be paid for the loss already suffered by the applicant for enforcement; if no loss has been caused, the liquidated damages for delayed performance may be determined by the People's Court based on the specific circumstances of the case.

<a id="art-506"></a>第五百零六条　被执行人为公民或者其他组织，在执行程序开始后，被执行人的其他已经取得执行依据的债权人发现被执行人的财产不能清偿所有债权的，可以向人民法院申请参与分配。  
对人民法院查封、扣押、冻结的财产有优先权、担保物权的债权人，可以直接申请参与分配，主张优先受偿权。

**Article 506** Where the person subjected to enforcement is a citizen or any other organization, if, after the enforcement procedure commences, other creditors who have obtained enforcement instruments discover that the property of the person subjected to enforcement is insufficient to satisfy all claims, they may apply to the People's Court to participate in the distribution.  
Creditors with priority rights or security interests over the property sealed up, distrained or frozen by the People's Court may directly apply to participate in the distribution and claim the right to be paid in priority.

<a id="art-507"></a>第五百零七条　申请参与分配，申请人应当提交申请书。申请书应当写明参与分配和被执行人不能清偿所有债权的事实、理由，并附有执行依据。  
参与分配申请应当在执行程序开始后，被执行人的财产执行终结前提出。

**Article 507** To apply for participation in distribution, the applicant shall submit a written application. The application shall state the facts and reasons for participating in distribution and that the person subjected to enforcement cannot satisfy all claims, and shall attach the enforcement instrument.  
The application for participation in distribution shall be submitted after the enforcement procedure commences and before the enforcement of the property of the person subjected to enforcement is concluded.

<a id="art-508"></a>第五百零八条　参与分配执行中，执行所得价款扣除执行费用，并清偿应当优先受偿的债权后，对于普通债权，原则上按照其占全部申请参与分配债权数额的比例受偿。清偿后的剩余债务，被执行人应当继续清偿。债权人发现被执行人有其他财产的，可以随时请求人民法院执行。
